/* ============================================================
   LAYOUT ONLY. Colors, radii, borders, shadows and sizes are
   generated by lib/theme.js and lib/sizes.js and loaded into
   the theme at enable() time. Do not add color/size rules
   here — they will be overridden or duplicate the generated
   sheet. Keep only: padding, spacing, margin, letter-spacing,
   font-weight, transitions, scrollbar dimensions, vfade offset,
   gradient direction, pointer-events, alignment, and the two
   intentional overrides noted inline.
   ============================================================ */

// ===== FILE: ./lib/genericOverlay.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/genericOverlay.js =====
// Dmenu/rofi-style picker over a list of strings, with optional argument
// controls (toggles / choice selectors). Reports the result through
// SearchOverlayBase._notifyResult as:
//     [...selectedLabels (original order), 'args::', ...argLines]
//
// All size metrics live in sizes.js (S.dmenu, S.flyout). Constants kept
// here are layout-only: panel padding / spacing / fraction, and the list
// threshold.

import Clutter from 'gi://Clutter';
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import Pango from 'gi://Pango';
import St from 'gi://St';

import * as Main from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/main.js';
import * as PopupMenu from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/popupMenu.js';

import * as Fuzzy from './fuzzy.js';
import * as UI from './ui.js';
import { S } from './sizes.js';
import { PagedStrip, VISIBLE_SLOTS } from './strip.js';

import { SearchOverlayBase, clamp } from './overlayBase.js';

const ARGS_MARKER = 'args::';

const D = S.dmenu;

const LIST_SPACING = 6;
const STRIP_THRESHOLD = 5;

// Panel geometry (layout-only)
const PANEL_WIDTH_FRACTION = 0.75;
const PANEL_MAX_WIDTH = 2000;
const PANEL_V_MARGIN = 40;
const PANEL_PADDING = 32;
const SPACING = 16;
const CONTROL_SPACING = 6;

export class GenericSearchOverlay extends SearchOverlayBase {
    constructor(items, settings, options = {}) {
        super(settings);
        this._multi = !!options.multi;
        this._onResult = options.onResult || null;
        this._hint = options.hint || null;
        this._checked = new Set();
        this._items = items.map(it => ({
            id: String(it.id ?? it.label ?? it),
            label: String(it.label ?? it),
            icon: it.icon || null,
        }));
        this._controls = (options.controls ?? []).map(c => ({
            kind: c.kind, label: c.label, options: c.options,
            enabled: false, selected: 0,
        }));

        this._rows = [];
        this._rowRegistry = new Map();
        this._useStrip = this._items.length > STRIP_THRESHOLD;
        this._strip = null;
        this._centerOnSelect = true;
        this._wheelAccum = 0;
        this._summary = null;

        this._buildUI();
        this._open();
        this._onSearchChanged();
        this._updateSummary();
    }

    _trackFullscreen() { return false; }
    _getRestoreKey(item) { return item.id; }
    _shouldCloseOnWindowRemoved() { return false; }

    _computeResults(query) {
        if (!query) return this._items.map(it => ({ ...it, indices: null }));
        const scored = [];
        for (const item of this._items) {
            const r = Fuzzy.fuzzyMatch(query, item.label);
            if (r.matched) scored.push({ ...item, score: r.score, indices: r.indices });
        }
        return scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
    }

    // ==================== UI construction ====================
    _controlsHeight() {
        const n = this._controls.length;
        return n ? D.controlHeadH + n * D.controlRowH + n * CONTROL_SPACING : 0;
    }

    _buildUI() {
        const monitor = Main.layoutManager.primaryMonitor;
        this._monitor = monitor;

        const ctrlH = this._controlsHeight();
        const footH = this._multi ? D.footerH : 0;
        const children = 2 + (ctrlH ? 1 : 0) + (footH ? 1 : 0);
        const fixedH = PANEL_PADDING * 2 + D.searchHeight + ctrlH + footH + (children - 1) * SPACING;
        const avail = monitor.height - PANEL_V_MARGIN * 2;
        const room = Math.max(0, avail - fixedH);

        let listH;
        if (this._useStrip) {
            const want = Math.min(VISIBLE_SLOTS, this._items.length);
            const fit = Math.floor((room - D.edgeH * 2) / S.flyout.rowSlot);
            this._stripSlots = clamp(fit, 1, want);
            listH = D.edgeH * 2 + this._stripSlots * S.flyout.rowSlot;
        } else {
            const content = this._items.length * (D.rowHeight + LIST_SPACING) + 8;
            listH = Math.max(D.rowHeight + 16, Math.min(room, content));
        }

        const panelW = Math.min(PANEL_MAX_WIDTH, Math.round(monitor.width * PANEL_WIDTH_FRACTION));
        const panelH = fixedH + listH;
        const innerW = panelW - PANEL_PADDING * 2;
        const panelX = Math.round(monitor.x + (monitor.width - panelW) / 2);
        const panelY = Math.round(monitor.y + Math.max(PANEL_V_MARGIN, (monitor.height - panelH) / 3));

        this._container = UI.createFullscreenContainer(monitor, 'dmenu-overlay');

        this._panel = new St.BoxLayout({
            style_class: 'dmenu-panel', vertical: true, reactive: true,
            x: panelX, y: panelY, width: panelW, height: panelH,
        });
        this._container.add_child(this._panel);

        const search = UI.createSearchField({ width: innerW, hint: 'Type to filter…' });
        this._wireEntry(search.entry);
        if (this._hint) search.entry.set_hint_text(this._hint);
        this._panel.add_child(search.actor);

        this._panel.add_child(this._buildList(innerW, listH));

        const controls = this._buildControls();
        if (controls) this._panel.add_child(controls);
        const footer = this._buildFooter();
        if (footer) this._panel.add_child(footer);

        this._scope.connect(this._container, 'button-press-event', (a, e) => {
            if (e.get_button() !== Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            if (this._panel.contains(e.get_source())) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            this._close();
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        });

        if (this._useStrip)
            this._scope.connect(this._container, 'scroll-event', (a, e) => this._onWheel(e));
    }

    _buildList(w, h) {
        this._listHost = new St.Widget({ clip_to_allocation: true, width: w, height: h });

        if (this._useStrip) this._buildStrip(w, h);
        else this._buildScroll(w, h);

        this._emptyLabel = new St.Label({
            text: 'No matching items', style_class: 'dmenu-empty',
            visible: false, width: w,
        });
        this._emptyLabel.clutter_text.set_line_alignment(Pango.Alignment.CENTER);
        this._emptyLabel.set_position(0, 12);
        this._listHost.add_child(this._emptyLabel);
        return this._listHost;
    }

    _buildScroll(w, h) {
        this._listScroll = new St.ScrollView({
            style_class: 'launcher-scroll', overlay_scrollbars: false,
        });
        this._listScroll.set_policy(St.PolicyType.NEVER, St.PolicyType.AUTOMATIC);
        this._listScroll.set_size(w, h);

        this._listBox = new St.BoxLayout({
            style_class: 'dmenu-results-box', vertical: true, x_expand: true,
        });
        this._listScroll.set_child(this._listBox);
        this._listHost.add_child(this._listScroll);
    }

    _buildStrip(w, h) {
        const slots = this._stripSlots;
        const rowsH = slots * S.flyout.rowSlot;
        this._stripRowsW = w - D.railW - D.railGap;

        const upEl = new St.Widget({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-edge ws-flyout-edge-up' });
        const downEl = new St.Widget({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-edge ws-flyout-edge-down' });
        const rowsEl = new St.Widget({ clip_to_allocation: true });
        const rail = UI.createRail();

        upEl.set_position(0, 0);
        upEl.set_size(w, D.edgeH);
        rowsEl.set_position(0, D.edgeH);
        rowsEl.set_size(this._stripRowsW, rowsH);
        rail.root.set_position(w - D.railW, D.edgeH);
        rail.root.set_size(D.railW, rowsH);
        downEl.set_position(0, D.edgeH + rowsH);
        downEl.set_size(w, D.edgeH);

        for (const a of [upEl, downEl, rail.root, rowsEl]) this._listHost.add_child(a);

        this._strip = new PagedStrip({
            rowsEl, upEl, downEl, rail,
            makeRow: (item, index) => this._makeRowActor(item, index, {
                width: this._stripRowsW,
                height: S.flyout.rowSlot - D.stripGap,
            }),
            alive: () => !this._closed,
        });
        this._strip.setMaxSlots(slots);
        this._strip.setViewport(h);
    }

    _onWheel(event) {
        let step = 0;
        const dir = event.get_scroll_direction();
        if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.UP) step = -1;
        else if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.DOWN) step = 1;
        else if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.SMOOTH) {
            const [, dy] = event.get_scroll_delta();
            this._wheelAccum += dy;
            if (Math.abs(this._wheelAccum) >= 1) {
                step = Math.sign(this._wheelAccum);
                this._wheelAccum = 0;
            }
        } else return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;

        if (step && this._results.length)
            this._selectIndex(clamp(this._selectedIndex + step, 0, this._results.length - 1));
        return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
    }

    // ---------- controls ----------
    _buildControls() {
        if (!this._controls.length) return null;
        const n = this._controls.length;

        const box = new St.BoxLayout({ style_class: 'dmenu-controls', vertical: true, x_expand: true });

        const head = new St.BoxLayout({ height: D.controlHeadH, x_expand: true });
        head.add_child(new St.Label({
            text: 'OPTIONS', style_class: 'dmenu-section-label',
            x_expand: true, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        }));
        head.add_child(new St.Label({
            text: `${n} control${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`, style_class: 'dmenu-section-count',
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        }));
        box.add_child(head);

        for (const c of this._controls) box.add_child(this._buildControlRow(c));
        return box;
    }

    _buildControlRow(c) {
        const row = new St.BoxLayout({
            style_class: 'dmenu-control-row', x_expand: true,
            height: D.controlRowH, reactive: true, track_hover: true,
        });
        const name = new St.Label({
            text: c.label, style_class: 'dmenu-control-name',
            x_expand: true, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        name.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.END);
        row.add_child(name);

        if (c.kind === 'toggle') {
            const sw = new PopupMenu.Switch(c.enabled);
            sw.y_align = Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER;
            sw.set_pivot_point(1, 0.5);
            sw.set_scale(1.7, 1.7);
            sw.margin_left = 40;
            row.add_child(sw);

            this._scope.connect(sw, 'notify::state', () => {
                c.enabled = sw.state;
            });

            this._scope.connect(row, 'button-press-event', (a, e) => {
                if (e.get_button() !== Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
                sw.toggle();
                this._entry.grab_key_focus();
                return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
            });
            return row;
        }

        const seg = new St.BoxLayout({
            style_class: 'dmenu-segmented', y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        const buttons = [];
        const sync = () => buttons.forEach((b, i) => {
            if (i === c.selected) b.add_style_class_name('active');
            else b.remove_style_class_name('active');
        });
        c.options.forEach((opt, i) => {
            const b = new St.Button({
                label: opt.label, style_class: 'dmenu-segment',
                can_focus: false, reactive: true, track_hover: true,
            });
            this._scope.connect(b, 'clicked', () => {
                c.selected = i;
                sync();
                this._entry.grab_key_focus();
            });
            buttons.push(b);
            seg.add_child(b);
        });
        sync();
        row.add_child(seg);
        return row;
    }

    _collectArgs() {
        const out = [];
        for (const c of this._controls) {
            const opt = c.kind === 'toggle' ? (c.enabled ? c.options[0] : null) : c.options[c.selected];
            const arg = opt?.arg?.trim();
            if (arg) out.push(arg);
        }
        return out;
    }

    // ---------- footer (multi only) ----------
    _buildFooter() {
        if (!this._multi) return null;
        const box = new St.BoxLayout({ style_class: 'dmenu-footer', x_expand: true, height: D.footerH });
        this._summary = new St.Label({
            style_class: 'dmenu-summary', x_expand: true, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        const confirm = new St.Button({
            label: 'Confirm selection ↵', style_class: 'dmenu-confirm',
            can_focus: false, reactive: true, track_hover: true,
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        this._scope.connect(confirm, 'clicked', () => this._confirm());
        box.add_child(this._summary);
        box.add_child(confirm);
        return box;
    }

    _updateSummary() {
        if (!this._summary) return;
        this._summary.set_text(`${this._checked.size} selected · click or Ctrl+Space to toggle`);
    }

    _confirm() {
        this._finish(this._results[this._selectedIndex] ?? null);
    }

    // ==================== rows ====================
    _makeRowActor(item, index, { width = null, height }) {
        const props = {
            style_class: 'dmenu-result-row', x_expand: true,
            reactive: true, track_hover: true, height,
        };
        if (width) props.width = width;
        const row = new St.BoxLayout(props);

        if (item.icon) {
            row.add_child(new St.Icon({
                gicon: item.icon, icon_size: D.rowIcon,
                y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER, style: 'margin-right: 20px;',
            }));
        }

        const label = new St.Label({
            style_class: 'dmenu-result-label', x_expand: true,
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        label.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.END);
        label.clutter_text.set_markup(UI.highlightMarkup(item.label, item.indices));
        row.add_child(label);

        let badge = null;
        if (this._multi) {
            badge = new St.Label({
                text: '', style_class: 'dmenu-badge', y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            });
            row.add_child(badge);
        }
        this._applyChecked(row, badge, item.id);

        this._rowRegistry.set(item.id, { row, badge });
        row.connect('destroy', () => {
            if (this._rowRegistry.get(item.id)?.row === row) this._rowRegistry.delete(item.id);
        });

        row.connect('button-press-event', (a, e) => {
            if (e.get_button() !== Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            this._onRowClick(index);
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        });
        row.connect('motion-event', () => {
            if (this._closed || this._selectedIndex === index) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            this._centerOnSelect = false;
            this._selectIndex(index);
            this._centerOnSelect = true;
            return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
        });
        return row;
    }

    _applyChecked(row, badge, id) {
        const on = this._checked.has(id);
        if (on) row.add_style_class_name('selected');
        else row.remove_style_class_name('selected');
        if (badge) { badge.set_text(on ? '✓' : ''); badge.visible = on; }
    }

    _onRowClick(index) {
        this._centerOnSelect = false;
        this._selectIndex(index);
        this._centerOnSelect = true;
        if (this._multi) this._toggleAt(index);
        else this._activateResult(index);
    }

    _renderResults(results) {
        this._rowRegistry.clear();
        if (this._emptyLabel) this._emptyLabel.visible = results.length === 0;

        if (this._useStrip) {
            this._strip.setItems(results, 0);
            return;
        }

        this._listBox.destroy_all_children();
        this._rows = [];
        results.forEach((item, i) => {
            const row = this._makeRowActor(item, i, { height: D.rowHeight });
            this._listBox.add_child(row);
            this._rows.push(row);
        });
    }

    _onSelectionChanged(index) {
        if (this._useStrip) {
            this._strip.setHighlight(index);
            if (index >= 0 && this._centerOnSelect) this._strip.center(index);
            return;
        }

        for (let i = 0; i < this._rows.length; i++) {
            if (i === index) this._rows[i].add_style_class_name('sel');
            else this._rows[i].remove_style_class_name('sel');
        }
        if (index < 0) return;

        this._scope.idle(() => {
            if (this._closed || index < 0) return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
            const row = this._rows[index];
            const adj = this._listScroll.vadjustment;
            if (!row || !adj) return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
            const box = row.get_allocation_box();
            const v = adj.value, page = adj.page_size;
            let target = null;
            if (box.y1 < v) target = box.y1;
            else if (box.y2 > v + page) target = box.y2 - page;
            if (target == null) return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
            target = Math.max(adj.lower, Math.min(target, adj.upper - page));
            if (target !== v) adj.ease(target, { duration: 120, mode: Clutter.AnimationMode.EASE_OUT_QUAD });
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    // ==================== selection ====================
    _updateCheckmarks() {
        for (const [id, { row, badge }] of this._rowRegistry)
            this._applyChecked(row, badge, id);
        this._updateSummary();
    }

    _toggleAt(index) {
        const item = this._results[index];
        if (!item) return;
        if (this._checked.has(item.id)) this._checked.delete(item.id);
        else this._checked.add(item.id);
        this._updateCheckmarks();
    }

    _handleExtraKeys(event, symbol) {
        const state = event.get_state();

        if (symbol === Clutter.KEY_Page_Down) {
            if (this._results.length)
                this._selectIndex(Math.min(this._selectedIndex + 5, this._results.length - 1));
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        }
        if (symbol === Clutter.KEY_Page_Up) {
            if (this._results.length) this._selectIndex(Math.max(this._selectedIndex - 5, 0));
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        }

        if (!this._multi) return undefined;

        if (symbol === Clutter.KEY_Tab) {
            this._toggleAt(this._selectedIndex);
            this._selectIndex(this._selectedIndex + 1);
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        }
        if (symbol === Clutter.KEY_space && (state & Clutter.ModifierType.CONTROL_MASK)) {
            this._toggleAt(this._selectedIndex);
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        }
        if ((symbol === Clutter.KEY_Return || symbol === Clutter.KEY_KP_Enter) &&
            (state & Clutter.ModifierType.SHIFT_MASK)) {
            this._toggleAt(this._selectedIndex);
            this._selectIndex(this._selectedIndex + 1);
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        }
        return undefined;
    }

    _handleEscape() {
        if (this._entry.get_text().length > 0) { this._entry.set_text(''); return; }
        this._close();
    }

    // ==================== result ====================
    _activateFocused() {
        if (this._selectedIndex >= 0) this._activateResult(this._selectedIndex);
        else if (this._checked.size > 0) this._finish(null);
        else if (this._query) {
            this._notifyResult([this._query, ARGS_MARKER, ...this._collectArgs()]);
            this._close();
        }
    }

    _activateResult(index) {
        this._finish(this._results[index] ?? null);
    }

    _finish(item) {
        if (this._closed) return;
        const checked = this._items.filter(it => this._checked.has(it.id));
        const chosen = checked.length ? checked : (item ? [item] : []);
        const labels = chosen.map(it => it.label).filter(Boolean);
        if (labels.length === 0) return;

        this._notifyResult([...labels, ARGS_MARKER, ...this._collectArgs()]);
        this._close();
    }

    _onBeforeClose() {
        this._strip?.destroy();
        this._strip = null;
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/workspaceIndicator.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/workspaceIndicator.js =====
import Clutter from 'gi://Clutter';
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import GObject from 'gi://GObject';
import Meta from 'gi://Meta';
import St from 'gi://St';

import * as ExtensionModule from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/extensions/extension.js';
import * as Main from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/main.js';
import * as PanelMenu from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/panelMenu.js';
import * as PopupMenu from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/popupMenu.js';
import * as AppFavorites from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/appFavorites.js';

import * as Model from './model.js';
import * as Scheduling from './scheduling.js';
import * as Interactions from './interactions.js';
import * as UI from './ui.js';
import * as WorkspaceActions from './workspaceActions.js';
import * as Motion from './motion.js';
import { S, REL } from './sizes.js';
import { Dwell, Poller } from './util.js';
import { GroupFlyout } from './flyout.js';
import { externalDrag, buttonHeld } from './externalDrag.js';

import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

const _ = ExtensionModule.gettext;

let _instance = null;
let _settleWindow = null;

const MIN_RENDER_INTERVAL_MS = 100;
const EXT_TICK_MS = 40;
const EXT_PREVIEW_MS = 150;
const SLACK_PX = 32;
const MAX_SOLVE_STEPS = 6;

const fmtNum = v => Number.isFinite(v) ? String(Math.round(v)) : 'NaN';

export function thumbnailAt(x, y) { return _instance?._thumbnailAt(x, y) ?? null; }
export function settleOnNextRender(window) { _settleWindow = window; }

export function toggleGroupFlyout(workspaceIndex, wmClass = '') {
    return _instance?._toggleGroupFlyout(workspaceIndex, wmClass) ?? false;
}

export function closeGroupFlyout() {
    return _instance?._closeGroupFlyout() ?? false;
}

export class WorkspaceIndicator extends PanelMenu.Button {
    static { GObject.registerClass(this); }

    constructor(settings, commands) {
        super(0.0, _('Workspace Indicator'), true);
        this.reactive = false;
        this._settings = settings;
        this._commands = commands;
        _instance = this;

        this._focusRelease = null;

        this._controllers = new Map();
        this._lastIconSize = undefined;
        this._lastRenderTime = undefined;
        this._toolbarKey = null;
        this._staticBox = null;
        this._nameLabel = null;
        this._thumbsBox = null;
        this._entries = [];

        this._collapsed = new Set();
        this._denseStep = 0;

        this._scope = new Scheduling.Scope();
        this._renderCoalescer = new Scheduling.Coalescer(() => this._render());
        this._focusCoalescer = new Scheduling.Coalescer(() => this._syncFocusStates());

        // DEBUG: log every focus change and its new id so we can correlate
        // the signal against _syncFocusStates' match. Remove after diagnosis.
        this._scope.connect(global.display, 'notify::focus-window', () => {
            const f = global.display.focus_window;
            let desc = 'null';
            try {
                if (f) desc = `${f.get_id()} "${f.get_title?.() ?? ''}" (${f.get_wm_class?.() ?? ''})`;
            } catch (e) { /* dead */ }
            journal(`[DEBUG-FOCUS] notify::focus-window -> ${desc}`);
            this._focusCoalescer.queue();
        });

        this._leavePollId = 0;

        this._groupDwell = new Dwell(this._scope,
            () => settings.get_int('flyout-dwell-ms'),
            { rearmAfterFire: true });
        this._extRowDwell = new Dwell(this._scope, EXT_PREVIEW_MS);
        this._extWsDwell = new Dwell(this._scope,
            () => settings.get_int('ext-ws-dwell-ms'));
        this._extPoll = new Poller(this._scope, {
            interval: EXT_TICK_MS,
            name: 'ext',
            tick: () => this._externalTickInner(),
        });

        this._groupIcons = new Map();

        this._flyout = new GroupFlyout(settings);

        this._extSawButton = false;
        this._extGroupKey = null;
        this._geoRelogId = 0;

        this._stickyRequest = null;

        this._lastHintWs = Model.intent.hintWs;
        this._lastDragActive = !!Model.intent.drag;
        this._lastMenuOpen = !!Model.intent.menuOpen;
        this._lastPick = Model.intent.overlayPick;

        this._reorder = Interactions.createReorderBar({ axis: 'x' });

        this._mainBox = new St.BoxLayout({
            style_class: 'workspace-indicator-main-box',
            y_expand: true, x_expand: true, reactive: true,
        });
        this.add_child(this._mainBox);

        this._unsubIntent = Model.intentStore.changed.on(() => this._onIntentChanged());
        this._unsubWorld = Model.world.on(() => this._queueRender());

        this._extUnsub = externalDrag.onChanged(state => this._onExternalChanged(state));

        this._render();

        // DEBUG: probe the generated CSS to confirm the pill rules reached
        // disk and the border-* longhand form is present. Remove after
        // diagnosis.
        try {
            const p = GLib.build_filenamev([GLib.get_user_cache_dir(), 'topnotch-theme.css']);
            const [ok, bytes] = GLib.file_get_contents(p);
            if (ok) {
                const css = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
                journal(`[DEBUG-FOCUS] CSS ${p}: ${css.length} bytes, ` +
                    `.focused=${css.includes('window-preview-icon.focused')}, ` +
                    `border-bottom-width=${css.includes('border-bottom-width')}`);
                const idx = css.indexOf('.window-preview-icon.focused');
                if (idx >= 0) {
                    const snippet = css.slice(idx, idx + 220).replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
                    journal(`[DEBUG-FOCUS] CSS snippet: ${snippet}`);
                }
            } else {
                journal(`[DEBUG-FOCUS] CSS not readable at ${p}`);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            journal(`[DEBUG-FOCUS] CSS probe threw: ${e.message}`);
        }
    }

    _dragActive() { return !!Model.intent.drag || externalDrag.active; }

    _onIntentChanged() {
        const pickChanged = Model.intent.overlayPick !== this._lastPick;
        this._lastPick = Model.intent.overlayPick;

        const hintChanged = Model.intent.hintWs !== this._lastHintWs;
        const isDragging = !!Model.intent.drag;
        const isMenu = !!Model.intent.menuOpen;
        const dragChanged = isDragging !== this._lastDragActive;
        const menuChanged = isMenu !== this._lastMenuOpen;

        this._lastHintWs = Model.intent.hintWs;
        this._lastDragActive = isDragging;
        this._lastMenuOpen = isMenu;

        if (dragChanged && isDragging) {
            Interactions.getTooltip().hide();
            if (this._flyout.isOpen) {
                this._cancelDwell();
                this._flyout.close(true);
            }
        }
        if (!this._dragActive() && !this._flyout.sticky) {
            this._cancelDwell();
            this._flyout.close(true);
        }
        if (hintChanged) this._syncHint();
        if (dragChanged || menuChanged) this._queueRender();
        if (pickChanged) this._syncFocusStates();
    }

    _queueRender(delay = 0) {
        const now = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
        const since = now - (this._lastRenderTime ?? 0);
        if (since < MIN_RENDER_INTERVAL_MS)
            delay = Math.max(delay, MIN_RENDER_INTERVAL_MS - since);
        this._renderCoalescer.queue(delay);
    }

    _render() {
        journal(`[DEBUG-FOCUS] _render entry menuOpen=${Model.intent.menuOpen} ` +
            `drag=${!!Model.intent.drag} extDrag=${externalDrag.active}`);

        if (Model.intent.menuOpen) return;
        if (Model.intent.drag) return;
        if (externalDrag.active) return;

        const hadFlyout = this._flyout.isOpen;
        const keepKey = hadFlyout ? this._flyout.anchorKey : null;
        if (hadFlyout && !keepKey) {
            journal('[flyout] re-render -> closing unkeyed flyout');
            this._flyout.close(true);
        }

        this._reorder.clear();

        const model = Model.buildModel();
        const s = this._settings;

        const iconSize = S.indicator.windowIcon;
        if (this._lastIconSize !== undefined && this._lastIconSize !== iconSize) {
            for (const ctrl of this._controllers.values()) ctrl.destroy();
            this._controllers.clear();
        }
        this._lastIconSize = iconSize;

        for (const ctrl of this._controllers.values()) {
            const parent = ctrl.actor.get_parent();
            if (parent) parent.remove_child(ctrl.actor);
        }

        const toolbarKey = [
            s.get_boolean('show-window-button'),
            s.get_boolean('show-workspace-names'),
            S.indicator.toolbarIcon,
            S.indicator.workspaceName,
        ].join('|');
        if (this._toolbarKey !== toolbarKey) {
            this._toolbarKey = toolbarKey;
            this._buildToolbar(s);
        }
        if (this._nameLabel) {
            this._nameLabel.set_text(Meta.prefs_get_workspace_name(
                Model.WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace_index()));
        }

        this._thumbsBox?.destroy();
        this._settleActor = null;
        this._entries = [];
        this._groupIcons.clear();

        this._thumbsBox = new St.BoxLayout({
            style_class: 'workspace-indicator-class',
            y_expand: true, reactive: true,
            clip_to_allocation: true,
        });
        this._mainBox.add_child(this._thumbsBox);

        const seen = new Set();
        for (const entry of model.workspaces) {
            this._entries.push(this._buildThumbShell(entry));
            this._thumbsBox.add_child(this._entries[this._entries.length - 1].actor);
        }
        for (const entry of model.workspaces)
            for (const w of entry.windows) seen.add(`${w.get_id()}:${entry.index}`);

        this._solveGrouping(model);

        for (const [key, ctrl] of this._controllers) {
            if (!seen.has(key)) {
                ctrl.destroy();
                this._controllers.delete(key);
            }
        }

        if (keepKey && this._flyout.isOpen) {
            const anchor = this._anchorForKey(keepKey);
            if (anchor) {
                this._flyout.retarget(anchor);
            } else {
                journal(`[flyout] re-render -> anchor for ${keepKey} gone, closing`);
                this._flyout.close(true);
            }
        }

        this._syncFocusStates();
        if (this._settleActor) Motion.settle(this._settleActor);
        this._syncHint();

        this._lastRenderTime = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
    }

    _syncFocusStates() {
        const pick = Model.intent.overlayPick;
        const focus = pick ? null : Model.focusedWindow();
        const focusId = focus?.get_id() ?? null;
        const pickId = pick?.get_id() ?? null;

        journal(`[DEBUG-FOCUS] sync: pick=${pickId} focus=${focusId} ` +
            `ctrls=${this._controllers.size} groups=${this._groupIcons.size}`);

        if (pick && !focus) {
            try {
                journal(`[DEBUG-FOCUS] pick active "${pick.get_title?.()}" — focus suppressed`);
            } catch (e) { /* dead */ }
        }

        const wid = w => { try { return w?.get_id?.(); } catch (e) { return undefined; } };

        const apply = (actor, windows, label) => {
            const matchFocus = focusId !== null && windows.some(w => wid(w) === focusId);
            const matchPick = pickId !== null && windows.some(w => wid(w) === pickId);

            let title = '?';
            try { title = windows[0]?.get_title?.() ?? '?'; } catch (e) { /* dead */ }

            const hadFocus = actor.has_style_class_name?.('focused') ?? false;
            const hadPick = actor.has_style_class_name?.('pick') ?? false;

            if (matchFocus !== hadFocus || matchPick !== hadPick) {
                journal(`[DEBUG-FOCUS] ${label} "${title}": ` +
                    `focused ${hadFocus}→${matchFocus} pick ${hadPick}→${matchPick}`);
            }

            UI.setFocusState(actor, { focused: matchFocus, pick: matchPick });
        };

        for (const ctrl of this._controllers.values()) apply(ctrl.actor, [ctrl.window], 'icon');
        for (const [actor, info] of this._groupIcons) apply(actor, info.group.windows, 'group');
    }

    _buildToolbar(s) {
        if (!this._staticBox) {
            this._staticBox = new St.BoxLayout({
                y_expand: true, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            });
            this._mainBox.insert_child_at_index(this._staticBox, 0);
        }
        this._staticBox.destroy_all_children();
        this._nameLabel = null;

        const iconSize = S.indicator.toolbarIcon;
        if (s.get_boolean('show-window-button')) {
            const b = UI.createToolbarButton('focus-windows-symbolic', iconSize, 'All windows');
            b.connect('clicked', () => this._commands.showAllWindows());
            this._staticBox.add_child(b);
        }
        if (s.get_boolean('show-workspace-names')) {
            this._nameLabel = UI.createWorkspaceNameLabel(
                Meta.prefs_get_workspace_name(Model.WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace_index()),
                S.indicator.workspaceName);
            this._staticBox.add_child(this._nameLabel);
        }
    }

    _buildThumbShell(entry) {
        const s = this._settings;

        const { actor: thumb, box } = UI.createThumbnail({
            minWidth: s.get_int('thumbnail-min-width'),
            active: entry.index === Model.WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace_index(),
        });
        thumb._wsEntry = { workspace: entry.workspace, index: entry.index };

        const content = new St.BoxLayout({
            style_class: 'ws-contents',
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        box.add_child(content);

        const shell = { entry, actor: thumb, content, strip: null };

        const strip = Interactions.createDropStrip({
            container: content,
            reorder: this._reorder,
            acceptSource: source => source?._dragPayload?.kind === 'window',
            externalResult: Interactions.DragMotionResult.MOVE_DROP,
            onDragOver: () => {
                this._ensureLeavePoll();
                Model.setIntent({ hintWs: entry.index });
            },
            onExternalDragOver: () => {
                if (externalDrag.active) return;
                if (entry.workspace !== Model.WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace())
                    entry.workspace.activate(global.get_current_time());
            },
            onDrop: (source, dropIndex, dragActor, insertion) => {
                this._cancelDwell();
                this._flyout.close(true);
                return this._dropOnWorkspace(thumb, entry, source,
                    this._resolveDrop(insertion), dragActor);
            },
        });
        shell.strip = strip;
        thumb._delegate = strip.delegate();

        thumb.connect('button-press-event', (actor, event) => {
            const b = event.get_button();
            if (b === Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY) entry.workspace.activate(0);
            else if (b === Clutter.BUTTON_SECONDARY) this._openThumbnailMenu(thumb, entry.workspace);
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        });

        return shell;
    }

    _isCollapsed(group) {
        if (group.count < 2) return false;
        if (this._settings.get_boolean('group-apps-always')) return true;
        return this._collapsed.has(group.key);
    }

    _isGrouped(entry) {
        return entry.groups.some(g => this._isCollapsed(g));
    }

    _iconSize() {
        const base = S.indicator.windowIcon;
        const dense = this._entries.some(e => this._isGrouped(e.entry));
        if (!dense) return base;
        const rung = Math.min(this._denseStep, REL.denseLadder.length - 1);
        return Math.max(S.indicator.denseFloor, Math.round(base * REL.denseLadder[rung]));
    }

    _widthBudget() {
        const panelW = Main.panel.width > 0
            ? Main.panel.width
            : Main.layoutManager.primaryMonitor.width;
        const [x] = this.get_transformed_position();
        const left = Number.isFinite(x) ? Math.max(0, x) : 0;

        let reserve = 28;
        const rb = Main.panel._rightBox;
        if (rb && rb.width > 0) reserve += rb.width + 12;
        else reserve += Math.round(panelW * 0.30);

        return Math.max(200, Math.min(panelW - left - reserve, Math.round(panelW * 0.62)));
    }

    _naturalWidth() {
        try { return this._mainBox.get_preferred_width(-1)[1]; }
        catch (e) { return 0; }
    }

    _pruneCollapsed(entries) {
        const valid = new Set();
        for (const e of entries)
            for (const g of e.entry.groups)
                if (g.count > 1) valid.add(g.key);
        for (const key of [...this._collapsed])
            if (!valid.has(key)) this._collapsed.delete(key);
    }

    _collapseCandidates(entries) {
        if (this._settings.get_boolean('group-apps-always')) return [];

        const activeIdx = Model.WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace_index();
        const out = [];
        for (const e of entries) {
            for (const g of e.entry.groups)
                if (g.count > 1) out.push({ key: g.key, count: g.count, wsIndex: e.entry.index });
        }
        out.sort((a, b) =>
            b.count - a.count ||
            (Number(b.wsIndex === activeIdx) - Number(a.wsIndex === activeIdx)) ||
            a.wsIndex - b.wsIndex);
        return out;
    }

    _solveGrouping() {
        const budget = this._widthBudget();
        this._pruneCollapsed(this._entries);
        const candidates = this._collapseCandidates(this._entries);

        this._denseStep = 0;
        this._renderContents();

        let steps = 0;

        if (this._naturalWidth() > budget) {
            for (const c of candidates) {
                if (steps++ >= MAX_SOLVE_STEPS) break;
                if (this._collapsed.has(c.key)) continue;
                this._collapsed.add(c.key);
                this._renderContents();
                if (this._naturalWidth() <= budget) break;
            }
        } else {
            const reverted = [...candidates].reverse();
            for (const c of reverted) {
                if (steps++ >= MAX_SOLVE_STEPS) break;
                if (!this._collapsed.has(c.key)) continue;
                this._collapsed.delete(c.key);
                this._renderContents();
                if (budget - this._naturalWidth() < SLACK_PX) {
                    this._collapsed.add(c.key);
                    this._renderContents();
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        let rungs = 0;
        while (this._naturalWidth() > budget &&
            this._denseStep < REL.denseLadder.length - 1 &&
            rungs++ < REL.denseLadder.length) {
            this._denseStep++;
            this._renderContents();
        }

        this._thumbsBox.style_class = this._collapsed.size > 0
            ? 'workspace-indicator-class grouped-layout'
            : 'workspace-indicator-class';
    }

    _renderContents() {
        for (const ctrl of this._controllers.values()) {
            const parent = ctrl.actor.get_parent();
            if (parent) parent.remove_child(ctrl.actor);
        }
        this._groupIcons.clear();

        const sz = this._iconSize();

        for (const shell of this._entries) {
            const { entry, content } = shell;
            content.destroy_all_children();

            if (!entry.windows.length) continue;

            for (const group of entry.groups) {
                if (this._isCollapsed(group)) {
                    content.add_child(this._buildGroupIcon(shell, group, sz));
                    continue;
                }
                group.windows.forEach(w =>
                    content.add_child(this._windowActor(w, entry.index, sz, shell.strip)));
            }
        }
    }

    _windowActor(w, wsIndex, iconSize, strip) {
        const key = `${w.get_id()}:${wsIndex}`;
        let ctrl = this._controllers.get(key);

        if (ctrl?.stale) {
            ctrl.destroy();
            this._controllers.delete(key);
            ctrl = undefined;
        }

        if (ctrl) {
            ctrl.setStrip(strip);
        } else {
            const { actor, icon } = UI.createWindowIconActor(w, iconSize);
            ctrl = new Interactions.WindowIconController({
                window: w, actor, icon,
                settings: this._settings,
                strip,
                onRequestRender: () => { ctrl.stale = true; this._queueRender(); },
                onHoverFlyout: (anchor, hoverWsIndex) =>
                    this._openHoverFlyout(anchor, hoverWsIndex, w),
                onHoverExit: () => this._closeHoverFlyout(),
            });
            this._controllers.set(key, ctrl);
        }

        if (w === _settleWindow) { _settleWindow = null; this._settleActor = ctrl.actor; }
        return ctrl.actor;
    }

    _buildGroupIcon(shell, group, iconSize) {
        const { entry } = shell;
        const { actor } = UI.createGroupButton({
            gicon: UI.getWindowAppIcon(group.windows[0]),
            count: group.count,
            size: iconSize,
        });
        actor.add_style_class_name('window-preview-icon');
        actor.accessible_name = `${group.appName}, ${group.count} windows`;
        actor.set_name(`group-${group.key}`);
        actor._groupWindows = group.windows;

        this._groupIcons.set(actor, { shell, entry, group });

        actor.connect('clicked', () => this._activateGroupWorkspace(entry));

        actor.connect('button-press-event', (a, e) => {
            if (e.get_button() === Clutter.BUTTON_SECONDARY) {
                this._openGroupMenu(actor, entry, group);
                return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
            }
            return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
        });

        actor.connect('notify::hover', () => this._onGroupHover(actor, shell, group));

        actor._delegate = {
            handleDragOver: source => {
                this._dwellGroup(shell, group, actor);
                return shell.strip.handleDragOver(source);
            },
            acceptDrop: (source, dragActor) => {
                this._cancelDwell();
                this._flyout.close(true);
                return shell.strip.acceptDrop(source, dragActor);
            },
        };

        return actor;
    }

    _activateGroupWorkspace(entry) {
        if (this._dragActive()) return;
        entry.workspace.activate(global.get_current_time());
    }

    _openGroupMenu(anchor, entry, group) {
        const app = UI.getWindowApp(group.windows[0]);
        const favorites = AppFavorites.getAppFavorites();

        Interactions.openContextMenu(anchor, menu => {
            if (app) {
                const appId = app.get_id();
                if (app.can_open_new_window())
                    menu.addAction(`New ${group.appName} window`,
                        () => app.open_new_window(global.get_current_time()));

                const appInfo = app.get_app_info();
                const actions = appInfo?.list_actions();
                if (actions?.length) {
                    for (const action of actions)
                        menu.addAction(`${appInfo.get_action_name(action)}`,
                            () => app.launch_action(action, 0, -1));
                }

                menu.addMenuItem(new PopupMenu.PopupSeparatorMenuItem());

                const isFav = favorites.isFavorite(appId);
                menu.addAction(isFav ? `Unpin ${group.appName}` : `Pin ${group.appName}`,
                    () => isFav ? favorites.removeFavorite(appId)
                        : favorites.addFavorite(appId));
                menu.addAction(`Close all ${group.appName} windows on workspace ${entry.index}`,
                    () => {
                        const now = global.get_current_time();
                        for (const w of group.windows) w.delete(now);
                    });
            }

            menu.addMenuItem(new PopupMenu.PopupSeparatorMenuItem());
            const wsWindows = Model.getModelWindows(entry.workspace);
            menu.addAction('Close all windows on all workspaces', () => {
                const now = global.get_current_time();
                for (const w of Model.getModelWindows(null)) w.delete(now);
            });
            if (wsWindows.length > 0) {
                menu.addAction(`Close all windows except workspace ${entry.index}`, () => {
                    const now = global.get_current_time();
                    for (const w of Model.getModelWindows(null)
                        .filter(w => w.get_workspace() !== entry.workspace))
                        w.delete(now);
                });
                menu.addAction(`Close all windows on workspace ${entry.index}`, () => {
                    const now = global.get_current_time();
                    for (const w of wsWindows) w.delete(now);
                });
            }
        }, { onStateChange: Model.menuIntent });
    }

    _dwellGroup(shell, group, anchor) {
        if (!this._dragActive()) return;
        if (group.count < 2) {
            journal(`[flyout] dwell skipped: ${group.appName} count=${group.count}`);
            return;
        }
        if (this._flyout.isOpen && this._flyout.anchor === anchor) return;
        const armed = this._groupDwell.arm(group.key, () => {
            if (!this._dragActive()) return;
            this._openFlyout(shell, group, anchor);
        });
        if (armed) journal(`[flyout] dwell armed: ${group.appName} x${group.count} key=${group.key}`);
    }

    _cancelDwell() {
        this._groupDwell.cancel();
    }

    _openFlyout(shell, group, anchor, { sticky = false, anchorKey = null, attempt = 0 } = {}) {
        if (!sticky && !this._dragActive()) {
            journal('[flyout] refusing non-sticky open — drag is not active');
            return;
        }

        try {
            const { entry } = shell;
            let anchorOnStage = false;
            try { anchorOnStage = !!anchor.get_stage(); } catch (e) { anchorOnStage = false; }
            journal(`[flyout] _openFlyout ${group.appName} ws=${entry.index} ` +
                `sticky=${sticky} attempt=${attempt} ` +
                `hasStrip=${!!shell.strip} anchorOnStage=${anchorOnStage}`);
            shell.strip.cancel();

            const items = group.windows.map((w, k) => ({
                window: w,
                index: group.indices[k],
                icon: UI.getWindowAppIcon(w),
                title: w.get_title() || 'Untitled Window',
                sub: `${group.appName}`.toUpperCase(),
                color: UI.colorForApp(group.appName),
            }));
            journal(`[flyout] items=${items.length}`);

            const opened = this._flyout.open({
                anchor,
                title: `${group.appName.toUpperCase()} · ${group.count} WINDOWS · WS ${entry.index + 1}`,
                items,
                onDrop: (source, item) => this._dropIntoGroup(shell, source, item),
                sticky,
                anchorKey,
                hoverActivate: sticky,
            });

            if (!opened) {
                let anchorAlive = false;
                try { anchorAlive = !!anchor.get_stage(); } catch (e) { anchorAlive = false; }
                if (attempt < 5 && anchorAlive) {
                    journal(`[flyout] deferring open — retry ${attempt + 1}/5 in 60ms`);
                    this._scope.timeout(60, () => {
                        if (!this._flyout.isOpen)
                            this._openFlyout(shell, group, anchor,
                                { sticky, anchorKey, attempt: attempt + 1 });
                        return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
                    });
                } else {
                    journal(`[flyout] giving up after ${attempt + 1} attempt(s)`);
                }
            }

            journal(`[flyout] open() returned, isOpen=${this._flyout.isOpen}`);
        } catch (e) {
            journal(`[flyout] _openFlyout THREW: ${e.message}\n${e.stack}`);
        }
    }

    _toggleGroupFlyout(workspaceIndex, wmClass = '') {
        if (this._dragActive()) return false;

        const entry = Model.buildModel().workspaces[workspaceIndex];
        if (!entry) return false;

        const group = entry.groups.find(g =>
            g.count > 1 && g.windows.some(w => w.get_wm_class() === wmClass));
        if (!group) {
            journal(`[flyout.dbus] no badged group with wmclass "${wmClass}" on ws ${workspaceIndex}`);
            return false;
        }

        const key = group.key;
        if (this._flyout.isOpen && this._flyout.anchorKey === key) {
            this._closeGroupFlyout();
            return false;
        }

        let anchor = null, shell = null;
        for (const [actor, info] of this._groupIcons)
            if (info.group.key === group.key) { anchor = actor; shell = info.shell; break; }
        if (!anchor) {
            const ctrl = this._controllers.get(`${group.windows[0].get_id()}:${entry.index}`);
            if (ctrl) {
                anchor = ctrl.actor;
                shell = this._entries.find(s => s.entry.index === entry.index) ?? null;
            }
        }
        if (!anchor || !shell) {
            journal(`[flyout.dbus] no anchor actor for ${group.key}`);
            return false;
        }

        this._openFlyout(shell, group, anchor, { sticky: true, anchorKey: key });
        if (this._flyout.isOpen) {
            this._stickyRequest = { workspaceIndex, wmClass };
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    _closeGroupFlyout() {
        this._stickyRequest = null;
        if (!this._flyout.isOpen) return false;
        journal('[flyout.dbus] close');
        this._flyout.close(true);
        return true;
    }

    _onGroupHover(actor, shell, group) {
        if (this._dragActive()) return;

        if (!actor.hover) {
            Interactions.getTooltip().hide();
            this._cancelHoverGroupDwell(group);
            return;
        }

        Interactions.getTooltip().showAbove(
            actor,
            `${group.appName} — ${group.count} windows`);

        this._groupDwell.arm(`hover-${group.key}`, () => {
            if (this._dragActive()) return;
            Interactions.getTooltip().hide();
            this._openHoverGroupFlyout(shell, group, actor);
        });
    }

    _cancelHoverGroupDwell(group) {
        if (this._groupDwell.key === `hover-${group.key}`) this._groupDwell.cancel();
    }

    _openHoverGroupFlyout(shell, group, anchor) {
        if (this._dragActive()) return;
        if (!anchor.get_stage()) return;

        const key = `hover-group:${group.key}`;
        if (this._flyout.isOpen && this._flyout.anchorKey === key) return;
        if (this._flyout.isOpen) this._flyout.close(true);

        const { entry } = shell;
        const items = group.windows.map((w, k) => ({
            window: w,
            index: group.indices[k],
            icon: UI.getWindowAppIcon(w),
            title: w.get_title() || 'Untitled Window',
            sub: group.appName.toUpperCase(),
            color: UI.colorForApp(group.appName),
        }));

        this._flyout.open({
            anchor,
            title: `${group.appName.toUpperCase()} · ${group.count} WINDOWS · WS ${entry.index + 1}`,
            items,
            onDrop: null,
            sticky: false,
            anchorKey: key,
            hoverActivate: true,
        });
    }

    _openHoverFlyout(anchor, wsIndex, win = null) {
        if (this._dragActive()) return;

        const key = `hover-ws:${wsIndex}`;
        if (this._flyout.isOpen) {
            if (this._flyout.anchorKey === key) {
                this._flyout.retarget(anchor);
                this._flyout.highlightWindow(win);
                return;
            }
            this._flyout.close(true);
        }

        const entry = Model.buildModel().workspaces[wsIndex];
        if (!entry || entry.windows.length === 0) return;

        const items = entry.windows.map(w => {
            const app = UI.getWindowApp(w);
            const appName = app?.get_name() || w.get_wm_class() || 'Unknown';
            return {
                window: w,
                icon: UI.getWindowAppIcon(w),
                title: w.get_title() || 'Untitled Window',
                sub: appName.toUpperCase(),
                color: UI.colorForApp(appName),
            };
        });

        const n = items.length;
        this._flyout.open({
            anchor,
            title: `WORKSPACE ${wsIndex + 1} · ${n} ${n === 1 ? 'WINDOW' : 'WINDOWS'}`,
            items,
            onDrop: null,
            sticky: false,
            anchorKey: key,
            hoverActivate: true,
            selectedWindow: win,
        });
    }

    _closeHoverFlyout() {
        if (!this._flyout?.isOpen) return;
        if (!this._flyout.anchorKey?.startsWith('hover-')) return;
        this._flyout.close(true);
    }

    _dropIntoGroup(shell, source, item) {
        const { entry } = shell;
        const win = source?._dragPayload?.window;
        if (!win) return false;

        const sameWs = win.get_workspace() === entry.workspace;
        Model.placeWindowNear(win, entry.workspace, item?.window ?? null, true);

        if (!sameWs) {
            const monitorIndex = Main.layoutManager.findIndexForActor(shell.actor);
            if (monitorIndex !== win.get_monitor()) win.move_to_monitor(monitorIndex);
            win.change_workspace(entry.workspace);
        }

        _settleWindow = win;
        Model.setIntent({ drag: null, hintWs: null });
        return true;
    }

    _onExternalChanged(state) {
        if (state) {
            if (!this._extPoll.running) this._startExternalPoll();
        } else {
            this._stopExternalPoll(true);
        }
    }

    _stopExternalPoll(teardown) {
        this._extPoll.stop();
        if (this._geoRelogId) { this._scope.cancel(this._geoRelogId); this._geoRelogId = 0; }

        this._focusRelease?.();
        this._focusRelease = null;

        if (!teardown) return;

        this._cancelExtRow();
        this._cancelExtWorkspace();
        this._cancelDwell();
        this._flyout?.close(true);

        this._extGroupKey = null;

        if (Model.intent.drag) Model.setIntent({ drag: null });
        Model.setIntent({ hintWs: null });
        this._queueRender();
    }

    _startExternalPoll() {
        this._cancelDwell();
        this._flyout.close(true);

        if (!this._focusRelease) this._focusRelease = Model.holdFocusHistory();

        this._extRowDwell.cancel();
        this._extSawButton = false;
        this._extWsDwell.cancel();
        this._extGroupKey = null;
        this._extLogAt = 0;
        if (this._geoRelogId) { this._scope.cancel(this._geoRelogId); this._geoRelogId = 0; }

        journal('[ext] poll START');

        if (this._extPoll.running) return;
        this._extPoll.start();

        this._geoRelogId = this._scope.timeout(120, () => {
            this._geoRelogId = 0;
            if (externalDrag.active) this._logGroupIcons();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _logGroupIcons(attempt = 0) {
        journal(`[ext] groupIcons=${this._groupIcons.size}`);

        let pending = false;
        for (const [actor, info] of this._groupIcons) {
            const [x, y] = actor.get_transformed_position();
            const [w, h] = actor.get_transformed_size();
            const ready = [x, y, w, h].every(Number.isFinite);
            if (!ready) pending = true;

            journal(`[ext]   group ${info.group.appName} x${info.group.count} ` +
                `pos=${fmtNum(x)},${fmtNum(y)} size=${fmtNum(w)}x${fmtNum(h)} ` +
                `visible=${actor.visible} mapped=${actor.is_mapped()} onStage=${!!actor.get_stage()}`);

            if (!ready) continue;

            let a = actor;
            let depth = 0;
            while (a && depth++ < 12) {
                let line;
                try {
                    const [px, py] = a.get_transformed_position();
                    const [aw, ah] = a.get_size();
                    line = `[ext]     ${a.constructor.name}` +
                        `${a.name ? `(${a.name})` : ''} ` +
                        `pos=${fmtNum(px)},${fmtNum(py)} size=${fmtNum(aw)}x${fmtNum(ah)} ` +
                        `mapped=${a.is_mapped()} scale=${a.scale_x},${a.scale_y}`;
                } catch (e) {
                    line = `[ext]     ${a.constructor?.name} threw: ${e.message}`;
                }
                journal(line);
                a = a.get_parent();
            }
        }

        if (pending && attempt < 3) {
            journal(`[ext] geometry pending — re-logging in 120ms (${attempt + 1}/3)`);
            if (this._geoRelogId) this._scope.cancel(this._geoRelogId);
            this._geoRelogId = this._scope.timeout(120, () => {
                this._geoRelogId = 0;
                if (externalDrag.active) this._logGroupIcons(attempt + 1);
                return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
            });
        }
    }

    _externalTickInner() {
        if (!externalDrag.active) {
            this._stopExternalPoll(true);
            return false;
        }

        const [x, y, mods] = global.get_pointer();

        if (y < Main.panel.height + 4) {
            journal(`[ext] PANEL x=${x} y=${y} ` +
                `overGroup=${!!this._groupIconAt(x, y)} ` +
                `overWinIcon=${!!this._windowIconAt(x, y)} ` +
                `overThumb=${!!this._thumbnailAt(x, y)}`);
        }

        const nowMs = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
        if (nowMs - (this._extLogAt ?? 0) > 500) {
            this._extLogAt = nowMs;
            const picked = global.stage.get_actor_at_pos(
                Clutter.PickMode.REACTIVE, x, y);
            journal(`[ext] tick x=${x} y=${y} mods=${mods} held=${buttonHeld(mods)} ` +
                `sawButton=${this._extSawButton} ` +
                `flyoutOpen=${this._flyout.isOpen} sticky=${this._flyout.sticky} ` +
                `overGroup=${!!this._groupIconAt(x, y)} ` +
                `overWinIcon=${!!this._windowIconAt(x, y)} ` +
                `overThumb=${!!this._thumbnailAt(x, y)} ` +
                `picked=${picked?.constructor?.name}:${picked?.name ?? ''}`);
        }

        if (buttonHeld(mods)) this._extSawButton = true;
        else if (this._extSawButton) {
            journal('[ext] button released -> end');
            externalDrag.end('button-released');
            return false;
        }

        if (this._flyout.isOpen) {
            const hit = this._flyout.pointerAt(x, y);
            if (hit?.item?.window) this._trackExternalRow(hit.item.window);
            else this._cancelExtRow();
            return true;
        }

        const group = this._groupIconAt(x, y);
        if (group) {
            if (this._extGroupKey !== group.group.key) {
                this._extGroupKey = group.group.key;
                const [gx, gy] = group.actor.get_transformed_position();
                journal(`[ext] group hit ${group.group.appName} x${group.group.count} ` +
                    `key=${group.group.key} anchorPos=${fmtNum(gx)},${fmtNum(gy)}`);
            }
            this._dwellGroup(group.shell, group.group, group.actor);
            this._cancelExtRow();
            return true;
        }
        if (this._extGroupKey) {
            this._extGroupKey = null;
            journal('[ext] group left');
        }

        const ctrl = this._windowIconAt(x, y);
        if (ctrl) {
            ctrl.notifyExternalDrag();
            this._cancelDwell();
            this._cancelExtRow();
            return true;
        }

        this._cancelDwell();
        this._maybeDwellWorkspace(x, y);
        return true;
    }

    _trackExternalRow(win) {
        this._extRowDwell.arm(String(win.get_id()), () => this._previewExternalWindow(win));
    }

    _cancelExtRow() {
        this._extRowDwell.cancel();
    }

    _previewExternalWindow(win) {
        WorkspaceActions.focusWindow(win);
        try {
            journal(`[ext] preview ${win.get_id()} "${win.get_title()}" (kept maximized)`);
        } catch (e) { /* window may have been destroyed mid-call */ }
    }

    _maybeDwellWorkspace(x, y) {
        const thumb = this._thumbnailAt(x, y);
        if (!thumb || thumb.workspace === Model.WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace()) {
            this._cancelExtWorkspace();
            return;
        }

        this._extWsDwell.arm(`ws:${thumb.index}`, () => {
            if (!externalDrag.active) return;
            try {
                Model.WorkspaceManager.get_workspace_by_index(thumb.index)
                    ?.activate(global.get_current_time());
            } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
        });

        Model.setIntent({ hintWs: thumb.index });
    }

    _cancelExtWorkspace() {
        this._extWsDwell.cancel();
    }

    _actorHit(x, y, actor) {
        if (!actor || !actor.get_stage() || !actor.visible) return false;
        const [ax, ay] = actor.get_transformed_position();
        const [aw, ah] = actor.get_transformed_size();
        if (![ax, ay, aw, ah].every(Number.isFinite)) return false;
        if (ay + ah < Main.panel.y) return false;
        return x >= ax && x <= ax + aw && y >= ay && y <= ay + ah;
    }

    _groupIconAt(x, y) {
        for (const [actor, info] of this._groupIcons)
            if (this._actorHit(x, y, actor)) return { ...info, actor };
        return null;
    }

    _windowIconAt(x, y) {
        for (const ctrl of this._controllers.values())
            if (this._actorHit(x, y, ctrl.actor)) return ctrl;
        return null;
    }

    _anchorForKey(key) {
        const alive = a => { try { return !!a?.get_stage(); } catch (e) { return false; } };

        if (key.startsWith('hover-ws:')) {
            const wsIndex = parseInt(key.slice('hover-ws:'.length), 10);
            const shell = this._entries.find(s => s.entry.index === wsIndex);
            if (!shell) return null;
            for (const w of shell.entry.windows) {
                const ctrl = this._controllers.get(`${w.get_id()}:${wsIndex}`);
                if (ctrl && alive(ctrl.actor)) return ctrl.actor;
            }
            for (const [actor, info] of this._groupIcons)
                if (info.entry.index === wsIndex && alive(actor)) return actor;
            return null;
        }

        const groupKey = key.startsWith('hover-group:') ? key.slice('hover-group:'.length) : key;
        for (const [actor, info] of this._groupIcons)
            if (info.group.key === groupKey && alive(actor)) return actor;

        for (const shell of this._entries) {
            const group = shell.entry.groups.find(g => g.key === groupKey);
            if (!group) continue;
            const ctrl = this._controllers.get(`${group.windows[0].get_id()}:${shell.entry.index}`);
            if (ctrl && alive(ctrl.actor)) return ctrl.actor;
        }
        return null;
    }

    _syncHint() {
        const s = this._settings;
        const wsIndex = Model.intent.hintWs;

        this._thumbsBox?.get_children().forEach((thumb, i) => {
            if (i === wsIndex) thumb.add_style_class_name('drop-target');
            else thumb.remove_style_class_name('drop-target');
        });

        const target = (wsIndex !== null && this._thumbsBox)
            ? this._thumbsBox.get_child_at_index(wsIndex)
            : null;
        if (!target || !s.get_boolean('show-workspace-names')) {
            Interactions.getTooltip().hide();
            return;
        }
        Interactions.getTooltip().showAbove(
            target,
            Meta.prefs_get_workspace_name(wsIndex));
    }

    _openThumbnailMenu(anchor, workspace) {
        const windows = Model.getModelWindows(workspace);
        Interactions.openContextMenu(anchor, menu => {
            menu.addAction('Close all windows on all workspaces', () => {
                const now = global.get_current_time();
                for (const w of Model.getModelWindows(null)) w.delete(now);
            });
            if (windows.length > 0) {
                menu.addAction(`Close all windows except workspace ${workspace.index()}`, () => {
                    const now = global.get_current_time();
                    for (const w of Model.getModelWindows(null).filter(w => w.get_workspace() !== workspace))
                        w.delete(now);
                });
                menu.addAction(`Close all windows on workspace ${workspace.index()}`, () => {
                    const now = global.get_current_time();
                    for (const w of windows) w.delete(now);
                });
            }
        }, { onStateChange: Model.menuIntent });
    }

    _resolveDrop(insertion) {
        const a = insertion?.neighbor;
        if (!a) return null;
        const wins = a._dragPayload?.window ? [a._dragPayload.window] : a._groupWindows;
        if (!wins?.length) return null;
        return {
            neighbor: insertion.insertBefore ? wins[0] : wins[wins.length - 1],
            before: insertion.insertBefore,
        };
    }

    _dropOnWorkspace(thumb, entry, source, target, dragActor) {
        Model.setIntent({ hintWs: null });

        const win = source?._dragPayload?.window ?? null;
        if (!win) return true;

        const sameWs = win.get_workspace() === entry.workspace;

        if (dragActor && dragActor !== source._actor) {
            dragActor.get_parent()?.remove_child(dragActor);
            try { dragActor.destroy(); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
        }

        if (sameWs && !target) { Model.setIntent({ drag: null }); return true; }

        Model.placeWindowNear(win, entry.workspace,
            target?.neighbor ?? null, target?.before ?? true);
        if (!sameWs) {
            const monitorIndex = Main.layoutManager.findIndexForActor(thumb);
            if (monitorIndex !== win.get_monitor()) win.move_to_monitor(monitorIndex);
            win.change_workspace(entry.workspace);
        }
        _settleWindow = win;
        Model.setIntent({ drag: null });
        return true;
    }

    _ensureLeavePoll() {
        if (this._leavePollId) return;
        this._leavePollId = this._scope.timeout(100, () => {
            const [px, py, mods] = global.get_pointer();
            const held = buttonHeld(mods) || externalDrag.active;
            if (!held || !this._thumbnailAt(px, py)) {
                this._leavePollId = 0;
                this._cancelDwell();
                Model.setIntent({ hintWs: null });
                this._reorder.clear();
                return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
            }
            return GLib.SOURCE_CONTINUE;
        });
    }

    _thumbnailAt(x, y) {
        if (!this._thumbsBox) return null;
        for (const thumb of this._thumbsBox.get_children()) {
            if (!thumb.get_stage() || !thumb._wsEntry) continue;
            const [tx, ty] = thumb.get_transformed_position();
            const [tw, th] = thumb.get_transformed_size();
            if (![tx, ty, tw, th].every(Number.isFinite)) continue;
            if (ty + th < Main.panel.y) continue;
            if (x >= tx && x <= tx + tw && y >= ty && y <= ty + th)
                return { ...thumb._wsEntry, actor: thumb };
        }
        return null;
    }

    destroy() {
        this._cancelDwell();
        this._stopExternalPoll(true);
        this._extUnsub?.();
        this._extUnsub = null;

        this._renderCoalescer.cancel();
        this._focusCoalescer.cancel();
        this._unsubIntent?.(); this._unsubIntent = null;
        this._unsubWorld?.(); this._unsubWorld = null;
        Model.resetIntent();

        for (const ctrl of this._controllers.values()) ctrl.destroy();
        this._controllers.clear();

        this._flyout?.destroy();
        this._flyout = null;

        this._groupIcons.clear();

        this._reorder?.destroy();
        this._reorder = null;

        _instance = null;
        _settleWindow = null;

        this._scope.dispose();
        this._mainBox?.destroy();

        Main.panel.set_offscreen_redirect(Clutter.OffscreenRedirect.ALWAYS);

        super.destroy();
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/focusedWindowBorder.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/focusedWindowBorder.js =====
// Border around the focused window. Eligibility: Model.focusedWindow().
// A suppressor can veto it (selected windows keep their own border).
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import * as Model from './model.js';
import { WindowBorder } from './windowBorder.js';

const border = new WindowBorder('focused-border');
let _suppressFn = () => false;
let _refreshId = 0;
let _ids = [];

function _draw(win) {
    if (!win || win.minimized || _suppressFn(win)) { border.hide(); return; }
    border.show(win);
}
function _refresh() { _draw(Model.focusedWindow()); }
function _scheduleRefresh() {
    if (_refreshId) return;
    _refreshId = GLib.idle_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT_IDLE, () => {
        _refreshId = 0;
        _refresh();
        return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
    });
}

export function setFocusBorderSuppressor(fn) { _suppressFn = fn ?? (() => false); _refresh(); }
export function refreshFocusedWindowBorder() { _refresh(); }

export function initFocusedWindowBorder() {
    const d = global.display, wm = global.window_manager;
    const onWm = (_wm, actor) => { if (actor === border.actor) _scheduleRefresh(); };
    _ids = [
        [d, d.connect('notify::focus-window', _scheduleRefresh)],
        [wm, wm.connect('minimize', onWm)],
        [wm, wm.connect('unminimize', onWm)],
        [d, d.connect('restacked', () => { border.restack(); _scheduleRefresh(); })],
    ];
    _refresh();
}

export function destroyFocusedWindowBorder() {
    for (const [obj, id] of _ids) { try { obj.disconnect(id); } catch (e) { /* gone */ } }
    _ids = [];
    if (_refreshId) { GLib.Source.remove(_refreshId); _refreshId = 0; }
    border.destroy();
    _suppressFn = () => false;
}

const RADIUS_RATIO = { xs: 0.15, sm: 0.20, md: 0.30, lg: 0.50, xl: 0.70 };
export const BORDER = { hair: 1, thin: 2, thick: 3, frame: 4 };

export const SHADOW = {
    glow: [0, 56, 22, 'bgDeep', 0.48],
    drop: [12, 36, 10, 'shadow', 0.78],
    panel: [0, 48, 20, 'shadow', 0.55],
    edge: [0, 40, 10, 'shadow', 0.65],
    band: [0, 90, 48, 'shadow', 0.34],
};

let _glass = GLASS_DEFAULT;
const _hex = nameOrHex => PALETTE[nameOrHex] ?? PALETTE[ROLES[nameOrHex]] ?? nameOrHex;
const _clamp01 = v => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));

export function rgba(color, a = 1) {
    const h = _hex(color).replace('#', '');
    const n = parseInt(h, 16);
    return `rgba(${(n >> 16) & 255}, ${(n >> 8) & 255}, ${n & 255}, ${Math.round(a * 1000) / 1000})`;
}
export function alpha(tier) {
    const a = TIERS[tier] ?? 1;
    return SCALABLE.has(tier) ? _clamp01(a * _glass) : a;
}
export const color = nameOrHex => _hex(nameOrHex);
export const surface = name => {
    const [role, tier] = SURFACES[name];
    return rgba(role, alpha(tier));
};
export const line = name => {
    const [role, a] = LINES[name];
    return rgba(role, a);
};
export const radius = key => px(RADIUS_RATIO[key]);
export const shadow = (name, xOffset = 0) => {
    const [y, blur, spread, role, a] = SHADOW[name];
    return `${xOffset}px ${y}px ${blur}px ${spread}px ${rgba(role, a)}`;
};

export function appColor(name) {
    const s = name || '';
    let h = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
    return color(APP_COLORS[Math.abs(h) % APP_COLORS.length]);
}

export const C = {
    get accent() { return color('accent'); },
    get muted() { return color('textMuted'); },
    get text() { return color('text'); },
    get danger() { return color('danger'); },
};

const bg = name => `background-color: ${surface(name)};`;
const ln = (name, w = BORDER.hair, style = 'solid') => `border: ${w}px ${style} ${line(name)};`;
const rad = key => `border-radius: ${radius(key)}px;`;
const halo = `text-shadow: 0px 0px 3px ${rgba('base03', 1)};`;
const ring = name => `box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px ${line(name)};`;

.pinned-edge-menu .popup-menu-item { ${t('menuItem')} ${rad('md')} }
.pinned-edge-menu .popup-menu-item:hover { ${bg('menuItemHover')} color: ${color('textStrong')}; }
.pinned-edge-menu .popup-menu-item:insensitive { ${t('menuItemDisabled')} }

}

const _css = createCssLoader('topnotch-theme', buildCss);
const _listeners = new Set();
let _settings = null;
let _ids = [];

export function onThemeChanged(cb) { _listeners.add(cb); return () => _listeners.delete(cb); }

function _refresh() {
    _css.apply();
    for (const cb of [..._listeners]) {
        try { cb(); } catch (e) { journal(`[theme] listener: ${e.message}`); }
    }
}

export function initTheme(settings = null) {
    _settings = settings;
    const read = () => {
        const schema = settings?.settings_schema;
        _glass = schema?.has_key('glass-strength')
            ? settings.get_int('glass-strength') / 100 : GLASS_DEFAULT;
    };
    read();
    _css.apply();

    if (settings && settings.settings_schema.has_key('glass-strength'))
        _ids.push(settings.connect('changed::glass-strength', () => { read(); _refresh(); }));
}

export const refreshTheme = _refresh;

export function destroyTheme() {
    for (const id of _ids) _settings?.disconnect(id);
    _ids = [];
    _settings = null;
    _listeners.clear();
    _css.destroy();
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/motion.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/motion.js =====
import Clutter from 'gi://Clutter';

const Mode = Clutter.AnimationMode;

const _cfg = {
    reduce: false,
    speed: 1,
    durations: {
        fadeIn: 200, fadeOut: 130, settle: 300, pop: 220,
        slideIn: 190, slideOut: 130, glide: 150,
        tooltipFade: 120, tooltipGlide: 100, dashOpen: 180, dashClose: 140,
        selectBorder: 150, selectPopOut: 150, overlayFade: 220, cancelFlash: 180,
    },
};

export function configureMotion({ reduceMotion, speed, durations } = {}) {
    if (reduceMotion !== undefined) _cfg.reduce = !!reduceMotion;
    if (speed !== undefined) _cfg.speed = speed;
    if (durations) Object.assign(_cfg.durations, durations);
}
export const reduced = () => _cfg.reduce;

export function dur(nameOrMs) {
    if (_cfg.reduce) return 0;
    const ms = typeof nameOrMs === 'number' ? nameOrMs : _cfg.durations[nameOrMs];
    if (ms === undefined) {
        console.error(`[motion] unknown duration: ${nameOrMs}`);
        return 0;
    }
    return Math.round(ms * _cfg.speed);
}

export function animate(actor, props, { duration = 'fadeIn', mode = Mode.EASE_OUT_QUAD, onComplete } = {}) {
    if (!actor) return;
    try { if (!actor.get_stage()) return; } catch (e) { return; }
    actor.remove_all_transitions();
    const ms = dur(duration);
    if (ms === 0) {
        for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(props)) actor[k] = v;
        onComplete?.();
        return;
    }
    actor.ease({ ...props, duration: ms, mode, onComplete });
}

export function fadeIn(actor, o = {}) {
    if (!actor) return;
    try { if (!actor.get_stage()) return; } catch (e) { return; }
    actor.opacity = 0;
    animate(actor, { opacity: 255 }, { duration: 'fadeIn', ...o });
}
export function fadeOut(actor, o = {}) {
    animate(actor, { opacity: 0 }, { duration: 'fadeOut', mode: Mode.EASE_IN_QUAD, ...o });
}
export function settle(actor, o = {}) {
    if (!actor) return;
    try { if (!actor.get_stage()) return; } catch (e) { return; }
    actor.remove_all_transitions();
    actor.set_pivot_point(0.5, 0.5);
    actor.set_scale(0.85, 0.85);
    actor.opacity = 160;
    animate(actor, { scale_x: 1, scale_y: 1, opacity: 255 }, { duration: 'settle', ...o });
}
export function pop(actor, { from = 0.96, ...o } = {}) {
    if (!actor) return;
    actor.set_pivot_point(0.5, 0.5);
    actor.set_scale(from, from);
    animate(actor, { scale_x: 1, scale_y: 1 }, { duration: 'pop', mode: Mode.EASE_OUT_BACK, ...o });
}
export function enter(actor, { dx = 0, dy = 0, scale = 0.96, ...o } = {}) {
    actor.remove_all_transitions();
    actor.show();
    actor.opacity = 255;
    actor.translation_x = dx; actor.translation_y = dy;
    actor.set_scale(scale, scale);
    animate(actor, { translation_x: 0, translation_y: 0, scale_x: 1, scale_y: 1 },
        { duration: 'slideIn', mode: Mode.EASE_OUT_CUBIC, ...o });
}
export function exit(actor, { dx = 0, dy = 0, scale = 0.97, ...o } = {}) {
    animate(actor, { opacity: 0, translation_x: dx, translation_y: dy, scale_x: scale, scale_y: scale },
        { duration: 'slideOut', mode: Mode.EASE_IN_QUAD, ...o });
}
export function glide(actor, x, y, o = {}) {
    if (Math.abs(actor.x - x) < 1 && Math.abs(actor.y - y) < 1) return;
    animate(actor, { x, y }, { duration: 'glide', ...o });
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/overlayBase.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/overlayBase.js =====
// Shared base for all full-screen search overlays: modal grab, entry
// wiring, keyboard dispatch, and — critically — the focus-window
// watcher that closes an overlay when focus changes to something the
// overlay didn't cause.
//
// The focus check is state-based, not timer-based. An overlay records
// the window that had focus when it opened; a focus change to that
// window, to a window the overlay itself revealed, or to the origin
// window of a cancelled overlay is ignored. Any other change (a real
// app getting focus, a window being closed, etc.) closes the overlay.
// A transient null focus — which happens for one or two signal
// emissions while pushModal/popModal run — is ignored too.

import Clutter from 'gi://Clutter';
import Shell from 'gi://Shell';

import * as Main from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/main.js';

import * as Scheduling from './scheduling.js';
import * as Interactions from './interactions.js';

import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

export const clamp = (v, lo, hi) => Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v));
export const ease = t => { t = clamp(t, 0, 1); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };

// ==================== shared overlay base ====================
export class SearchOverlayBase {
    static _current = null;

    static get current() {
        if (SearchOverlayBase._current?._closed) SearchOverlayBase._current = null;
        return SearchOverlayBase._current;
    }

    static closeCurrent() {
        const overlay = SearchOverlayBase.current;
        if (!overlay) return false;
        overlay._close();
        return true;
    }

    constructor(settings) {
        this._settings = settings;
        this._results = [];
        this._selectedIndex = -1;
        this._query = '';
        this._initialKey = null;
        this._modalGrab = null;
