        journal(`[dbusService] CLI symlinked: ${symlinkPath}`);
    } catch (e) {
        journal(`[dbusService] CLI install failed (${name}): ${e.message}`, true);
    }
}

function _removeOneCli(extensionPath, { name, path }) {
    const cliScript = GLib.build_filenamev([extensionPath, ...path]);
    const symlinkPath = GLib.build_filenamev([
        GLib.get_home_dir(), '.local', 'bin', name]);

    try {
        const linkFile = Gio.File.new_for_path(symlinkPath);
        if (!linkFile.query_exists(null)) return;
        const info = linkFile.query_info(
            Gio.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_STANDARD_SYMLINK_TARGET,
            Gio.FileQueryInfoFlags.NOFOLLOW_SYMLINKS, null);
        if (info.get_symlink_target() === cliScript) {
            linkFile.delete(null);
            journal(`[dbusService] CLI symlink removed: ${name}`);
        }
    } catch (e) {
        journal(`[dbusService] CLI remove failed (${name}): ${e.message}`, true);
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/flyout.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/flyout.js =====
// The prototype's #fan.local-anchored: the same strip, detached from the
// HUD and parked next to a dock icon. Opened by a dwell during an internal
// window drag (delegate.handleDragOver) or by the external-drag poll
// (pointerAt) — both drive the same PagedStrip. Also openable without a
// drag ("sticky", e.g. from DBus; or "hover", from an icon hover).
//
// Sizing: every dimension below scales with sizes.js. ROW_SLOT in
// strip.js is sized so row icons and labels fit without clipping.

import Clutter from 'gi://Clutter';
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import Pango from 'gi://Pango';
import St from 'gi://St';

import * as Main from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/main.js';

import * as Model from './model.js';
import * as Scheduling from './scheduling.js';
import * as Interactions from './interactions.js';
import * as UI from './ui.js';
import * as WorkspaceActions from './workspaceActions.js';
import * as Motion from './motion.js';
import { S } from './sizes.js';
import { Dwell, Poller, Misses } from './util.js';
import { PagedStrip, VISIBLE_SLOTS } from './strip.js';

import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));

// Hover-activate velocity gate — dwell only arms while the pointer has been
// moving slower than HOVER_VELOCITY_MAX for HOVER_VELOCITY_WINDOW_MS.
// Prevents activation while the pointer is still traveling from the anchor
// to a target row.
const HOVER_VELOCITY_WINDOW_MS = 120;
const HOVER_VELOCITY_MAX = 0.25;   // px / ms
const HOVER_POLL_MS = 30;

export class GroupFlyout {
    constructor(settings) {
        this._settings = settings;
        this._scope = new Scheduling.Scope();
        this._open = false;
        this._items = [];
        this._anchor = null;
        this._onDrop = null;
        this._opensDown = true;
        this._rowsW = 240;
        this._watchLogAt = 0;
        this._sticky = false;
        this._anchorKey = null;
        this._side = null;

        this._misses = new Misses(2, () => {
            journal('[flyout.watch] pointer left -> close');
            this.close();
        });
        this._hoverDwell = new Dwell(this._scope,
            () => this._settings.get_int('flyout-dwell-ms'));
        this._watch = new Poller(this._scope, {
            interval: 120,
            name: 'flyout.watch',
            tick: () => this._watchTick(),
        });

        // Velocity-gated hover activation (see constants above).
        this._motionSamples = [];
        this._hoverPoll = new Poller(this._scope, {
            interval: HOVER_POLL_MS,
            name: 'flyout.hover',
            tick: () => this._hoverTick(),
        });

        this._wheelAccum = 0;

        this._hoverActivate = false;

        this._root = new St.Widget({
            style_class: 'ws-flyout',
            reactive: true, track_hover: true, clip_to_allocation: true,
        });
        this._root.opacity = 0;
        this._root.visible = false;

        this._head = new St.Label({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-head', text: '' });
        this._head.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.END);
        this._head.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(false);

        this._rowsEl = new St.Widget({ clip_to_allocation: true });
        this._upEl = new St.Widget({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-edge ws-flyout-edge-up' });
        this._downEl = new St.Widget({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-edge ws-flyout-edge-down' });
        this._rail = UI.createRail();

        this._root.add_child(this._head);
        this._root.add_child(this._upEl);
        this._root.add_child(this._downEl);
        this._root.add_child(this._rail.root);
        this._root.add_child(this._rowsEl);

        Main.layoutManager.addChrome(this._root);

        this._strip = new PagedStrip({
            rowsEl: this._rowsEl,
            upEl: this._upEl,
            downEl: this._downEl,
            rail: this._rail,
            makeRow: item => this._makeRow(item),
            onEdgeStart: () => { this._misses.reset(); },
            alive: () => this._open,
        });

        this._root._delegate = {
            handleDragOver: () => {
                this._misses.reset();
                const [, py] = global.get_pointer();
                const [, ry] = this._root.get_transformed_position();
                const y = py - ry;
                this._strip.over(y);
                return Interactions.DragMotionResult.MOVE_DROP;
            },
            acceptDrop: source => {
                const idx = this._strip.highlighted;
                const item = idx >= 0 ? this._items[idx] : null;
                const handled = this._onDrop ? this._onDrop(source, item) : false;
                this.close(true);
                return handled;
            },
        };

        this._scope.connect(this._root, 'captured-event', (a, event) => {
            if (!this._open) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            const type = event.type();

            if (type === Clutter.EventType.SCROLL) {
                let step = 0;
                const dir = event.get_scroll_direction();
                if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.UP) step = -1;
                else if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.DOWN) step = 1;
                else if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.SMOOTH) {
                    const [, dy] = event.get_scroll_delta();
                    this._wheelAccum += dy;
                    if (Math.abs(this._wheelAccum) >= 1) {
                        step = Math.sign(this._wheelAccum);
                        this._wheelAccum = 0;
                    }
                } else return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;

                if (step) {
                    this._hoverDwell.cancel();
                    this._strip.scrollBy(step);
                }
                return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
            }

            if (type !== Clutter.EventType.MOTION) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            const [, sy] = event.get_coords();
            const [, ry] = this._root.get_transformed_position();
            // Edge-scroll + highlight only. Activation is driven by _hoverPoll.
            this._strip.over(sy - ry);
            return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
        });
        this._scope.connect(this._root, 'leave-event', () => {
            this._hoverDwell.cancel();
            if (this._open) {
                this._strip.stopEdge();
                this._strip.clearHover();
            }
            return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
        });
    }

    get isOpen() { return this._open; }
    get anchor() { return this._anchor; }
    get items() { return this._items; }
    get sticky() { return this._sticky; }
    get anchorKey() { return this._anchorKey; }

    _onHoverRow(idx) {
        const item = idx >= 0 ? this._items[idx] : null;
        if (!item?.window) {
            this._hoverDwell.cancel();
            return;
        }
        this._hoverDwell.arm(idx, () => {
            if (!this._open) return;
            WorkspaceActions.focusWindow(item.window);
            journal(`[flyout] hover preview "${item.title}"`);
        });
    }

    _cancelHoverActivate() { this._hoverDwell.cancel(); }

    // Called every HOVER_POLL_MS while a hover-activate flyout is open.
    // Samples the pointer, gates dwell arming on velocity, and lets
    // _onHoverRow arm/cancel the dwell exactly as before once the pointer
    // has settled.
    _hoverTick() {
        if (!this._open || !this._hoverActivate) return false;

        const [px, py] = global.get_pointer();
        if (!this.contains(px, py)) {
            this._hoverDwell.cancel();
            this._motionSamples.length = 0;
            return true;
        }

        this._pushSample(px, py);
        if (this._pointerMoving()) {
            this._hoverDwell.cancel();
            return true;
        }

        const [, ry] = this._root.get_transformed_position();
        this._onHoverRow(this._strip.over(py - ry));
        return true;
    }

    _pushSample(x, y) {
        const now = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
        const s = this._motionSamples;
        s.push({ t: now, x, y });
        while (s.length > 1 && now - s[0].t > HOVER_VELOCITY_WINDOW_MS) s.shift();
    }

    _pointerMoving() {
        const s = this._motionSamples;
        if (s.length < 2) return true;    // no data yet — be conservative
        const first = s[0], last = s[s.length - 1];
        const dt = last.t - first.t;
        if (dt <= 0) return true;
        return Math.hypot(last.x - first.x, last.y - first.y) / dt > HOVER_VELOCITY_MAX;
    }

    _startWatch() {
        if (this._watch.running) return;
        journal('[flyout.watch] started');
        this._watchLogAt = 0;
        this._watch.start();
    }

    _watchTick() {
        if (!this._open || this._sticky) {
            journal('[flyout.watch] stopped');
            return false;
        }
        const [x, y] = global.get_pointer();
        const over = this._isOver(x, y);

        const nowMs = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
        if (nowMs - (this._watchLogAt ?? 0) > 500) {
            this._watchLogAt = nowMs;
            let rx = NaN, ry = NaN, rw = NaN, rh = NaN;
            try {
                [rx, ry] = this._root.get_transformed_position();
                [rw, rh] = this._root.get_transformed_size();
            } catch (e) { /* actor may be mid-destroy */ }
            journal(`[flyout.watch] ptr=${x},${y} over=${over} ` +
                `bounds=${Math.round(rx)},${Math.round(ry)} ${Math.round(rw)}x${Math.round(rh)} ` +
                `opacity=${this._root.opacity} visible=${this._root.visible} ` +
                `mapped=${this._root.is_mapped()} scale=${this._root.get_scale().join(',')}`);
        }

        if (over) this._misses.reset();
        else this._misses.miss();
        return true;
    }

    _makeRow(item) {
        const row = UI.createFlyoutRow({
            gicon: item.icon,
            title: item.title,
            sub: item.sub || '',
            badge: item.badge || '',
            width: this._rowsW,
            height: S.flyout.rowSlot,
            iconSize: S.flyout.rowIcon,
            labelSize: S.flyout.rowLabel,
        });

        row.connect('button-press-event', (a, e) => {
            if (e.get_button() !== Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            this._activateItem(item);
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        });
        return row;
    }

    _activateItem(item) {
        if (!item?.window) return;
        WorkspaceActions.focusWindow(item.window);
        journal(`[flyout] row activate "${item.title}"`);
        this.close(true);
    }

    _isOver(x, y) {
        return this.contains(x, y) || this.isOverAnchor(x, y);
    }

    contains(x, y) {
        if (!this._open || !this._root.get_stage()) return false;
        const [rx, ry] = this._root.get_transformed_position();
        const [rw, rh] = this._root.get_transformed_size();
        return x >= rx && x <= rx + rw && y >= ry && y <= ry + rh;
    }

    isOverAnchor(x, y) {
        const a = this._anchor;
        if (!this._open || !a) return false;
        try {
            if (!a.get_stage()) return false;
            const [ax, ay] = a.get_transformed_position();
            const [aw, ah] = a.get_transformed_size();
            return x >= ax && x <= ax + aw && y >= ay && y <= ay + ah;
        } catch (e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    pointerAt(x, y) {
        if (!this.contains(x, y)) {
            this._strip.stopEdge();
            if (this._strip.highlighted >= 0) this._strip.clearHover();
            return null;
        }
        const [, ry] = this._root.get_transformed_position();
        const idx = this._strip.over(y - ry);
        if (idx < 0) return null;
        return { index: idx, item: this._items[idx] ?? null };
    }

    retarget(anchor) {
        if (!this._open || !anchor) return;
        if (this._anchor === anchor) return;
        this._anchor = anchor;
        this._misses.reset();
    }

    get actor() { return this._root; }

    highlightIndex(idx) {
        if (!this._open) return;
        this._strip.stopEdge();
        this._strip.setHighlight(idx);
    }

    highlightWindow(win) {
        if (!this._open || !win) return;
        const idx = this._items.findIndex(i => i.window === win);
        if (idx < 0) return;
        this._strip.setHighlight(idx);
        this._strip.center(idx);
    }

    open({
        anchor, title, items, onDrop,
        sticky = false, anchorKey = null, hoverActivate = false,
        selectedWindow = null,
        side = null, sideGap = 8, width = null,
    }) {
        let onStage = false;
        try { onStage = !!anchor?.get_stage(); } catch (e) { onStage = false; }
        if (!anchor || !onStage || !items?.length) {
            journal(`[flyout.open] rejected anchor=${!!anchor} onStage=${onStage} items=${items?.length}`);
            return false;
        }

        if (this._open && this._anchor === anchor) { this._misses.reset(); return true; }

        // Reset any in-flight hover poll from a previous open().
        this._hoverPoll.stop();
        this._motionSamples.length = 0;

        const monitorIndex = Math.max(0, Main.layoutManager.findIndexForActor(anchor));
        const monitor = Main.layoutManager.monitors[monitorIndex] ?? Main.layoutManager.primaryMonitor;

        this._anchor = anchor;
        this._items = items;
        this._onDrop = onDrop;
        this._hoverActivate = hoverActivate;
        this._wheelAccum = 0;
        this._hoverDwell.cancel();

        const F = S.flyout;
        const PAD = F.pad, RAIL_W = F.railW, GAP = F.gap, HEAD_H = F.headH, EDGE_H = F.edgeH;

        const W = width ?? clamp(Math.round(monitor.width * 0.26), 720, 1440);
        const rowsW = W - PAD * 2 - RAIL_W - GAP;

        const chromeH = PAD * 2 + HEAD_H + EDGE_H * 2;
        const maxRowsH = Math.max(F.rowSlot, monitor.height - 120 - chromeH);
        const slots = Math.max(1, Math.min(VISIBLE_SLOTS, items.length,
            Math.floor(maxRowsH / F.rowSlot)));
        const rowsH = slots * F.rowSlot;
        const H = chromeH + rowsH;

        const headY = PAD;
        const upY = headY + HEAD_H;
        const rowsY = upY + EDGE_H;
        const downY = rowsY + rowsH;

        this._rowsW = rowsW;

        this._head.set_position(PAD, headY);
        this._head.set_size(W - PAD * 2, HEAD_H);
        this._head.set_text(title || '');

        this._rowsEl.set_position(PAD, rowsY);
        this._rowsEl.set_size(rowsW, rowsH);

        this._rail.root.set_position(W - PAD - RAIL_W, rowsY);
        this._rail.root.set_size(RAIL_W, rowsH);

        this._upEl.set_position(0, upY);
        this._upEl.set_size(W, EDGE_H);
        this._downEl.set_position(0, downY);
        this._downEl.set_size(W, EDGE_H);

        this._root.set_size(W, H);

        let ax, ay, aw, ah;
        try {
            [ax, ay] = anchor.get_transformed_position();
            [aw, ah] = anchor.get_transformed_size();
        } catch (e) {
            journal(`[flyout.open] anchor geometry threw: ${e.message}`);
            return false;
        }
        if (![ax, ay, aw, ah].every(Number.isFinite) || aw <= 0 || ah <= 0) {
            journal(`[flyout.open] anchor not allocated yet: ax=${ax} ay=${ay} aw=${aw} ah=${ah}`);
            return false;
        }

        this._side = side;
        let left, top;
        if (side === 'left') {
            this._opensDown = false;
            left = Math.max(monitor.x + 8, ax - W - sideGap);
            top = clamp(ay + ah / 2 - H / 2, monitor.y + 8,
                Math.max(monitor.y + 8, monitor.y + monitor.height - H - 8));
        } else {
            left = clamp(ax + aw / 2 - W / 2, monitor.x + 8, monitor.x + monitor.width - W - 8);
            top = ay - H - 16;
            this._opensDown = false;
            if (top < monitor.y + 8) {
                top = Math.max(monitor.y + 8, ay + ah + 16);
                this._opensDown = true;
            }
            if (top + H > monitor.y + monitor.height - 8)
                top = Math.max(monitor.y + 8, monitor.y + monitor.height - H - 8);
        }

        this._root.set_position(Math.round(left), Math.round(top));

        try {
            Main.layoutManager.uiGroup.set_child_above_sibling(this._root, null);
        } catch (e) {
            journal(`[flyout.open] set_child_above_sibling failed: ${e.message}`);
        }

        journal(`[flyout.open] W=${W} H=${H} slots=${slots} ` +
            `left=${Math.round(left)} top=${Math.round(top)} side=${side} ` +
            `hoverActivate=${hoverActivate} sticky=${sticky}`);

        if (side) {
            this._root.set_pivot_point(1, 0.5);
        } else {
            const px = clamp((ax + aw / 2 - left) / W, 0.08, 0.92);
            this._root.set_pivot_point(px, this._opensDown ? 0 : 1);
        }

        this._strip.setMaxSlots(slots);
        this._strip.setViewport(H);

        const selIdx = selectedWindow
            ? items.findIndex(i => i.window === selectedWindow)
            : -1;

        this._strip.setItems(items, Math.max(0, selIdx));
        this._strip.clearHover();
        if (selIdx >= 0) this._strip.setHighlight(selIdx);

        this._open = true;
        this._sticky = sticky;
        this._anchorKey = anchorKey;
        this._misses.reset();

        // Velocity-gated hover activation only runs for hover-activate
        // flyouts; every other mode relies on clicks / drags.
        if (hoverActivate) this._hoverPoll.start();

        this._root.remove_all_transitions();
        Motion.enter(this._root, {
            dx: side ? 16 : 0,
            dy: side ? 0 : (this._opensDown ? -12 : 12),
        });

        if (sticky) {
            journal('[flyout.open] sticky — watchdog off, rows are click-to-activate');
        } else {
            this._startWatch();
        }

        return true;
    }

    close(instant = false) {
        if (!this._open) return;
        journal(`[flyout.close] instant=${instant} sticky=${this._sticky}`);
        this._open = false;
        this._sticky = false;
        this._anchorKey = null;
        this._hoverDwell.cancel();
        this._hoverPoll.stop();
        this._motionSamples.length = 0;
        this._wheelAccum = 0;
        this._strip.stopEdge();
        this._strip.clearHover();
        this._onDrop = null;
        this._root.remove_all_transitions();

        if (instant || Motion.dur('slideOut') === 0) {
            this._root.hide();
            this._anchor = null;
            this._side = null;
            return;
        }

        Motion.exit(this._root, {
            dx: this._side ? 16 : 0,
            dy: this._side ? 0 : (this._opensDown ? -12 : 12),
            onComplete: () => { if (!this._open) this._root.hide(); },
        });
        this._anchor = null;
        this._side = null;
    }

    destroy() {
        this._open = false;
        this._sticky = false;
        this._anchorKey = null;
        this._side = null;
        this._hoverDwell.cancel();
        this._hoverPoll.stop();
        this._motionSamples.length = 0;
        this._watch.stop();
        this._strip?.destroy();
        this._scope.dispose();
        try { Main.layoutManager.removeChrome(this._root); } catch (e) { /* already gone */ }
        this._root.destroy();
        this._root = null;
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/edgeHud.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/edgeHud.js =====
// Right-edge HUD for external drags (files from Nautilus, Firefox, ...).
//
//   edge dwell  -> HUD opens: all active apps, grouped across workspaces
//   hover app   -> app's window is focused, sub HUD lists its windows
//   hover window-> that window is focused
//   leave both  -> everything closes
//
// Both lists are GroupFlyout instances. External drags never enter St's
// DND chain, so everything is driven by a pointer poll via
// GroupFlyout.pointerAt().

import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import St from 'gi://St';

import * as Main from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/main.js';

import * as Model from './model.js';
import * as Scheduling from './scheduling.js';
import * as UI from './ui.js';
import * as WorkspaceActions from './workspaceActions.js';
import { GroupFlyout } from './flyout.js';
import { externalDrag } from './externalDrag.js';
import { Dwell, Poller, Misses } from './util.js';
import { base } from './sizes.js';

import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

const EDGE_PX = 8;
const OPEN_DWELL_MS = 150;
const TICK_MS = 40;
const PREVIEW_MS = 150;
const MISS_LIMIT = 5;

const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));
const plural = (n, w) => `${n} ${w}${n === 1 ? '' : 'S'}`;

export class EdgeHud {
    constructor(settings) {
        this._settings = settings;
        this._scope = new Scheduling.Scope();
        this._hud = new GroupFlyout(settings);
        this._sub = new GroupFlyout(settings);

        this._edge = null;
        this._edgeKey = '';

        this._apps = [];
        this._appIdx = -1;

        this._edgeSince = 0;

        this._appDwell = new Dwell(this._scope, PREVIEW_MS);
        this._winDwell = new Dwell(this._scope, PREVIEW_MS);

        this._poll = new Poller(this._scope, {
            interval: TICK_MS,
            name: 'edgeHud',
            tick: () => this._tick(),
        });
        this._missCount = new Misses(MISS_LIMIT, () => {
            journal('[edgeHud] pointer left -> close');
            this._closeAll();
        });

        this._focusRelease = null;

        this._unsub = externalDrag.onChanged(state => state ? this._start() : this._stop());
        if (externalDrag.active) this._start();
    }

    // ---------- lifecycle ----------
    _start() {
        if (this._poll.running) return;
        const mon = Main.layoutManager.primaryMonitor;
        if (!mon) return;
        this._ensureEdge(mon);
        this._edgeSince = 0;
        this._missCount.reset();
        this._poll.start();
    }

    _stop() {
        this._poll.stop();
        this._closeAll();
    }

    _closeAll() {
        this._cancelApp();
        this._cancelWin();
        this._sub.close(true);
        this._hud.close(true);
        this._appIdx = -1;
        this._edgeSince = 0;
        this._missCount.reset();
        this._focusRelease?.();
        this._focusRelease = null;
    }

    // ---------- geometry ----------
    _hasRightNeighbor(mon) {
        return Main.layoutManager.monitors.some(o =>
            o !== mon
            && o.x >= mon.x + mon.width
            && o.y < mon.y + mon.height
            && o.y + o.height > mon.y);
    }

    _ensureEdge(mon) {
        const panelH = Main.panel?.height ?? 0;
        const key = `${mon.x},${mon.y},${mon.width},${mon.height},${panelH}`;
        if (this._edge && this._edgeKey === key) return this._edge;
        this._removeEdge();
        this._edge = new St.Widget({
            reactive: false,
            x: mon.x + mon.width - 1, y: mon.y + panelH,
            width: 1, height: mon.height - panelH,
        });
        Main.layoutManager.addChrome(this._edge,
            { affectsInputRegion: false, trackFullscreen: false });
        this._edgeKey = key;
        return this._edge;
    }

    _removeEdge() {
        if (!this._edge) return;
        try { Main.layoutManager.removeChrome(this._edge); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
        try { this._edge.destroy(); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
        this._edge = null;
        this._edgeKey = '';
    }

    _width(mon) {
        const b = base();
        return Math.round(clamp(mon.width * 0.22, 13 * b, 22.5 * b));
    }

    // ---------- data ----------
    _buildApps() {
        const byId = new Map();
        const seen = new Set();
        const focus = Model.Display.focus_window;

        for (const w of Model.getModelWindows(null)) {
            if (seen.has(w)) continue;
            seen.add(w);
            const { id, name } = Model.appIdentity(w);
            let g = byId.get(id);
            if (!g) { g = { id, name, windows: [] }; byId.set(id, g); }
            g.windows.push(w);
        }

        return [...byId.values()].map(g => {
            const rep = g.windows.includes(focus) ? focus
                : (g.windows.find(w => !w.minimized) ?? g.windows[0]);
            const n = g.windows.length;
            return {
                ...g, rep,
                item: {
                    window: rep,
                    icon: UI.getWindowAppIcon(rep),
                    title: g.name,
                    sub: plural(n, 'WINDOW'),
                    badge: n > 1 ? `×${n}` : '',
                    color: UI.colorForApp(g.name),
                },
            };
        });
    }

    _windowItems(app) {
        return app.windows.map(w => ({
            window: w,
            icon: UI.getWindowAppIcon(w),
            title: w.get_title() || 'Untitled Window',
            sub: `WS ${(w.get_workspace()?.index() ?? 0) + 1}`,
            color: UI.colorForApp(app.name),
        }));
    }

    // ---------- poll ----------
    _tick() {
        if (!externalDrag.active) return;
        const mon = Main.layoutManager.primaryMonitor;
        if (!mon || this._hasRightNeighbor(mon)) return;

        const [x, y] = global.get_pointer();
        const top = mon.y + (Main.panel?.height ?? 0) + 4;
        const atEdge = x >= mon.x + mon.width - EDGE_PX
            && y >= top && y < mon.y + mon.height;

        if (!this._hud.isOpen) {
            if (!atEdge) { this._edgeSince = 0; return; }
            const now = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
            if (!this._edgeSince) { this._edgeSince = now; return; }
            if (now - this._edgeSince >= OPEN_DWELL_MS) this._open(mon);
            return;
        }

        const subIn = this._sub.isOpen && this._sub.contains(x, y);
        if (subIn) {
            this._hud.highlightIndex(this._appIdx);
            this._cancelApp();
            const hit = this._sub.pointerAt(x, y);
            this._trackWindow(hit?.item?.window ?? null);
            this._missCount.reset();
            return;
        }

        if (this._sub.isOpen) this._sub.pointerAt(x, y);
        this._cancelWin();

        if (this._hud.contains(x, y)) {
            const hit = this._hud.pointerAt(x, y);
            this._trackApp(hit ? hit.index : -1);
            this._missCount.reset();
            return;
        }

        if (atEdge) { this._missCount.reset(); return; }

        this._cancelApp();
        this._missCount.miss();
    }

    _open(mon) {
        this._apps = this._buildApps();
        if (!this._apps.length) return;

        const opened = this._hud.open({
            anchor: this._ensureEdge(mon),
            title: `APPS · ${this._apps.length}`,
            items: this._apps.map(a => a.item),
            onDrop: null,
            sticky: true,
            anchorKey: 'edge-hud',
            hoverActivate: false,
            side: 'left', sideGap: 0,
            width: this._width(mon),
        });
        if (!opened) return;

        this._edgeSince = 0;
        this._missCount.reset();
        this._appIdx = -1;
        if (!this._focusRelease) this._focusRelease = Model.holdFocusHistory();
        journal(`[edgeHud] open apps=${this._apps.length}`);
    }

    // ---------- app rows ----------
    _trackApp(idx) {
        if (idx < 0 || idx === this._appIdx) {
            this._appDwell.cancel();
            return;
        }
        this._appDwell.arm(idx, () => this._showApp(idx));
    }

    _showApp(idx) {
        const app = this._apps[idx];
        if (!app || !this._hud.isOpen) return;

        this._appIdx = idx;
        WorkspaceActions.focusWindow(app.rep);

        this._sub.close(true);
        if (app.windows.length < 2) return;

        const mon = Main.layoutManager.primaryMonitor;
        this._sub.open({
            anchor: this._hud.actor,
            title: `${app.name.toUpperCase()} · ${plural(app.windows.length, 'WINDOW')}`,
            items: this._windowItems(app),
            onDrop: null,
            sticky: true,
            anchorKey: 'edge-hud-sub',
            hoverActivate: false,
            selectedWindow: app.rep,
            side: 'left', sideGap: 8,
            width: this._width(mon),
        });
    }

    _cancelApp() {
        this._appDwell.cancel();
    }

    // ---------- window rows ----------
    _trackWindow(win) {
        if (!win) {
            this._winDwell.cancel();
            return;
        }
        this._winDwell.arm(String(win.get_id()), () => WorkspaceActions.focusWindow(win));
    }

    _cancelWin() {
        this._winDwell.cancel();
    }

    destroy() {
        this._unsub?.();
        this._unsub = null;
        this._poll.stop();
        this._closeAll();
        this._scope.dispose();
        this._sub.destroy();
        this._hud.destroy();
        this._removeEdge();
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/smoother.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/smoother.js =====
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import { reduced } from './motion.js';

export class Smoother {
    constructor(scope, { tau = 70, epsilon = 0.002, frameMs = 16, onFrame, onSettle = null }) {
        this._scope = scope;
        this.tau = tau; this.epsilon = epsilon; this.frameMs = frameMs;
        this._onFrame = onFrame; this._onSettle = onSettle;
        this.value = 0; this.target = 0;
        this._id = 0; this._last = 0;
    }
    get running() { return this._id !== 0; }

    snap(v) { this.stop(); this.value = this.target = v; this._onFrame(v); }

    to(target) {
        this.target = target;
        if (reduced()) return this.snap(target);
        if (this._id) return;
        this._last = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
        this._id = this._scope.timeout(this.frameMs, () => this._step());
    }

    _step() {
        const now = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
        const dt = Math.min(now - this._last, 50);
        this._last = now;
        const diff = this.target - this.value;
        if (Math.abs(diff) < this.epsilon) {
            this.value = this.target;
            this._id = 0;
            this._onFrame(this.value);
            this._onSettle?.();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        }
        this.value += diff * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / this.tau));
        this._onFrame(this.value);
        return GLib.SOURCE_CONTINUE;
    }

    stop() { if (this._id) { this._scope.cancel(this._id); this._id = 0; } }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/sizes.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/sizes.js =====
// SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH for text/icon sizes.  px = round(BASE × ratio).
//
// ICONS: every content icon renders at 96px — the original `icon-size`
// default from the schema. This ratio (2.40) is applied to the panel
// window icon, flyout row icon, dmenu row icon, launcher tile / pinned
// icon, selector band / row icon, and dash icon, so every icon you see
// that represents a window or app is the same size.
//
// TEXT: 40px uniform (ratio 1.00) — matches the original
// `workspace-name-font-size` default.
//
// UI CHROME icons (search magnifier, toolbar button, close X) are
// NOT content icons and stay at their original smaller sizes.
//
// Layout ratios are sized so 96px icons + 40px text fit comfortably.
import { createCssLoader } from './cssLoader.js';

export const BASE_DEFAULT = 40;

const RATIOS = {
    // Magnifier in the search field — UI chrome, not a content icon
    search: { text: 1.00, icon: 1.00, hint: 1.00 },

    indicator: {
        windowIcon: 2.00,       //  96 — panel window / drag icon (was icon-size=96)
        toolbarIcon: 2.00,      //  56 — toolbar button (was toolbar-icon-size=56, UI chrome)
        workspaceName: 1.00,    //  40 — text
        closeButton: 0.80,      //  32 — close X (was close-button-size=32, UI chrome)
        closeOffsetX: 1.50,     //  60 — positioning
        closeOffsetY: 0.25,     //  10 — positioning
        previewHeight: 20,      // 800 — clone preview height
        denseFloor: 2.40,       //  96 — never shrink below the global content-icon size
    },

    badge: { min: 0.60, labelMin: 0.35 },

    tooltip: { text: 1.00 },    //  40 — text

    flyout: {
        head: 1.00,             //  40 — text
        rowIcon: 2.00,          //  96 — icon (was ROW_ICON_SIZE=96)
        rowLabel: 1.00,         //  40 — text
        rowSub: 1.00,           //  40 — text
        rowBadge: 1.00,         //  40 — text
        cancel: 1.00,           //  40 — text
        edge: 1.00,             //  40 — text
        rowSlot: 3.60,          // 144 — fits 96 icon (matches original)
        headH: 1.80,            //  72 — header strip
        edgeH: 1.50,            //  60 — edge hold zones
        cancelH: 2.00,          //  80 — cancel drop zone

    },

    dmenu: {

        rowLabel: 1.00,         //  40
        rowIcon: 1.00,          //  96 — icon (was 72)
        rowHeight: 3.40,        // 136 — row fits 96 icon
        badge: 1.00,            //  40
        empty: 1.00,            //  40
        sectionLabel: 1.00,     //  40
        sectionCount: 1.00,     //  40
        controlName: 1.00,      //  40
        segment: 1.00,          //  40
        summary: 1.00,          //  40
        confirm: 1.00,          //  40
        controlRowH: 2.10,      //  84
        controlHeadH: 1.20,     //  48
        footerH: 2.10,          //  84
        edgeH: 1.20,            //  48
        railW: 1.20,            //  48
        railGap: 0.30,          //  12
        stripGap: 0.30,         //  12
        searchHeight: 3.00,     // 120
    },

    dash: {
        icon: 2.00,             //  96 — icon (was 128)
        menuItem: 1.00,         //  40 — menu text
        tooltipGap: 0.55,       //  22
    },
};

export const REL = {
    badgeOfIcon: 0.75,
    badgeLabelOfBadge: 0.56,
    denseLadder: [1],   // content icons stay at the global size (no dense down-scaling)
};

export const S = {};
let _base = BASE_DEFAULT;
const _listeners = new Set();

export const base = () => _base;
export const px = r => Math.max(1, Math.round(_base * r));

function _resolve() {
    for (const [g, entries] of Object.entries(RATIOS)) {
        S[g] ??= {};
        for (const [k, r] of Object.entries(entries)) S[g][k] = px(r);
    }
}
_resolve();

const f = v => `font-size: ${v}px;`;
const i = v => `icon-size: ${v}px;`;

function buildCss() {
    return `
.topnotch-tooltip { ${f(S.tooltip.text)} }
.overlay-search-entry { ${f(S.search.text)} }
.overlay-search-icon { ${i(S.search.icon)} }
.overlay-search-hint { ${f(S.search.hint)} }
.ws-flyout-head { ${f(S.flyout.head)} }
.ws-flyout-row-app { ${f(S.flyout.rowSub)} }
.ws-flyout-row-badge { ${f(S.flyout.rowBadge)} }
.ws-flyout-cancel-label { ${f(S.flyout.cancel)} }
.ws-flyout-edge-label { ${f(S.flyout.edge)} }
.launcher-heading { ${f(S.launcher.heading)} }
.launcher-tile-label { ${f(S.launcher.tileLabel)} }
.dmenu-panel .overlay-search-entry { ${f(S.dmenu.search)} }
.dmenu-panel .overlay-search-icon { ${i(S.dmenu.searchIcon)} }
.dmenu-panel .overlay-search-hint { ${f(S.dmenu.searchHint)} }
.dmenu-panel .ws-flyout-edge-label { ${f(S.dmenu.edgeLabel)} }
.dmenu-result-label { ${f(S.dmenu.rowLabel)} }
.dmenu-badge { ${f(S.dmenu.badge)} }
.dmenu-empty { ${f(S.dmenu.empty)} }
.dmenu-section-label { ${f(S.dmenu.sectionLabel)} }
.dmenu-section-count { ${f(S.dmenu.sectionCount)} }
.dmenu-control-name { ${f(S.dmenu.controlName)} }
.dmenu-segment { ${f(S.dmenu.segment)} }
.dmenu-summary { ${f(S.dmenu.summary)} }
.dmenu-confirm { ${f(S.dmenu.confirm)} }
.pinned-edge-menu .popup-menu-item { ${f(S.dash.menuItem)} }
`;
}

const _css = createCssLoader('topnotch-sizes', buildCss);
let _settings = null, _sid = 0;

export function setBase(v) {
    v = Math.max(8, Math.round(v));
    if (v === _base) return;
    _base = v; _resolve(); _css.apply();
    for (const cb of [..._listeners]) {
        try { cb(); } catch (e) { console.error(`[sizes] listener: ${e.message}`); }
    }
}
export function onSizesChanged(cb) { _listeners.add(cb); return () => _listeners.delete(cb); }

export function initSizes(settings = null) {
    _settings = settings;
    const has = settings?.settings_schema?.has_key('base-size');
    _base = has ? settings.get_int('base-size') : BASE_DEFAULT;
    _resolve(); _css.apply();
    if (has) _sid = settings.connect('changed::base-size',
        () => setBase(settings.get_int('base-size')));
}
export function destroySizes() {
    if (_settings && _sid) _settings.disconnect(_sid);
    _settings = null; _sid = 0; _listeners.clear(); _css.destroy();
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/panel.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/panel.js =====
// Panel reorder / reposition / session-mode-layout edits, ported from
// fix-panel-order-by-blueray453. Uses this extension's Scope for signal cleanup.

import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import * as Main from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/main.js';

import { Scope } from './scheduling.js';
import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

const BOXES = ['left', 'center', 'right'];
const DELAY_MS = 100;
const HIDDEN_ROLES = new Set(['activities']);
const DATE_MENU = 'dateMenu';

export class PanelManager {
    constructor(settings) {
        this._settings = settings;
        this._scope = new Scope();
        this._positionScope = null;
        this._timeoutId = 0;

        this._modePanel = null;
        this._origPanelLayout = null;
        this._origFindDraggable = null;
        this._panelMoved = false;

        this._panelMoved = settings.get_boolean('move-panel-to-bottom');
        if (this._panelMoved) this._movePanelPosition(true);

        this._backupLayout();
        this._applyLayout();
        this._disablePanelWindowDrag(settings.get_boolean('disable-panel-window-drag'));

        for (const type of BOXES) {
            const box = Main.panel[`_${type}Box`];
            for (const sig of ['child-added', 'child-removed'])
                this._scope.connect(box, sig, () => this._schedule());
            this._scope.connect(settings, `changed::order-${type}`, () => this._sync());
        }

        this._scope.connect(settings, 'changed::move-panel-to-bottom',
            () => this._onPanelPositionChanged());
        this._scope.connect(settings, 'changed::hide-activities',
            () => this._applyLayout());
        this._scope.connect(settings, 'changed::date-menu-position',
            () => this._applyLayout());
        this._scope.connect(settings, 'changed::disable-panel-window-drag',
            () => this._disablePanelWindowDrag(
                this._settings.get_boolean('disable-panel-window-drag')));

        this._schedule();
    }

    destroy() {
        if (this._timeoutId) {
            try { GLib.Source.remove(this._timeoutId); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
            this._timeoutId = 0;
        }
        this._scope.dispose();
        this._positionScope?.dispose();
        this._positionScope = null;

        if (this._panelMoved) {
            this._movePanelPosition(false);
            this._panelMoved = false;
        }
        this._restoreLayout();
        this._disablePanelWindowDrag(false);
        this._settings = null;
    }

    // ----- panel position -----
    _placePanel() {
        const { panelBox, primaryMonitor: m } = Main.layoutManager;
        if (!m) return;
        panelBox.set_position(m.x, m.y + m.height - panelBox.height);
    }

    _movePanelPosition(active) {
        this._positionScope?.dispose();
        this._positionScope = null;

        const lm = Main.layoutManager;
        if (active) {
            this._placePanel();
            this._positionScope = new Scope();
            this._positionScope.connect(lm, 'monitors-changed', () => this._placePanel());
            this._positionScope.connect(lm.panelBox, 'notify::height', () => this._placePanel());
        } else {
            const m = lm.primaryMonitor;
            if (m) lm.panelBox.set_position(m.x, m.y);
        }
    }

    _onPanelPositionChanged() {
        const should = this._settings.get_boolean('move-panel-to-bottom');
        if (should === this._panelMoved) return;
        this._movePanelPosition(should);
        this._panelMoved = should;
    }

    // ----- session-mode layout -----
    _backupLayout() {
        if (this._modePanel) return;
        const panel = Main.sessionMode.panel;
        this._modePanel = panel;
        this._origPanelLayout = {
            left: [...panel.left],
            center: [...panel.center],
            right: [...panel.right],
        };
    }

    _restoreLayout() {
        if (!this._modePanel) return;
        Object.assign(this._modePanel, this._origPanelLayout);
        this._modePanel = null;
        this._origPanelLayout = null;
        Main.panel._updatePanel();
    }

    _applyLayout() {
        if (!this._modePanel) return;
        this._modePanel.left = [...this._origPanelLayout.left];
        this._modePanel.center = [...this._origPanelLayout.center];
        this._modePanel.right = [...this._origPanelLayout.right];

        if (this._settings.get_boolean('hide-activities'))
            this._hideActivities();
        this._applyDateMenuPosition();

        Main.panel._updatePanel();
    }

    _hideActivities() {
        const panel = this._modePanel;
        for (const box of BOXES)
            panel[box] = panel[box].filter(role => !HIDDEN_ROLES.has(role));
    }

    _applyDateMenuPosition() {
        const choice = this._settings.get_string('date-menu-position');
        if (choice === 'stock') return;
        const panel = this._modePanel;
        for (const box of BOXES)
            panel[box] = panel[box].filter(role => role !== DATE_MENU);
        if (choice === 'left-first') panel.left = [DATE_MENU, ...panel.left];
        else if (choice === 'center-first') panel.center = [DATE_MENU, ...panel.center];
        else if (choice === 'right-first') panel.right = [DATE_MENU, ...panel.right];
    }

    // ----- disable panel window drag -----
    _disablePanelWindowDrag(active) {
        const panel = Main.panel;
        if (active) {
            if (this._origFindDraggable) return;
            this._origFindDraggable = panel._getDraggableWindowForPosition;
            panel._getDraggableWindowForPosition = () => null;
        } else if (this._origFindDraggable) {
            panel._getDraggableWindowForPosition = this._origFindDraggable;
            this._origFindDraggable = null;
        }
    }

    // ----- indicator order -----
    _schedule() {
        if (this._timeoutId) return;
        this._timeoutId = this._scope.timeout(DELAY_MS, () => {
            this._timeoutId = 0;
            this._sync();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _sync() {
        if (!this._settings) return;
        const area = Main.panel.statusArea;
        const roleOf = new Map(
            Object.entries(area)
                .filter(([role]) => !HIDDEN_ROLES.has(role))
                .map(([role, ind]) => [ind?.container, role]));

        for (const type of BOXES) {
            const box = Main.panel[`_${type}Box`];

            let index = 0;
            for (const role of this._settings.get_strv(`order-${type}`)) {
                const actor = area[role]?.container;
                if (actor?.get_parent() === box)
                    box.set_child_at_index(actor, index++);
            }

            const roles = box.get_children().map(c => roleOf.get(c)).filter(Boolean);
            const key = `discovered-${type}`;
            if (roles.join('\n') !== this._settings.get_strv(key).join('\n'))
                this._settings.set_strv(key, roles);
        }
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/scheduling.js =====
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';

/**
 * Owns the signals and GLib sources created by one imperative object.
 */
export class Scope {
    constructor() {
        this._signals = [];
        this._sources = [];
        this._disposed = false;
    }

    get disposed() { return this._disposed; }

    connect(obj, signal, cb) {
        if (this._disposed) return 0;
        const id = obj.connect(signal, cb);
        this._signals.push([obj, id]);
        return id;
    }

    timeout(ms, cb, priority = GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT) {
        if (this._disposed) return 0;
        let id = 0;
        id = GLib.timeout_add(priority, ms, () => {
            const r = cb();
            if (r !== GLib.SOURCE_CONTINUE) this._forgetSource(id);
            return r ?? GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
        this._sources.push(id);
        return id;
    }

    idle(cb, priority = GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT) {
        if (this._disposed) return 0;
        let id = 0;
        id = GLib.idle_add(priority, () => {
            const r = cb();
            if (r !== GLib.SOURCE_CONTINUE) this._forgetSource(id);
            return r ?? GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
        this._sources.push(id);
        return id;
    }

    cancel(id) {
        if (!id) return;
        this._forgetSource(id);
        try { GLib.Source.remove(id); } catch (e) { /* already gone */ }
    }

    disconnectSignals() {
        for (const [obj, id] of this._signals) {
            try { obj.disconnect(id); } catch (e) { /* obj may be gone */ }
        }
        this._signals = [];
    }

    _forgetSource(id) {
        const i = this._sources.indexOf(id);
        if (i !== -1) this._sources.splice(i, 1);
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this._disposed) return;
        this._disposed = true;
        for (const [obj, id] of this._signals) {
            try { obj.disconnect(id); } catch (e) { /* obj may be gone */ }
        }
        for (const id of this._sources) {
            try { GLib.Source.remove(id); } catch (e) { /* already gone */ }
        }
        this._signals = [];
        this._sources = [];
    }
}

export class Coalescer {
    constructor(fn) {
        this._fn = fn;
        this._id = 0;
        this._delay = 0;
    }

    queue(delay = 0) {
        if (this._id) {
            if (delay >= this._delay) return;
            GLib.Source.remove(this._id);
        }
        this._delay = delay;
        const run = () => { this._id = 0; this._fn(); return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE; };
        this._id = delay
            ? GLib.timeout_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT, delay, run)
            : GLib.idle_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT, run);
    }

    cancel() {
        if (this._id) { GLib.Source.remove(this._id); this._id = 0; }
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/selectedWindows.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/selectedWindows.js =====
import * as Model from './model.js';
import * as Motion from './motion.js';
import { WindowBorder } from './windowBorder.js';
import { setFocusBorderSuppressor, refreshFocusedWindowBorder } from './focusedWindowBorder.js';

import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

const Display = global.get_display();
const WindowManager = global.get_window_manager();
const WorkspaceManager = global.get_workspace_manager();

const state = {
    windowData: new Map(),
    activeWorkspaceId: 0,
    minimizeId: 0,
    unminimizeId: 0,
    restackedId: 0,
};

function _ensure(actor) {
    if (state.windowData.has(actor)) return state.windowData.get(actor);
    const win = actor.get_meta_window();
    if (!win) return null;

    const info = {
        win,
        selected: false,
        border: null,
        signals: {
            workspace: win.connect('workspace-changed', () => _refreshVisibility(actor)),
            unmanaging: win.connect('unmanaging', () => _onActorUnmanaging(actor)),
        },
    };
    state.windowData.set(actor, info);
    return info;
}

function _detach(actor) {
    const info = state.windowData.get(actor);
    if (!info) return;
    for (const id of Object.values(info.signals)) {
        if (!id) continue;
        try { info.win.disconnect(id); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
    }
}

function _addBorder(actor) {
    const info = state.windowData.get(actor);
    if (!info) return;
    info.border ??= new WindowBorder('selected-border', { fade: 'selectBorder' });
    info.border.show(info.win);
}

function _removeBorder(actor) {
    state.windowData.get(actor)?.border?.hide();
}

function _refreshVisibility(actor) {
    const info = state.windowData.get(actor);
    if (!info || !info.selected) { _removeBorder(actor); return; }
    const win = info.win;
    if (win.minimized) { _removeBorder(actor); return; }
    if (win.get_workspace() !== WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace()) {
        _removeBorder(actor);
        return;
    }
    _addBorder(actor);
}

function _toggle(actor) {
    if (!actor) return;
    const info = _ensure(actor);
    if (!info) return;
    info.selected = !info.selected;

    refreshFocusedWindowBorder();

    if (info.selected) {
        _refreshVisibility(actor);
        Motion.pop(actor, { from: 0.96 });
    } else {
        _removeBorder(actor);
        info.border?.destroy();
        info.border = null;
        Motion.animate(actor, { scale_x: 1, scale_y: 1 }, { duration: 'selectPopOut' });
    }

    // Notify panel + any open overlay that the pinned set changed.
    Model.requestRender();
}

function _isSelected(actor) { return !!state.windowData.get(actor)?.selected; }

function _onActorUnmanaging(actor) {
    _detach(actor);
    _removeBorder(actor);
    const info = state.windowData.get(actor);
    if (info?.border) { info.border.destroy(); info.border = null; }
    state.windowData.delete(actor);
    Model.requestRender();
}

function _onActiveWorkspaceChanged() {
    for (const actor of state.windowData.keys()) _refreshVisibility(actor);
}

function _onMinimize(wm, actor) { if (state.windowData.has(actor)) _refreshVisibility(actor); }
function _onUnminimize(wm, actor) { if (state.windowData.has(actor)) _refreshVisibility(actor); }
function _onRestacked() {
    for (const info of state.windowData.values()) info.border?.restack();
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PUBLIC API
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export function isWindowSelected(win) {
    if (!win) return false;
    const actor = win.get_compositor_private();
    if (!actor) return false;
    return _isSelected(actor);
}

export function hasAnySelected() {
    for (const info of state.windowData.values())
        if (info.selected) return true;
    return false;
}

export function selectedCount() {
    let n = 0;
    for (const info of state.windowData.values())
        if (info.selected) n++;
    return n;
}

export function toggleWindow(win) {
    if (!win) return;
    const actor = win.get_compositor_private();
    if (!actor) return;
    _toggle(actor);
}

export function toggleSelectedWindow() {
    const win = Model.focusedWindow();
    if (!win || !Model.isNormalWindow(win)) return;
    toggleWindow(win);
}

export function closeNotSelectedWindows() {
    const focusWin = Model.focusedWindow();
    if (!focusWin) return;
    const wmClass = focusWin.get_wm_class();
    if (!wmClass) return;

    const workspace = WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace();
    const victims = Model.getModelWindows(workspace).filter(w => {
        if (w === focusWin) return false;
        if (!Model.isNormalWindow(w)) return false;
        if (w.get_wm_class() !== wmClass) return false;
        if (w.is_on_all_workspaces()) return false;
        return true;
    });

    let closed = 0;
    for (const w of victims) {
        if (w.get_wm_class_instance() === 'file_progress') continue;
        if (isWindowSelected(w)) continue;
        w.delete(0);
        closed++;
    }

    for (const actor of [...state.windowData.keys()])
        if (_isSelected(actor)) _toggle(actor);

    journal(`[selectedWindows] closed ${closed} unselected ws-class siblings`);
}

export function initSelectedWindows() {
    setFocusBorderSuppressor(isWindowSelected);
    state.activeWorkspaceId = WorkspaceManager.connect('active-workspace-changed', _onActiveWorkspaceChanged);
    state.minimizeId = WindowManager.connect('minimize', _onMinimize);
    state.unminimizeId = WindowManager.connect('unminimize', _onUnminimize);
    state.restackedId = Display.connect('restacked', _onRestacked);
}

export function destroySelectedWindows() {
    setFocusBorderSuppressor(null);
    if (state.activeWorkspaceId) { WorkspaceManager.disconnect(state.activeWorkspaceId); state.activeWorkspaceId = 0; }
    if (state.minimizeId) { WindowManager.disconnect(state.minimizeId); state.minimizeId = 0; }
    if (state.unminimizeId) { WindowManager.disconnect(state.unminimizeId); state.unminimizeId = 0; }
    if (state.restackedId) { Display.disconnect(state.restackedId); state.restackedId = 0; }

    for (const actor of [...state.windowData.keys()]) {
        _detach(actor);
        const info = state.windowData.get(actor);
        if (info?.border) { info.border.destroy(); info.border = null; }
    }
    state.windowData.clear();
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/strip.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/strip.js =====
// The prototype's createStrip(): a fixed number of visible slots, a view
// position that scrolls by whole rows, edge hold zones with acceleration,
// and a rail with ticks + marker.
//
// ROW_SLOT is sized so the enlarged flyout rows fit without clipping. All
// metrics that vary with BASE come from sizes.js; per-app tick colors come
// from theme.js.
//
// Two scroll paths coexist:
//   • setEdge() — pointer parked in the top/bottom band; continuous,
//     accelerating; used during a drag because DND grabs the wheel.
//   • scrollBy() — discrete notches from a wheel event on the flyout;
//     used only when no drag owns the pointer. Both drive _view through
//     the same layout() so the visual result is identical.
//
// Rows render at full opacity.

import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import Pango from 'gi://Pango';
import St from 'gi://St';

import * as Scheduling from './scheduling.js';
import * as Theme from './theme.js';
import { S } from './sizes.js';
import { Smoother } from './smoother.js';

const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));

export const VISIBLE_SLOTS = 8;
const RAIL_PAD = 16;
const DEAD = 0.06;

const TICK_W = 18;
const TICK_H = 5;

const speedWord = t => (t < 0.25 ? 'SLOW' : t < 0.6 ? 'MEDIUM' : 'FAST');
const depthToT = d => Math.max(0, (d - DEAD) / (1 - DEAD));
const edgeSpeed = d => {
    const t = depthToT(d);
    return t <= 0 ? 0 : 0.8 + 30 * t * t;
};

export class PagedStrip {
    constructor({ rowsEl, upEl, downEl, rail, makeRow, onEdgeStart = null, alive = null }) {
        this._rowsEl = rowsEl;
        this._upEl = upEl;
        this._downEl = downEl;
        this._rail = rail;
        this._makeRow = makeRow;
        this._onEdgeStart = onEdgeStart;
        this._alive = alive ?? (() => true);

        this._upLabel = new St.Label({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-edge-label' });
        this._downLabel = new St.Label({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-edge-label' });
        upEl.add_child(this._upLabel);
        downEl.add_child(this._downLabel);

        this._items = [];
        this._rowEls = new Map();
        this._view = 0;
        this._highlight = -1;
        this._slots = 1;
        this._half = 0;
        this._maxSlots = null;
        this._hostH = 0;
        this._edgeDir = 0;
        this._edgeDepth = 0;
        this._raf = 0;
        this._last = 0;
        this._refitId = 0;
        this._zoneText = { up: '', down: '' };
        this._zoneAlpha = { up: -1, down: -1 };

        this._scope = new Scheduling.Scope();
        this._smooth = new Smoother(this._scope, {
            onFrame: v => {
                if (!this._alive()) { this._smooth.stop(); return; }
                this._view = v;
                const r = Math.round(v);
                if (r !== this._highlight) this._highlight = r;
                this.layout();
            },
        });
    }

    get count() { return this._items.length; }
    get highlighted() { return this._highlight; }
    get selectedIndex() { return Math.max(0, this._highlight); }
    scrolling() { return this._edgeDir !== 0 || this._smooth.running; }

    setViewport(height) { this._hostH = height; }
    setMaxSlots(n) { this._maxSlots = (n != null) ? Math.max(1, n) : null; }

    setItems(items, startIndex = 0) {
        this.stopEdge();
        this._items = items;
        this._slots = Math.min(VISIBLE_SLOTS, this._maxSlots ?? VISIBLE_SLOTS,
            Math.max(1, items.length));
        this._half = (this._slots - 1) / 2;
        this._rowsEl.set_height(this._slots * S.flyout.rowSlot);

        for (const [, el] of this._rowEls) el.destroy();
        this._rowEls.clear();

        this._view = this._clampV(Math.max(0, startIndex));
        this._highlight = Math.round(this._view);

        this._buildRail();
        this.layout();

        if (this._refitId) { GLib.Source.remove(this._refitId); this._refitId = 0; }
        this._refitId = GLib.timeout_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT, 80, () => {
            this._refitId = 0;
            if (this._alive() && this._items.length) this._refitEdges();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _isStrip() { return this._items.length > this._slots; }
    _minV() { return this._isStrip() ? this._half : (this._items.length - 1) / 2; }
    _maxV() { return this._isStrip() ? this._items.length - 1 - this._half : (this._items.length - 1) / 2; }
    _clampV(v) { return clamp(v, this._minV(), this._maxV()); }

    _railY(p) {
        const h = this._rail.root.height;
        if (this._items.length <= 1) return h / 2;
        const span = Math.max(1, h - RAIL_PAD * 2);
        return RAIL_PAD + (p / (this._items.length - 1)) * span;
    }

    _buildRail() {
        this._rail.ticks.destroy_all_children();
        this._rail.root.visible = this._isStrip();
        if (!this._isStrip()) return;

        const w = this._rail.root.width;
        this._items.forEach((it, i) => {
            const tick = new St.Widget({
                style_class: 'ws-flyout-rail-tick',
                x: Math.round((w - TICK_W) / 2),
                y: Math.round(this._railY(i)) - Math.round(TICK_H / 2),
                width: TICK_W, height: TICK_H,
            });
            tick.set_style(`background-color: ${it.color || Theme.C.muted};`);
            this._rail.ticks.add_child(tick);
        });
        this._updateMarker();
    }

    _updateMarker() {
        const marker = this._rail.marker;
        if (!marker) return;
        if (!this._isStrip()) { marker.visible = false; return; }
        marker.visible = true;

        const p = clamp(this._view, 0, Math.max(0, this._items.length - 1));
        const lo = Math.floor(p);
        const hi = Math.min(this._items.length - 1, lo + 1);
        const y = this._railY(lo) + (this._railY(hi) - this._railY(lo)) * (p - lo);
        marker.y = Math.round(y - marker.height / 2);
    }

    _fitEdgeLabel(label, band) {
        label.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.NONE);
        label.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(false);
        label.clutter_text.set_line_alignment(Pango.Alignment.CENTER);

        const [, natW] = label.get_preferred_width(-1);
        const w = natW > 0 ? Math.ceil(natW) : Math.max(1, Math.ceil(band.width));
        const [, natH] = label.get_preferred_height(w);
        const h = natH > 0 ? Math.ceil(natH) : Math.round(band.height * 0.6);

        label.set_size(w, h);
        label.set_position(
            Math.max(0, Math.round((band.width - w) / 2)),
            Math.max(0, Math.round((band.height - h) / 2)));
    }

    _refitEdges() {
        if (this._upEl.visible) this._fitEdgeLabel(this._upLabel, this._upEl);
        if (this._downEl.visible) this._fitEdgeLabel(this._downLabel, this._downEl);
    }

    layout() {
        const R = this._items.length;
        const vis = this._slots;
        const lo = this._isStrip() ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(this._view - this._half - 1)) : 0;
        const hi = this._isStrip() ? Math.min(R - 1, Math.ceil(this._view + this._half + 1)) : R - 1;

        for (const [r, el] of [...this._rowEls]) {
            if (r < lo || r > hi) { el.destroy(); this._rowEls.delete(r); }
        }

        for (let r = lo; r <= hi; r++) {
            let el = this._rowEls.get(r);
            if (!el) {
                el = this._makeRow(this._items[r], r);
                this._rowEls.set(r, el);
                this._rowsEl.add_child(el);
            }
            const d = r - this._view;
            el.translation_y = Math.round((d + (vis - 1) / 2) * S.flyout.rowSlot);

            const sel = r === this._highlight;
            if (sel) el.add_style_class_name('sel');
            else el.remove_style_class_name('sel');

            el.opacity = 255;
            el.show();
        }

        this._updateEdges();
        this._updateMarker();
    }

    setHighlight(i) {
        const idx = clamp(i, -1, Math.max(-1, this._items.length - 1));
        if (idx === this._highlight) return;
        this._highlight = idx;
        this.layout();
    }

    center(i) {
        this._view = this._clampV(i);
        this.layout();
    }

    _updateEdges() {
        const now = this._view;
        const t = depthToT(this._edgeDepth);
        const above = this._isStrip() ? Math.max(0, Math.round(now - this._half)) : 0;
        const below = this._isStrip()
            ? Math.max(0, Math.round(this._items.length - 1 - this._half - now)) : 0;

        const apply = (which, el, label, count, dir, arrow, word) => {
            const active = this._edgeDir === dir;
            el.visible = count > 0;
            if (!el.visible) return;
            if (active) el.add_style_class_name('active');
            else el.remove_style_class_name('active');

            const text = `${arrow} ${count} more ${word}${active && t > 0 ? ' · ' + speedWord(t) : ''}`;
            if (this._zoneText[which] !== text) {
                this._zoneText[which] = text;
                label.set_text(text);
                this._fitEdgeLabel(label, el);
            }
            const alpha = active ? Math.round(clamp(t * 0.42, 0, 1) * 100) / 100 : 0;
            if (this._zoneAlpha[which] !== alpha) {
                this._zoneAlpha[which] = alpha;
                el.set_style(`background-color: ${Theme.rgba('accent', alpha)};`);
            }
        };

        apply('up', this._upEl, this._upLabel, above, -1, '▲', 'above');
        apply('down', this._downEl, this._downLabel, below, 1, '▼', 'below');
    }

    setEdge(dir, depth) {
        this._smooth.stop();

        this._edgeDepth = depth;
        if (this._edgeDir !== dir) {
            this._edgeDir = dir;
            this._onEdgeStart?.();
        }
        if (!this._raf) {
            this._last = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
            this._raf = GLib.timeout_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT, 16, () => this._step());
        }
        this._updateEdges();
    }

    stopEdge() {
        if (this._raf) { GLib.Source.remove(this._raf); this._raf = 0; }
        if (!this._edgeDir) return;
        this._edgeDir = 0;
        this._edgeDepth = 0;
        this._updateEdges();
        this._updateMarker();
    }

    _step() {
        if (!this._edgeDir) { this._raf = 0; return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE; }
        if (!this._alive()) {
            this._raf = 0;
            this._edgeDir = 0;
            this._edgeDepth = 0;
            this._updateEdges();
            this._updateMarker();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        }

        const now = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
        const dt = Math.min(now - this._last, 60) / 1000;
        this._last = now;

        this._view = this._clampV(this._view + this._edgeDir * edgeSpeed(this._edgeDepth) * dt);
        const r = Math.round(this._view);
        if (r !== this._highlight) this._highlight = r;
        this.layout();

        if (this._view <= this._minV() || this._view >= this._maxV()) {
            this._raf = 0;
            this._edgeDir = 0;
            this._edgeDepth = 0;
            this._updateEdges();
            this._updateMarker();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        }
        return GLib.SOURCE_CONTINUE;
    }

    scrollBy(delta) {
        if (!this._isStrip() || !delta) return;
        this.stopEdge();
        if (!this._smooth.running) this._smooth.value = this._view;
        const base = this._smooth.running ? this._smooth.target : this._view;
        this._smooth.to(this._clampV(base + delta));
    }

    over(y) {
        if (this._smooth.running) return this._highlight;

        const rowsTop = this._rowsEl.y;
        const rowsH = this._rowsEl.height;

        if (this._isStrip()) {
            if (y < rowsTop && this._view > this._minV() + 0.001) {
                this.setEdge(-1, clamp((rowsTop - y) / Math.max(1, rowsTop), 0, 1));
                return this._highlight;
            }
            if (y > rowsTop + rowsH && this._view < this._maxV() - 0.001) {
                const below = Math.max(1, this._hostH - rowsTop - rowsH);
                this.setEdge(1, clamp((y - rowsTop - rowsH) / below, 0, 1));
                return this._highlight;
            }
        }
        this.stopEdge();

        if (y < rowsTop - 2 || y > rowsTop + rowsH + 2) return -1;

        const idx = Math.round((y - rowsTop) / S.flyout.rowSlot - (this._slots - 1) / 2 + this._view);
        if (idx < 0 || idx >= this._items.length) return -1;
        this.setHighlight(idx);
        return idx;
    }

    clearHover() { this.setHighlight(-1); }

    destroy() {
        if (this._refitId) { GLib.Source.remove(this._refitId); this._refitId = 0; }
        this._smooth.stop();
        this.stopEdge();
        for (const [, el] of this._rowEls) el.destroy();
        this._rowEls.clear();
        this._items = [];
        this._scope.dispose();
    }
}

import Clutter from 'gi://Clutter';
import Gio from 'gi://Gio';
import Pango from 'gi://Pango';
import Shell from 'gi://Shell';
import St from 'gi://St';

import { S, REL } from './sizes.js';
import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';

const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

export { appColor as colorForApp } from './theme.js';

// ==================== focus pill ====================
function createFocusPill(size) {
    const pillH = Math.max(4, Math.round(size * 0.10));
    const pillW = Math.max(16, Math.round(size * 0.92));
    const pill = new St.Widget({
        style_class: 'ws-focus-pill',
        reactive: false, visible: false,
        width: pillW, height: pillH,
    });
    pill.set_style(`border-radius: ${Math.round(pillH / 2)}px;`);
    pill.set_position(Math.round((size - pillW) / 2), size - pillH);
    return pill;
}

export function setFocusState(actor, { focused = false, pick = false }) {
    const hadFocus = actor.has_style_class_name?.('focused') ?? false;
    const hadPick = actor.has_style_class_name?.('pick') ?? false;

    const toggle = (target, cls, on) => on
        ? target.add_style_class_name(cls)
        : target.remove_style_class_name(cls);

    toggle(actor, 'focused', focused);
    toggle(actor, 'pick', pick);

    const on = focused || pick;
    const pill = actor._focusPill;
    if (pill) {
        pill.visible = on;
        toggle(pill, 'pick', pick);
    }

    if (hadFocus !== focused || hadPick !== pick) {
        let cls = '';
        try { cls = actor.get_style_class_name?.() ?? ''; } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
        journal(`[DEBUG-UI] setFocusState ${actor.constructor?.name} -> ` +
            `classes="${cls}" pill=${!!pill && pill.visible}`);
    }
}

// ==================== app / icon resolution ====================
function resolveApp(window) {
    return Shell.WindowTracker.get_default().get_window_app(window)
        || Shell.AppSystem.get_default().lookup_app(window.get_wm_class());
}

export function getWindowApp(window) { return resolveApp(window); }

export function getWindowAppIcon(window) {
    const app = resolveApp(window);
    if (app?.get_app_info()?.get_icon()) return app.get_icon();
    return window.get_gicon() || new Gio.ThemedIcon({ name: 'applications-system-symbolic' });
}

function createWindowIconTexture(window, iconSize) {
    const app = resolveApp(window);
    if (app?.get_app_info()?.get_icon()) return app.create_icon_texture(iconSize);
    return St.TextureCache.get_default().load_gicon(null, getWindowAppIcon(window), iconSize);
}

export function createDragActor(gicon, size) {
    const bin = new St.Bin({ style_class: 'reorder-drag-source', width: size, height: size });
    bin.opacity = 178;
    if (gicon) bin.set_child(new St.Icon({ gicon, icon_size: size }));
    return bin;
}

export function createToolbarButton(iconName, size, accessibleName) {
    return new St.Button({
        style_class: 'workspace-toolbar-button',
        accessible_name: accessibleName,
        can_focus: true, track_hover: true,
        child: new St.Icon({ icon_name: iconName, icon_size: size }),
    });
}

export function createWorkspaceNameLabel(text, fontSizePx) {
    const label = new St.Label({
        style_class: 'workspace-name-label',
        y_expand: true, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER, text,
    });
    label.set_style(`font-size: ${fontSizePx}px;`);
    return label;
}

export function createThumbnail({ minWidth, active }) {
    const actor = new St.Button({
        style_class: 'workspace-thumbnail',
        x_expand: false, y_expand: true,
        reactive: true, track_hover: true,
    });
    actor.set_style(`min-width: ${minWidth}px;`);
    if (active) actor.add_style_class_name('active');
    const box = new St.BoxLayout({ y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER });
    actor.set_child(box);
    return { actor, box };
}

export function createBadgedIcon({ gicon, size, count }) {
    const badgeSize = Math.max(S.badge.min, Math.round(size * REL.badgeOfIcon));
    const wrap = new St.Widget({
        style_class: 'ws-ico-wrap',
        width: size, height: size,
    });

    const icon = new St.Icon({ gicon, icon_size: size });
    wrap.add_child(icon);
    icon.set_position(0, 0);

    if (count > 1) {
        const label = new St.Label({
            style_class: 'ws-badge-label',
            text: String(count),
            x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        label.set_style(`font-size: ${Math.max(S.badge.labelMin, Math.round(badgeSize * REL.badgeLabelOfBadge))}px;`);

        const badge = new St.Bin({
            style_class: 'ws-badge',
            width: badgeSize, height: badgeSize,
            x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            child: label,
        });
        badge.set_style(`border-radius: ${Math.round(badgeSize / 2)}px;`);
        wrap.add_child(badge);
        badge.set_position(size - badgeSize, 0);
    }

    const pill = createFocusPill(size);
    wrap.add_child(pill);
    wrap._focusPill = pill;

    return { actor: wrap, icon };
}

export function createGroupButton({ gicon, count, size }) {
    const { actor: inner, icon } = createBadgedIcon({ gicon, size, count });
    const button = new St.Button({
        style_class: 'ws-app-group',
        reactive: true, track_hover: true, can_focus: true,
        child: inner,
    });
    button._focusPill = inner._focusPill;
    return { actor: button, icon };
}

export function createWindowIconActor(window, iconSize) {
    const actor = new St.Button({
        style_class: 'window-preview-icon',
        reactive: true, track_hover: true,
    });
    const wrap = new St.Widget({
        style_class: 'ws-ico-wrap',
        width: iconSize, height: iconSize,
    });

    const icon = createWindowIconTexture(window, iconSize);
    wrap.add_child(icon);
    icon.set_position(0, 0);

    const pill = createFocusPill(iconSize);
    wrap.add_child(pill);

    actor.set_child(wrap);
    actor._focusPill = pill;
    return { actor, icon };
}

export function createFlyoutRow({
    gicon, title, sub, badge = '',
    width, height, iconSize, labelSize,
}) {
    const row = new St.BoxLayout({
        style_class: 'ws-flyout-row',
        width, height,
        y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        reactive: true, track_hover: true,
    });

    row.add_child(new St.Icon({
        gicon, icon_size: iconSize,
        style_class: 'ws-flyout-row-icon',
        y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        style: `margin-right: ${S.flyout.iconGap}px;`,
    }));

    const text = new St.BoxLayout({
        vertical: true, x_expand: true,
        y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
    });

    const titleLabel = new St.Label({
        style_class: 'ws-flyout-row-title',
        x_expand: true, x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.START,
        text: title,
    });
    titleLabel.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.START);
    titleLabel.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(false);
    titleLabel.set_style(`font-size: ${labelSize}px;`);
    text.add_child(titleLabel);

    if (sub) {
        const subLabel = new St.Label({
            style_class: 'ws-flyout-row-app',
            x_expand: true, x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.START,
            text: sub,
        });
        subLabel.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.NONE);
        subLabel.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(false);
        subLabel.set_style(`font-size: ${S.flyout.rowSub}px;`);
        text.add_child(subLabel);
    }

    row.add_child(text);

    if (badge) {
        const badgeLabel = new St.Label({
            style_class: 'ws-flyout-row-badge',
            text: badge,
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        badgeLabel.set_style(`font-size: ${S.flyout.rowBadge}px;`);
        row.add_child(badgeLabel);
    }

    return row;
}

export function createCancelZone(text) {
    const label = new St.Label({
        style_class: 'ws-flyout-cancel-label', text,
        x_expand: true, y_expand: true,
        x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
    });
    label.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.NONE);
    label.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(false);
    const actor = new St.Bin({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-cancel', child: label });
    return { actor, label };
}

export function createRail() {
    const root = new St.Widget({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-rail', reactive: false });

    const TRACK_W = 4;
    const MARKER_W = 40;
    const MARKER_H = 12;

    const track = new St.Widget({ style_class: 'ws-flyout-rail-track' });
    root.add_child(track);

    const ticks = new St.Widget({ reactive: false });
    root.add_child(ticks);

    const marker = new St.Widget({
        style_class: 'ws-flyout-rail-marker',
        width: MARKER_W, height: MARKER_H,
    });
    root.add_child(marker);

    root.connect('notify::allocation', () => {
        const alloc = root.get_allocation_box();
        const w = Math.round(alloc.x2 - alloc.x1);
        const h = Math.round(alloc.y2 - alloc.y1);
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
        track.set_position(Math.round((w - TRACK_W) / 2), 0);
        track.set_size(TRACK_W, h);
        marker.x = Math.round((w - MARKER_W) / 2);
    });

    return { root, track, ticks, marker };
}

const PREVIEW = { TITLE_MIN: 40, TITLE_MAX: 100, TITLE_FRACTION: 0.2 };

export function createClonePreview(window, targetHeight, options = {}) {
    if (!window) return null;
    const windowActor = window.get_compositor_private();
    if (!windowActor) return null;

    const frame = window.get_frame_rect();
    const buffer = window.get_buffer_rect();
    if (frame.height === 0) return null;

    const width = targetHeight * (frame.width / frame.height);
    const scale = targetHeight / frame.height;
    const left = (frame.x - buffer.x) * scale;
    const top = (frame.y - buffer.y) * scale;
    const right = ((buffer.x + buffer.width) - (frame.x + frame.width)) * scale;
    const bottom = ((buffer.y + buffer.height) - (frame.y + frame.height)) * scale;

    const inner = new St.BoxLayout({
        style_class: 'collection-preview-inner',
        width, height: targetHeight, clip_to_allocation: true,
    });

    const clone = new Clutter.Clone({
        source: windowActor,
        width: width + left + right,
        height: targetHeight + top + bottom,
    });
    clone.set_position(-left, -top);

    const overlay = new Clutter.Actor();
    overlay.add_child(clone);
    inner.add_child(overlay);

    if (options.showTitle !== false) {
        const raw = window.get_title();
        const text = raw && raw.trim() ? raw : 'Untitled';
        const titleHeight = Math.min(PREVIEW.TITLE_MAX,
            Math.max(PREVIEW.TITLE_MIN, targetHeight * PREVIEW.TITLE_FRACTION));
        const title = new St.Label({
            style_class: 'clone-title-overlay', text,
            x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.FILL, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        title.clutter_text.set_x_align(Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER);
        title.clutter_text.set_y_align(Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER);
        title.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(true);
        title.set_size(width, titleHeight);
        title.set_position(0, (targetHeight - titleHeight) / 2);
        overlay.add_child(title);
    }

    let closeButton = null;
    if (options.closeButtonSize) {
        const size = options.closeButtonSize;
        closeButton = new St.Button({
            style_class: 'window-close-button',
            child: new St.Icon({ icon_name: 'window-close-symbolic', icon_size: size }),
            x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.END, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.START,
            reactive: true,
        });
        closeButton.set_position(width - (options.closeButtonOffsetX ?? (size + 14)),
            options.closeButtonOffsetY ?? 10);
        overlay.add_child(closeButton);
    }

    if (!options.wrapperStyleClass)
        return { actor: inner, inner, overlay, closeButton, width, height: targetHeight };

    const wrapper = new St.BoxLayout({
        style_class: options.wrapperStyleClass,
        reactive: true, track_hover: true,
    });
    wrapper.add_child(inner);
    return { actor: wrapper, wrapper, inner, overlay, closeButton, width, height: targetHeight };
}

export function computeInsertion(rects, p) {
    if (rects.length === 0) return { index: 0, neighbor: null, insertBefore: true };

    const first = rects[0];
    const last = rects[rects.length - 1];
    let target = last, before = false;

    if (p < first.start) { target = first; before = true; }
    else if (p < last.end) {
        for (let i = 0; i < rects.length; i++) {
            const r = rects[i], next = rects[i + 1];
            if (p >= r.start && p < r.end) { target = r; before = p < r.mid; break; }
            if (next && p >= r.end && p <= next.start) {
                before = (p - r.end) > (next.start - p);
