                target = before ? next : r;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    const i = rects.indexOf(target);
    return { index: before ? i : i + 1, neighbor: target.item, insertBefore: before };
}

function escapeMarkup(s) {
    return s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
}

export function highlightMarkup(text, indices) {
    if (!indices || indices.size === 0) return escapeMarkup(text);
    let out = '', open = false, i = 0;
    for (const ch of text) {
        const hit = indices.has(i);
        if (hit && !open) { out += '<b>'; open = true; }
        else if (!hit && open) { out += '</b>'; open = false; }
        out += escapeMarkup(ch);
        i += ch.length;
    }
    return open ? out + '</b>' : out;
}

const spacer = () => new St.Widget({ x_expand: true });

export function createFullscreenContainer(monitor, styleClass) {
    return new St.Widget({
        style_class: styleClass, reactive: true, can_focus: true, clip_to_allocation: true,
        x: monitor.x, y: monitor.y, width: monitor.width, height: monitor.height,
    });
}

export function createSearchField({ width, hint, keyHint = null, x = 0, y = 0 }) {
    const actor = new St.BoxLayout({ style_class: 'overlay-search', vertical: true, width, x, y });
    const entry = new St.Entry({
        style_class: 'overlay-search-entry', hint_text: hint, can_focus: true, x_expand: true,
        primary_icon: new St.Icon({ icon_name: 'edit-find-symbolic', style_class: 'overlay-search-icon' }),
    });
    entry.clutter_text.set_line_alignment(Pango.Alignment.CENTER);
    actor.add_child(entry);
    if (keyHint) {
        const hint = new St.Label({
            text: keyHint, style_class: 'overlay-search-hint',
            x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        hint.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.NONE);
        hint.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(false);
        actor.add_child(hint);
    }
    return { actor, entry };
}

export function createDividerHeading(text) {
    const actor = new St.BoxLayout({ style_class: 'launcher-divider-heading', x_expand: true });
    const line = () => new St.Widget({ style_class: 'launcher-divider', x_expand: true, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER });
    const label = new St.Label({ text, style_class: 'launcher-heading', y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER });
    label.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.NONE);
    label.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(false);
    actor.add_child(line());
    actor.add_child(label);
    actor.add_child(line());
    return { actor, label };
}

export function createPinnedRow() {
    const wrap = new St.Widget({
        style_class: 'launcher-pinned-wrap',
        x_expand: true, y_expand: true,
    });

    const scroll = new St.ScrollView({
        style_class: 'launcher-pinned-scroll',
        overlay_scrollbars: false,
    });
    scroll.set_policy(St.PolicyType.EXTERNAL, St.PolicyType.NEVER);
    const row = new St.BoxLayout({ style_class: 'launcher-pinned-row', x_expand: true });
    scroll.set_child(row);

    const FADE_W = 96;
    const leftFade = new St.Widget({
        style_class: 'launcher-pinned-fade-left',
        reactive: false, visible: false,
    });
    const rightFade = new St.Widget({
        style_class: 'launcher-pinned-fade-right',
        reactive: false, visible: false,
    });

    wrap.add_child(scroll);
    wrap.add_child(leftFade);
    wrap.add_child(rightFade);

    wrap.connect('notify::allocation', () => {
        const alloc = wrap.get_allocation_box();
        const w = alloc.x2 - alloc.x1;
        const h = alloc.y2 - alloc.y1;
        scroll.set_position(0, 0);
        scroll.set_size(w, h);
        leftFade.set_position(0, 0);
        leftFade.set_size(FADE_W, h);
        rightFade.set_position(w - FADE_W, 0);
        rightFade.set_size(FADE_W, h);
    });

    return { actor: wrap, scroll, box: row, leftFade, rightFade };
}

export function createGridScroll() {
    const scroll = new St.ScrollView({
        style_class: 'launcher-scroll', x_expand: true, y_expand: true, overlay_scrollbars: false,
    });
    scroll.set_policy(St.PolicyType.NEVER, St.PolicyType.AUTOMATIC);
    const box = new St.BoxLayout({ style_class: 'launcher-grid', vertical: true, x_expand: true });
    scroll.set_child(box);
    return { scroll, box };
}

export function createAppTile({ icon, name, indices = null, iconSize, width, height, showLabel = true }) {
    const button = new St.Button({
        style_class: 'launcher-tile', reactive: true, track_hover: true,
        accessible_name: name, width, height,
    });
    const box = new St.BoxLayout({ vertical: true, x_expand: true, y_expand: true });
    box.add_child(new St.Icon({
        gicon: icon ?? new Gio.ThemedIcon({ name: 'application-x-executable' }),
        icon_size: iconSize, x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
    }));
    if (showLabel) {
        const label = new St.Label({ style_class: 'launcher-tile-label', x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER });
        const text = label.clutter_text;
        text.set_line_wrap(true);
        text.set_line_wrap_mode(Pango.WrapMode.WORD_CHAR);
        text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.END);
        text.set_line_alignment(Pango.Alignment.CENTER);
        text.set_markup(highlightMarkup(name, indices));
        label.set_width(width - 24);
        box.add_child(label);
    }
    button.set_child(box);
    return button;
}

// ── Window selector primitives ──────────────────────────────
export function createWindowTitleRow({
    icon, title, indices = null,
    width, height, iconSize, labelSize,
    maxWidth = width * 0.60, selected = false,
}) {
    const row = new St.BoxLayout({
        style_class: selected ? 'selector-row selector-row-selected' : 'selector-row',
        width, height,
    });

    const inner = new St.BoxLayout({
        style_class: 'selector-row-inner', y_expand: true, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
    });

    const gap = Math.round((labelSize ?? 16) * 0.42);
    const labelMax = Math.max(80, Math.round(maxWidth) - (iconSize || 0) - gap);

    if (icon) {
        const iconActor = new St.Icon({
            gicon: icon, icon_size: iconSize,
            style_class: 'selector-row-icon',
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        iconActor.set_style(`margin-right: ${gap}px;`);
        inner.add_child(iconActor);
    }

    const label = new St.Label({
        style_class: selected ? 'selector-selected-label' : 'selector-row-label',
        y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
    });
    if (labelSize != null) label.set_style(`font-size: ${labelSize}px;`);
    label.set_x_align(Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER);
    label.set_width(labelMax);
    label.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.START);
    label.clutter_text.set_markup(highlightMarkup(title, indices));
    inner.add_child(label);

    row.add_child(spacer());
    row.add_child(inner);
    row.add_child(spacer());
    return row;
}

export function createBandTitle({
    icon, title,
    width, height, iconSize, labelSize,
}) {
    const row = new St.BoxLayout({
        style_class: 'selector-band-title',
        width, height, x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
    });

    const inner = new St.BoxLayout({
        style_class: 'selector-band-inner', y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
    });

    if (icon) {
        inner.add_child(new St.Icon({
            gicon: icon, icon_size: iconSize,
            style_class: 'selector-band-icon', y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        }));
    }

    const gap = 18;
    const textMax = Math.max(100, Math.round(width * 0.85) - (iconSize || 0) - gap);

    const titleLabel = new St.Label({
        style_class: 'selector-band-title-label',
        y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
    });
    titleLabel.set_x_align(Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER);
    titleLabel.set_width(textMax);
    titleLabel.set_style(`font-size: ${labelSize}px;`);
    titleLabel.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.START);
    titleLabel.set_text(title);
    inner.add_child(titleLabel);

    row.add_child(spacer());
    row.add_child(inner);
    row.add_child(spacer());
    return row;
}

export function createSelectionBand(bandL) {
    const band = new St.BoxLayout({
        style_class: 'selector-band', vertical: true,
        x: bandL.x, y: bandL.y, width: bandL.width, height: bandL.height,
    });
    const titleBin = new St.Bin({ x_expand: true, height: bandL.selectedHeight });
    band.add_child(titleBin);
    return { band, titleBin };
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/windowOverlay.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/windowOverlay.js =====
import Clutter from 'gi://Clutter';
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import Meta from 'gi://Meta';
import Pango from 'gi://Pango';
import Shell from 'gi://Shell';
import St from 'gi://St';

import * as Main from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/main.js';

import * as Fuzzy from './fuzzy.js';
import * as Model from './model.js';
import * as UI from './ui.js';
import * as WorkspaceActions from './workspaceActions.js';
import * as Motion from './motion.js';
import * as Interactions from './interactions.js';
import * as SelectedWindows from './selectedWindows.js';
import { Smoother } from './smoother.js';
import { S } from './sizes.js';

import { SearchOverlayBase, clamp, ease } from './overlayBase.js';

import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

// ==================== DISCRETE FOCUS STRIP ====================
function neighborOpacity(x) {
    if (x <= 1) return 1.00;
    if (x <= 2) return 1.00 - 0.15 * (x - 1);   // 1.0 -> 0.85, never dimmer while visible
    return Math.max(0, 0.85 * (1 - (x - 2) / 0.8));
}

const appColor = name => UI.colorForApp(name);

const MAX_ROWS_EACH_SIDE = 2;
const rowsEachSideFor = n => Math.min(MAX_ROWS_EACH_SIDE, Math.max(0, n - 1));

function usableArea(monitor) {
    const pb = Main.layoutManager.panelBox;
    let { x, y, width, height } = monitor;
    if (pb && pb.height > 0) {
        const atBottom = pb.y + pb.height / 2 > monitor.y + monitor.height / 2;
        height -= pb.height;
        if (!atBottom) y += pb.height;
    }
    return { x, y, width, height };
}

// ==================== LAYOUT ====================
function computeOverlayLayout(monitor) {
    return usableArea(monitor);
}

function computeRailLayout(overlay) {
    const width = 48;
    const margin = 14;
    const pad = 10;
    const height = Math.round(overlay.height * 0.80);
    return {
        x: overlay.width - margin - width,
        y: Math.round((overlay.height - height) / 2),
        width, height, margin, pad,
        gutter: margin + width + margin,
    };
}

function computeBandLayout(overlay, rail) {
    const selectedHeight = S.selector.bandH;
    const searchHeight = S.selector.searchH;
    const filterHeight = S.selector.filterH;
    // padding 48 + border 6 + two 16px gaps (32) + 4 slack
    const height = selectedHeight + searchHeight + filterHeight + 90;
    const width = Math.max(400, overlay.width - rail.gutter * 2);
    return {
        x: Math.round((overlay.width - width) / 2),
        y: Math.round((overlay.height - height) / 2),
        width, height,
        selectedHeight, searchHeight, filterHeight,
        searchWidth: Math.min(1200, Math.round(width * 0.55)),
    };
}

function computeRowLayout(overlay, band) {
    return {
        rowLabelSize: S.selector.rowLabel,
        rowIconSize: S.selector.rowIcon,
        slotH: S.selector.rowSlot,
        rowTextMax: Math.round(band.width * 0.85),
        tipWidth: Math.min(700, Math.round(overlay.width * 0.45)),
    };
}

// ==================== window selector ====================
export class WindowSearchOverlay extends SearchOverlayBase {
    constructor(windows, settings, focusedWindow = null, initialFilters = null) {
        super(settings);
        this._focusedWindow = focusedWindow ?? Model.Display.focus_window;

        this._items = windows.map(w => {
            const app = UI.getWindowApp(w);
            return {
                window: w,
                title: w.get_title() || 'Untitled Window',
                appName: app?.get_name() || w.get_wm_class() || 'Unknown',
                appId: app?.get_id() || null,
                icon: UI.getWindowAppIcon(w),
            };
        });

        // Read filters from intent (session persistence). `initialFilters`
        // lets the DBus/caller override on first open (e.g. workspace filter).
        const persisted = Model.intent.overlayFilters ?? {};
        this._filters = {
            workspace: initialFilters?.workspace ?? persisted.workspace ?? null,
            app: initialFilters?.app ?? persisted.app ?? null,
            pinnedOnly: initialFilters?.pinnedOnly ?? persisted.pinnedOnly ?? false,
        };
        this._persistFilters();

        this._rows = [];
        this._scrollAccum = 0;
        this._scrollPos = 0;
        this._lastScrollStep = 0;
        this._snap = true;

        this._origin = {
            window: Model.Display.focus_window,
            workspace: Model.WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace(),
        };
        this._revealed = null;
        this._revealedAny = false;
        this._revealTimer = 0;
        this._guardTimer = 0;
        this._cancelled = false;
        this._focusRelease = null;

        this._overlayL = null;
        this._railL = null;
        this._bandL = null;
        this._rowL = null;

        this._fadeTop = null;
        this._fadeBottom = null;

        this._rail = null;
        this._railTicksBox = null;
        this._railTicks = [];
        this._railMarker = null;
        this._railTip = null;
        this._railGrab = null;
        this._railDragging = false;
        this._railH = 0;
        this._railPad = 0;

        this._filterBar = null;
        this._countLabel = null;
        this._openChipMenu = null;
        this._chipL = null;

        this._buildUI();
        this._initialKey = this._focusedWindow ?? null;

        this._smooth = new Smoother(this._scope, {
            onFrame: v => { this._scrollPos = v; this._layoutRows(); },
        });

        this._onSearchChanged();
        this._focusRelease = Model.holdFocusHistory();
        this._open();
    }

    _trackFullscreen() { return false; }
    _getRestoreKey(item) { return item.window; }

    _shouldCloseOnWindowRemoved(window) {
        return this._items.some(item => item.window === window);
    }

    _persistFilters() {
        Model.setIntent({ overlayFilters: { ...this._filters } });
    }

    _hasActiveFilter() {
        return this._filters.workspace !== null
            || this._filters.app !== null
            || this._filters.pinnedOnly
            || (this._entry?.get_text?.().length ?? 0) > 0;
    }

    // ── filtering ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    _applyFilters(items) {
        const f = this._filters;
        let list = items;

        if (f.workspace !== null) {
            list = list.filter(item => {
                try { return item.window.get_workspace()?.index() === f.workspace; }
                catch (e) { return false; }
            });
        }
        if (f.app !== null) {
            list = list.filter(item => item.appId === f.app);
        }
        if (f.pinnedOnly) {
            list = list.filter(item => SelectedWindows.isWindowSelected(item.window));
        }
        return list;
    }

    _matchItem(query, item) {
        const r = Fuzzy.fuzzyMatch(query, `${item.title} ${item.appName}`);
        if (!r.matched) return r;
        return { ...r, indices: new Set([...r.indices].filter(i => i < item.title.length)) };
    }

    _computeResults(query) {
        const filtered = this._applyFilters(this._items);

        if (query) {
            const scored = [];
            for (const item of filtered) {
                const r = this._matchItem(query, item);
                if (r.matched) scored.push({ ...item, score: r.score, indices: r.indices });
            }
            return scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
        }

        // No query: panel order (first window of first workspace -> last of last).
        const order = new Map();
        Model.getModelWindows(null).forEach((w, i) => { if (!order.has(w)) order.set(w, i); });
        return filtered
            .map(item => ({ ...item, indices: null }))
            .sort((a, b) => (order.get(a.window) ?? Infinity) - (order.get(b.window) ?? Infinity));
    }

    _getAppName(window) {
        const app = Shell.WindowTracker.get_default().get_window_app(window);
        return app ? app.get_name() : (window.get_wm_class() || 'Unknown');
    }

    // ── UI construction ───────────────────────────────────────
    _buildUI() {
        const monitor = Main.layoutManager.primaryMonitor;
        this._monitor = monitor;
        this._overlayL = computeOverlayLayout(monitor);
        this._railL = computeRailLayout(this._overlayL);
        this._bandL = computeBandLayout(this._overlayL, this._railL);
        this._rowL = computeRowLayout(this._overlayL, this._bandL);

        const OV = this._overlayL;
        const BAND = this._bandL;
        const ROW = this._rowL;

        this._container = UI.createFullscreenContainer(this._overlayL, 'window-selector-overlay');
        this._container.add_style_class_name('search-mode');

        const size = { width: OV.width, height: OV.height };
        this._tint = new St.Widget({ style_class: 'selector-tint', reactive: false, opacity: 255, ...size });
        this._listLayer = new St.Widget(size);

        this._fadeTop = new St.Widget({ style_class: 'selector-fade-top', reactive: false });
        this._fadeBottom = new St.Widget({ style_class: 'selector-fade-bottom', reactive: false });
        this._resizeBackdrop(this._items.length);

        const { band, titleBin } = UI.createSelectionBand(BAND);
        this._bandBin = titleBin;
        this._band = band;

        // CONTROLS BOX — invisible; search entry and chip row share one width.
        const controls = new St.BoxLayout({
            vertical: true, width: BAND.searchWidth, style: 'spacing: 16px;',
        });

        const search = UI.createSearchField({ width: BAND.searchWidth, hint: 'Type to search…' });
        this._wireEntry(search.entry);
        search.entry.set_style(`font-size: ${S.selector.search}px;`);
        if (search.entry.primary_icon) search.entry.primary_icon.visible = false;
        this._searchRoot = search.actor;
        controls.add_child(search.actor);

        this._filterBar = new St.BoxLayout({
            style_class: 'selector-filter-bar',
            width: BAND.searchWidth, height: BAND.filterHeight,
        });
        controls.add_child(this._filterBar);

        band.add_child(new St.Bin({
            x_expand: true, x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER, child: controls,
        }));

        // Four equal chip slots on the left, count on the right.
        const gap = S.selector.filterRowGap;
        const countW = Math.round(BAND.searchWidth * 0.18);
        this._chipL = {
            gap, countW,
            slotW: Math.floor((BAND.searchWidth - countW - gap * 4) / 4),
            chipH: S.selector.filterChipH,
        };
        this._refreshFilterBar();

        for (const actor of [this._tint, this._fadeTop, this._fadeBottom, this._listLayer, band])
            this._container.add_child(actor);

        this._buildRail();

        this._scope.connect(this._container, 'scroll-event', (a, e) => this._onScroll(e));
        this._scope.connect(this._container, 'button-release-event', (a, e) => this._onClick(e));

        this._scope.connect(global.stage, 'notify::key-focus', () => {
            if (this._closed || this._closing) return;
            if (global.stage.get_key_focus() === this._entry.clutter_text) return;
            this._refocusEntry();
        });
    }

    _resizeBackdrop(n) {
        if (!this._fadeTop) return;
        const BAND = this._bandL, ROW = this._rowL;
        const rows = rowsEachSideFor(n);
        const h = rows * ROW.slotH;

        this._fadeTop.visible = this._fadeBottom.visible = rows > 0;
        if (!rows) return;

        this._fadeTop.set_position(BAND.x, BAND.y - h);
        this._fadeTop.set_size(BAND.width, h);
        this._fadeBottom.set_position(BAND.x, BAND.y + BAND.height);
        this._fadeBottom.set_size(BAND.width, h);
    }

    // ── filter chips ──────────────────────────────────────────
    _noEllipsis(lbl) {
        lbl.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.NONE);
        lbl.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(false);
        return lbl;
    }

    // No "…": if text would overflow, shrink the font to fit instead.
    _fitSize(text, size, maxW, floor = 0.6) {
        const est = [...text].length * size * 0.58;
        if (est <= maxW) return size;
        return Math.max(Math.round(size * floor), Math.floor(size * maxW / est));
    }

    _refreshFilterBar() {
        if (!this._filterBar || !this._chipL) return;
        this._filterBar.destroy_all_children();
        this._openChipMenu = null;

        const { gap, countW } = this._chipL;

        const chipBox = new St.BoxLayout({
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            style: `spacing: ${gap}px;`,
        });
        chipBox.add_child(this._makeResetChip());
        chipBox.add_child(this._makeWorkspaceChip());
        chipBox.add_child(this._makeAppChip());
        chipBox.add_child(this._makePinnedChip());
        this._filterBar.add_child(chipBox);

        this._filterBar.add_child(new St.Widget({ x_expand: true }));

        this._countLabel = this._noEllipsis(new St.Label({
            style_class: 'selector-filter-count',
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            width: countW,
        }));
        this._countLabel.clutter_text.set_line_alignment(Pango.Alignment.RIGHT);
        this._filterBar.add_child(this._countLabel);

        this._updateCountLabel();
    }

    _updateCountLabel() {
        if (!this._countLabel || !this._chipL) return;
        const n = this._results.length;
        const text = `${n} window${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
        this._countLabel.set_text(text);
        this._countLabel.set_style(
            `font-size: ${this._fitSize(text, S.selector.filterCountText, this._chipL.countW)}px;`);
    }

    // One generic chip: small uppercase label over a larger value.
    _makeChip({ cls, label = null, value, active = false, greyed = false,
        onClick, onMenu = null, onScroll = null }) {
        const { slotW, chipH } = this._chipL;

        const btn = new St.Button({
            style_class: `selector-filter-chip ${cls}`,
            reactive: true, track_hover: true, clip_to_allocation: true,
            y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            width: slotW, height: chipH,
        });
        if (active) btn.add_style_class_name('active');
        if (greyed) btn.add_style_class_name('greyed');

        const col = new St.BoxLayout({
            style_class: 'selector-filter-chip-box', vertical: true,
            x_expand: true, y_expand: true, y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });

        if (label) {
            const l = new St.Label({
                text: label.toUpperCase(),
                style_class: 'selector-filter-label',
                x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            });
            l.set_style(`font-size: ${S.selector.filterLabelText}px;`);
            col.add_child(this._noEllipsis(l));
        }

        const v = new St.Label({
            text: value,
            style_class: 'selector-filter-value',
            x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
        });
        v.set_style(`font-size: ${this._fitSize(value, S.selector.filterText, slotW - 40)}px;`);
        col.add_child(this._noEllipsis(v));

        btn.set_child(col);

        const scope = this._rowsScope;
        scope.connect(btn, 'clicked', onClick);
        if (onMenu) {
            scope.connect(btn, 'button-press-event', (a, e) => {
                if (e.get_button() === Clutter.BUTTON_SECONDARY) {
                    onMenu(btn);
                    return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
                }
                return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            });
        }
        if (onScroll) {
            scope.connect(btn, 'scroll-event', (a, e) => {
                const d = this._scrollDir(e);
                if (!d) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
                onScroll(d);
                return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
            });
        }
        return btn;
    }

    _makeResetChip() {
        return this._makeChip({
            cls: 'selector-filter-reset',
            value: '↺ Reset',
            greyed: !this._hasActiveFilter(),
            onClick: () => { if (this._hasActiveFilter()) this._resetFilters(); },
        });
    }

    _makeWorkspaceChip() {
        const ws = this._filters.workspace;
        return this._makeChip({
            cls: 'selector-filter-workspace',
            label: 'Workspace',
            value: ws === null ? 'None' : `${ws + 1} · ${this._workspaceNameForChip(ws)}`,
            active: ws !== null,
            onClick: () => this._cycleWorkspace(1),
            onMenu: btn => this._showWorkspaceMenu(btn),
            onScroll: d => this._cycleWorkspace(d),
        });
    }

    _makeAppChip() {
        let value = 'None';
        if (this._filters.app !== null) {
            const item = this._items.find(i => i.appId === this._filters.app);
            value = item ? item.appName : 'None';
        }
        return this._makeChip({
            cls: 'selector-filter-app',
            label: 'App',
            value,
            active: this._filters.app !== null,
            onClick: () => this._cycleApp(1),
            onMenu: btn => this._showAppMenu(btn),
            onScroll: d => this._cycleApp(d),
        });
    }

    _makePinnedChip() {
        const count = SelectedWindows.selectedCount();
        const on = this._filters.pinnedOnly;
        const chip = this._makeChip({
            cls: 'selector-filter-pinned',
            label: 'Pinned',
            value: on ? `Only · ${count}` : (count === 0 ? 'None' : String(count)),
            greyed: count === 0 && !on,
            onClick: () => {
                if (this._filters.pinnedOnly) {
                    this._filters.pinnedOnly = false;
                } else {
                    // Pinned activation resets other filters (per design)
                    this._filters.workspace = null;
                    this._filters.app = null;
                    this._filters.pinnedOnly = true;
                }
                this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
            },
        });
        if (on) chip.add_style_class_name('pinned-mode');
        return chip;
    }

    // ── cycling ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    _scrollDir(event) {
        const dir = event.get_scroll_direction();
        if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.UP) return -1;
        if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.DOWN) return 1;
        if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.SMOOTH) {
            const [, dy] = event.get_scroll_delta();
            return Math.sign(dy);
        }
        return 0;
    }

    _cycleWorkspace(dir) {
        const n = Model.WorkspaceManager.n_workspaces;
        const base = [null];
        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) base.push(i);
        const i = base.indexOf(this._filters.workspace);
        this._filters.workspace = base[(i + dir + base.length) % base.length];
        this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
    }

    _cycleApp(dir) {
        const apps = this._filterableApps();
        if (apps.length === 0) {
            this._filters.app = null;
            this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
            return;
        }
        const values = [null, ...apps.map(a => a.id)];
        const i = values.indexOf(this._filters.app);
        this._filters.app = values[(i + dir + values.length) % values.length];
        this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
    }

    _resetFilters() {
        this._filters.workspace = null;
        this._filters.app = null;
        this._filters.pinnedOnly = false;
        if (this._entry) this._entry.set_text('');
        this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
    }

    _applyFiltersAndRefresh() {
        this._persistFilters();
        this._initialKey = null;
        this._onSearchChanged();
    }

    // ── menus (via existing openContextMenu) ─────────────────
    _workspaceNameForChip(index) {
        try { return Meta.prefs_get_workspace_name(index) || `Workspace ${index + 1}`; }
        catch (e) { return `Workspace ${index + 1}`; }
    }

    _showWorkspaceMenu(anchor) {
        const n = Model.WorkspaceManager.n_workspaces;
        const current = this._filters.workspace;
        Interactions.openContextMenu(anchor, menu => {
            menu.addAction('None', () => {
                this._filters.workspace = null;
                this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
            });
            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                const marker = i === current ? ' ●' : '';
                menu.addAction(`${i + 1} · ${this._workspaceNameForChip(i)}${marker}`, () => {
                    this._filters.workspace = i;
                    this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
                });
            }
        }, { onStateChange: (open) => { if (!open) this._openChipMenu = null; } });
        this._openChipMenu = 'workspace';
    }

    _showAppMenu(anchor) {
        const apps = this._filterableApps();
        const current = this._filters.app;
        Interactions.openContextMenu(anchor, menu => {
            menu.addAction('None', () => {
                this._filters.app = null;
                this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
            });
            for (const a of apps) {
                const marker = a.id === current ? ' ●' : '';
                menu.addAction(`${a.name} (${a.count})${marker}`, () => {
                    this._filters.app = a.id;
                    this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
                });
            }
        }, { onStateChange: (open) => { if (!open) this._openChipMenu = null; } });
        this._openChipMenu = 'app';
    }

    _filterableApps() {
        const counts = new Map();
        for (const item of this._items) {
            if (!item.appId) continue;
            counts.set(item.appId, (counts.get(item.appId) || 0) + 1);
        }
        const out = [];
        for (const [id, count] of counts) {
            if (count < 2) continue;
            const item = this._items.find(i => i.appId === id);
            out.push({ id, name: item?.appName || id, count });
        }
        out.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
        return out;
    }

    // ── rail widget tree ─────────────────────────────────────
    _buildRail() {
        const RL = this._railL;
        const ROW = this._rowL;

        this._railH = RL.height;
        this._railPad = RL.pad;

        this._rail = new St.Widget({
            style_class: 'selector-rail',
            reactive: true, track_hover: true,
            x: RL.x, y: RL.y,
            width: RL.width, height: RL.height,
        });

        const track = new St.Widget({
            style_class: 'selector-rail-track',
            x: Math.round((RL.width - 2) / 2),
            y: 0, width: 2, height: RL.height,
        });
        this._rail.add_child(track);

        this._railTicksBox = new St.Widget({ x: 0, y: 0, width: RL.width, height: RL.height });
        this._rail.add_child(this._railTicksBox);

        this._railMarker = new St.Widget({
            style_class: 'selector-rail-marker',
            x: Math.round((RL.width - S.selector.railMarkerW) / 2),
            y: 0, width: S.selector.railMarkerW, height: S.selector.railMarkerH,
        });
        this._rail.add_child(this._railMarker);

        const tipFontSize = S.selector.tip;
        this._railTip = new St.Label({
            style_class: 'selector-rail-tip', text: '',
            x: 0, y: 0, width: ROW.tipWidth, opacity: 0,
        });
        this._railTip.set_style(`font-size: ${tipFontSize}px; font-weight: 600;`);
        this._railTip.clutter_text.set_ellipsize(Pango.EllipsizeMode.START);
        this._railTip.clutter_text.set_line_wrap(false);

        this._container.add_child(this._rail);
        this._container.add_child(this._railTip);

        this._wireRail();
    }

    _wireRail() {
        const rail = this._rail;
        const scope = this._scope;

        scope.connect(rail, 'button-press-event', (a, e) => {
            if (e.get_button() !== Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            this._railDragging = true;
            this._acquireRailGrab();
            this._railPointerFromEvent(e);
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        });

        scope.connect(rail, 'motion-event', (a, e) => {
            if (this._railDragging) {
                this._railPointerFromEvent(e);
                return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
            }
            const [, y] = e.get_coords();
            const [, ry] = rail.get_transformed_position();
            const idx = this._railIndexFromY(y - ry);
            const item = this._results[idx];
            if (item) this._showRailTip(idx, item);
            return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
        });

        scope.connect(rail, 'button-release-event', (a, e) => {
            if (!this._railDragging) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            this._railDragging = false;
            this._releaseRailGrab();
            this._hideRailTip();
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        });

        scope.connect(rail, 'leave-event', () => {
            if (!this._railDragging) this._hideRailTip();
            return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
        });
    }

    _acquireRailGrab() {
        try {
            const grab = global.stage.grab(this._rail);
            if (grab) { this._railGrab = { dismiss: () => grab.dismiss() }; return; }
        } catch (e) {
            journal(`[rail] stage.grab failed: ${e.message}`);
        }
        try {
            const modal = Main.pushModal(this._rail, { actionMode: Shell.ActionMode.NORMAL });
            this._railGrab = { modal };
        } catch (e) {
            journal(`[rail] pushModal fallback failed: ${e.message}`);
            this._railGrab = null;
            this._railDragging = false;
        }
    }

    _releaseRailGrab() {
        const g = this._railGrab;
        this._railGrab = null;
        if (!g) return;
        if (g.modal) { try { Main.popModal(g.modal); } catch (e) { /* already popped */ } }
        else { try { g.dismiss(); } catch (e) { /* already dismissed */ } }
    }

    _railY(i) {
        const n = this._results.length;
        const span = Math.max(1, this._railH - this._railPad * 2);
        if (n <= 1) return this._railH / 2;
        return this._railPad + (i / (n - 1)) * span;
    }

    _railIndexFromY(py) {
        const n = this._results.length;
        if (n <= 1) return 0;
        const span = Math.max(1, this._railH - this._railPad * 2);
        return clamp(Math.round(((py - this._railPad) / span) * (n - 1)), 0, n - 1);
    }

    _buildRailTicks() {
        if (!this._railTicksBox || !this._rail) return;
        this._railTicksBox.destroy_all_children();
        this._railTicks = [];

        const n = this._results.length;
        this._rail.visible = n > 0;
        if (n === 0) return;

        const W = this._railL.width;
        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            const item = this._results[i];
            const tick = new St.Widget({
                style_class: 'selector-rail-tick',
                x: Math.round((W - 12) / 2),
                y: Math.round(this._railY(i) - 2),
                width: 12, height: 4,
            });
            tick.set_style(`background-color: ${appColor(item.appName || '')};`);
            this._railTicksBox.add_child(tick);
            this._railTicks.push(tick);
        }
        this._updateRailMarker();
    }

    _updateRailMarker() {
        if (!this._railMarker || !this._results.length) return;
        const p = clamp(this._scrollPos, 0, this._results.length - 1);
        const lo = Math.floor(p);
        const hi = Math.min(this._results.length - 1, lo + 1);
        const y = this._railY(lo) + (this._railY(hi) - this._railY(lo)) * (p - lo);
        this._railMarker.set_position(this._railMarker.x, Math.round(y - this._railMarker.height / 2));
    }

    _railPointerFromEvent(event) {
        const [, y] = event.get_coords();
        const [, ry] = this._rail.get_transformed_position();
        const idx = this._railIndexFromY(y - ry);
        const item = this._results[idx];
        if (!item) return;
        this._selectIndex(idx);
        this._showRailTip(idx, item);
    }

    _showRailTip(idx, item) {
        if (!this._railTip) return;
        const RL = this._railL, OV = this._overlayL, BAND = this._bandL;
        const text = `${item.appName} — ${item.title}`;
        this._railTip.set_text(text);

        const h = this._railTip.height > 0 ? this._railTip.height : 58;
        const tipX = Math.max(8, RL.x - this._railTip.width - 8);
        let tipY = RL.y + this._railY(idx) - Math.round(h / 2);

        if (tipY + h > BAND.y && tipY < BAND.y + BAND.height) {
            const above = BAND.y - h - 8;
            tipY = above >= 8 ? above : Math.min(BAND.y + BAND.height + 8, OV.height - h - 8);
        }
        tipY = clamp(tipY, 8, OV.height - h - 8);

        this._railTip.set_position(tipX, tipY);
        this._railTip.opacity = 255;
    }

    _hideRailTip() {
        if (this._railTip) this._railTip.opacity = 0;
    }

    // ── rows ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _renderResults(results) {
        const OV = this._overlayL, ROW = this._rowL;

        this._releaseRailGrab();
        this._railDragging = false;
        this._hideRailTip();

        this._resizeBackdrop(results.length);
        this._updateCountLabel();
        this._refreshFilterBar();  // update chip states (pinned count, etc.)

        this._listLayer.destroy_all_children();
        this._rows = results.map((item, index) => {
            const row = UI.createWindowTitleRow({
                icon: item.icon, title: item.title, indices: item.indices,
                width: OV.width, height: ROW.slotH,
                iconSize: ROW.rowIconSize,
                labelSize: ROW.rowLabelSize,
                maxWidth: ROW.rowTextMax,
            });
            this._rowsScope.connect(row, 'button-press-event', (a, e) => {
                if (e.get_button() !== Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
                const state = e.get_state();
                if (state & Clutter.ModifierType.CONTROL_MASK) {
                    // Toggle pin
                    if (item.window) {
                        SelectedWindows.toggleWindow(item.window);
                        // If pinned-only filter is active and the last pin
                        // disappeared, drop the filter.
                        if (this._filters.pinnedOnly &&
                            SelectedWindows.selectedCount() === 0) {
                            this._filters.pinnedOnly = false;
                        }
                        this._applyFiltersAndRefresh();
                    }
                    return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
                }
                this._selectIndex(index);
                return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
            });
            this._rowsScope.connect(row, 'button-release-event', (a, e) => {
                if (e.get_button() !== Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
                return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
            });
            this._listLayer.add_child(row);
            return row;
        });
        this._snap = true;
        this._buildRailTicks();
    }

    // ── discrete focus strip layout ──────────────────────────
    _layoutRows() {
        const BAND = this._bandL, ROW = this._rowL;
        const pos = this._scrollPos;
        const a = BAND.y - ROW.slotH / 2;
        const b = BAND.y + BAND.height + ROW.slotH / 2;
        const mid = (a + b) / 2;
        const half = (b - a) / 2;

        this._rows.forEach((row, i) => {
            const d = i - pos;
            const x = Math.abs(d);

            if (x >= 2.8) { row.hide(); return; }

            let c, op;
            if (x < 1) {
                c = mid + d * half;
                op = neighborOpacity(1) * ease((x - 0.2) / 0.8);
            } else {
                c = d > 0 ? b + (x - 1) * ROW.slotH : a - (x - 1) * ROW.slotH;
                op = neighborOpacity(x);
            }
            if (op < 0.01) { row.hide(); return; }

            row.show();
            row.set_position(0, Math.round(c - ROW.slotH / 2));
            row.set_opacity(Math.round(op * 255));
            row.set_pivot_point(0.5, 0.5);
            row.set_scale(1, 1);
        });

        this._bandBin.opacity = Math.round(255 * ease(1 - Math.abs(this._selectedIndex - pos)));
        this._updateRailMarker();
    }

    // ── selection ────────────────────────────────────────────
    _onSelectionChanged(index) {
        const BAND = this._bandL;

        if (index < 0) {
            Model.setIntent({ overlayPick: null });
            const label = new St.Label({
                text: this._query ? 'No matching windows' : 'No windows',
                x_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
                y_align: Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER,
            });
            label.set_style(`font-size: ${S.selector.search}px;`);
            this._bandBin.set_child(label);
            this._cancelReveal();
            return;
        }

        const item = this._results[index];
        Model.setIntent({ overlayPick: item.window });

        this._bandBin.set_child(UI.createBandTitle({
            icon: item.icon,
            title: item.title,
            width: BAND.width,
            height: BAND.selectedHeight,
            iconSize: S.selector.bandIcon,
            labelSize: S.selector.center,
        }));

        if (this._snap || Motion.reduced()) {
            this._smooth.snap(index);
            this._snap = false;
        } else {
            this._smooth.to(index);
        }

        this._scheduleReveal(item);
    }

    _onScroll(event) {
        let step = 0;
        const dir = event.get_scroll_direction();
        if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.UP) step = -1;
        else if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.DOWN) step = 1;
        else if (dir === Clutter.ScrollDirection.SMOOTH) {
            const [, dy] = event.get_scroll_delta();
            this._scrollAccum += dy;
            if (Math.abs(this._scrollAccum) >= 1) {
                step = Math.sign(this._scrollAccum);
                this._scrollAccum = 0;
            }
        }
        if (!step) return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;

        const now = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
        if (now - this._lastScrollStep < 50) return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        this._lastScrollStep = now;

        this._selectIndex(clamp(this._selectedIndex + step, 0, this._results.length - 1));
        return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
    }

    _guardFocus(ms = 1200) {
        this._focusGuard = true;
        if (this._guardTimer) this._scope.cancel(this._guardTimer);
        this._guardTimer = this._scope.timeout(ms, () => {
            this._guardTimer = 0;
            this._focusGuard = false;
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _scheduleReveal(item) {
        this._cancelReveal();
        this._revealTimer = this._scope.timeout(120, () => {
            this._revealTimer = 0;
            this._revealWindow(item.window);
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _cancelReveal() {
        if (!this._revealTimer) return;
        this._scope.cancel(this._revealTimer);
        this._revealTimer = 0;
    }

    _revealWindow(win) {
        if (this._closed || this._closing || win === this._revealed) return;
        this._guardFocus();
        this._revealed = win;
        this._revealedAny = true;
        WorkspaceActions.focusWindow(win);
        this._refocusEntry();
    }

    _refocusEntry() {
        this._scope.idle(() => {
            if (this._closed || this._closing) return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
            this._entry.grab_key_focus();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _restoreOrigin() {
        if (!this._revealedAny) return;
        this._revealedAny = false;
        this._guardFocus();
        const { window: win, workspace } = this._origin;
        const time = global.get_current_time();
        try {
            const ws = win?.get_workspace();
            if (ws) ws.activate_with_focus(win, time);
            else workspace?.activate(time);
        } catch (e) { /* window gone */ }
    }

    _handleEscape() {
        if (this._closed || this._closing) return;
        if (this._entry.get_text().length > 0) {
            this._entry.set_text('');
            return;
        }
        this._cancelled = true;
        this._container.add_style_class_name('selector-cancelling');
        this._scope.timeout(Motion.dur('cancelFlash'), () => {
            if (!this._closed) this._close();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _onBeforeClose() {
        this._releaseRailGrab();
        this._railDragging = false;
        this._cancelReveal();
        Model.setIntent({ overlayPick: null });
        if (this._cancelled) this._restoreOrigin();
    }

    _handleExtraKeys(event, symbol) {
        const inEntry = global.stage.get_key_focus() === this._entry.clutter_text;
        const state = event.get_state();

        if (!inEntry && !(state & (Clutter.ModifierType.CONTROL_MASK |
            Clutter.ModifierType.MOD1_MASK | Clutter.ModifierType.SUPER_MASK))) {
            if (symbol === Clutter.KEY_BackSpace && this._entry.get_text().length > 0) {
                this._entry.grab_key_focus();
                this._entry.clutter_text.delete_text(this._entry.get_text().length - 1, -1);
                return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
            }
            const uc = event.get_key_unicode();
            const code = typeof uc === 'string' ? uc.codePointAt(0) : uc;
            if (code && code >= 0x20 && code !== 0x7f) {
                this._entry.grab_key_focus();
                this._entry.clutter_text.insert_unichar(code);
                return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
            }
        }

        if (symbol === Clutter.KEY_Page_Down) {
            this._selectIndex(Math.min(this._selectedIndex + 5, this._results.length - 1));
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        }
        if (symbol === Clutter.KEY_Page_Up) {
            this._selectIndex(Math.max(this._selectedIndex - 5, 0));
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        }
        if (symbol === Clutter.KEY_Home && !inEntry) {
            this._selectIndex(0);
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        }
        if (symbol === Clutter.KEY_End && !inEntry) {
            this._selectIndex(this._results.length - 1);
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        }
        return undefined;
    }

    _onClick(event) {
        if (event.get_button() !== Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY) return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
        if (this._closing) return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;

        // Click inside the filter bar or on an open chip menu: don't activate.
        const src = event.get_source();
        if (this._filterBar && src && this._filterBar.contains(src)) return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        if (this._openChipMenu) { this._openChipMenu = null; return Clutter.EVENT_STOP; }

        if (src && this._searchRoot.contains(src)) {
            this._entry.grab_key_focus();
            return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
        }
        this._activateFocused();
        return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
    }

    _activateResult(index) {
        if (this._closed || this._closing) return;
        const item = this._results[index];
        if (!item) return;
        const window = item.window;

        this._closing = true;
        this._cancelReveal();
        this._focusRelease?.();
        this._focusRelease = null;

        WorkspaceActions.focusWindow(window);

        Motion.fadeOut(this._container, { duration: 'overlayFade' });
        this._scope.timeout(Motion.dur('overlayFade') + 30, () => {
            if (!this._closed) this._close();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _close() {
        if (this._closed) return;
        this._focusRelease?.();
        this._focusRelease = null;
        journal('[WindowSearchOverlay] Closing');
        super._close();
        this._items = [];
        this._rows = [];
        this._revealed = null;
        this._origin = null;
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/model.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/model.js =====
import Meta from 'gi://Meta';
import Shell from 'gi://Shell';
import * as Main from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/main.js';

import { Store, Emitter, Hold } from './util.js';
import { configureMotion } from './motion.js';

import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

export const WorkspaceManager = global.get_workspace_manager();
export const Display = global.get_display();

export const TimeoutDelay = 200;

export function applySettings(settings) {
    if (!settings) return;
    configureMotion({ reduceMotion: settings.get_boolean('reduce-motion') });
}

// ---- intent ----
export const intentStore = new Store({
    drag: null, hintWs: null, menuOpen: false,
    overlayPick: null,
    // Persisted across overlay opens in the same session.
    // Reset on extension reload. See windowOverlay.js for shape.
    overlayFilters: {
        workspace: null,   // null | workspace index
        app: null,         // null | app id
        pinnedOnly: false, // bool
    },
});
export const intent = intentStore.state;
export const world = new Emitter();
export const requestRender = () => world.emit();
export const resetIntent = () => intentStore.reset();
export const setIntent = patch => intentStore.patch(patch);
export const menuIntent = open => setIntent({ menuOpen: open });

// ---- eligibility ----
function isEligible(w) {
    if (!w) return false;
    if (w.skip_taskbar || w.is_override_redirect()) return false;
    const t = w.get_window_type();
    return t === Meta.WindowType.NORMAL
        || t === Meta.WindowType.DIALOG
        || t === Meta.WindowType.MODAL_DIALOG
        || t === Meta.WindowType.UTILITY;
}

export function isNormalWindow(win) {
    return win?.get_window_type() === Meta.WindowType.NORMAL;
}

export function focusedWindow() {
    return isEligible(Display.focus_window) ? Display.focus_window : null;
}

// ---- ordering ----
const ranks = new Map();
let nextRank = 1;

// ---- last-focused-per-workspace ----
const lastFocusedByWs = new Map();

// ---- focus history suppression ----
const focusHold = new Hold();
export function holdFocusHistory() { return focusHold.acquire(); }

export function noteFocusedWindow() {
    if (focusHold.active) return;
    const window = focusedWindow();
    if (!window) return;
    try {
        const ws = window.get_workspace();
        if (ws) lastFocusedByWs.set(ws.index(), window);
    } catch (e) { /* dead window object */ }
}

export function lastFocusedWindow(workspaceIndex) {
    const win = lastFocusedByWs.get(workspaceIndex);
    if (!win) return null;
    try {
        const ws = win.get_workspace();
        if (!ws || ws.index() !== workspaceIndex) {
            lastFocusedByWs.delete(workspaceIndex);
            return null;
        }
    } catch (e) {
        lastFocusedByWs.delete(workspaceIndex);
        return null;
    }
    return win;
}

// ---- app identity / grouping ----
export function appIdentity(window) {
    const app = Shell.WindowTracker.get_default().get_window_app(window);
    if (app) {
        const id = app.get_id();
        if (id) return { id, name: app.get_name() || id };
    }
    const wm = window.get_wm_class() || window.get_wm_class_instance() || 'unknown';
    return { id: `wm:${wm}`, name: wm };
}

export function groupKey(workspaceIndex, appId, anchorId = '') {
    return `${workspaceIndex}::${appId}${anchorId !== '' ? `#${anchorId}` : ''}`;
}

export function groupWindowsInWorkspace(entry) {
    const out = [];
    let cur = null;
    entry.windows.forEach((w, i) => {
        const { id, name } = appIdentity(w);
        if (!cur || cur.appId !== id) {
            cur = {
                appId: id, appName: name,
                key: groupKey(entry.index, id, w.get_id()),
                windows: [], indices: [], count: 0,
            };
            out.push(cur);
        }
        cur.windows.push(w);
        cur.indices.push(i);
        cur.count++;
    });
    return out;
}

// ---- build ----
export function buildModel() {
    const live = Display.list_all_windows().filter(isEligible);

    const liveSet = new Set(live);
    for (const w of [...ranks.keys()])
        if (!liveSet.has(w)) ranks.delete(w);
    for (const w of live)
        if (!ranks.has(w)) ranks.set(w, nextRank++);

    for (const [idx, win] of lastFocusedByWs)
        if (!liveSet.has(win)) lastFocusedByWs.delete(idx);

    const workspaces = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < WorkspaceManager.n_workspaces; i++) {
        const workspace = WorkspaceManager.get_workspace_by_index(i);
        const windows = live
            .filter(w => w.located_on_workspace(workspace))
            .sort((a, b) => ranks.get(a) - ranks.get(b));

        const entry = {
            workspace, index: i, windows,
        };
        entry.groups = groupWindowsInWorkspace(entry);
        workspaces.push(entry);
    }
    return { workspaces, drag: intent.drag, hintWs: intent.hintWs };
}

export function placeWindow(window, workspace, index = null) {
    const fullList = buildModel().workspaces[workspace.index()].windows;
    const list = fullList.filter(w => w !== window);

    let at = index === null ? list.length : index;
    at = Math.max(0, Math.min(at, list.length));

    list.splice(at, 0, window);

    for (const w of list) ranks.set(w, nextRank++);
}

export function placeWindowNear(window, workspace, neighbor = null, before = true) {
    if (neighbor === window) return;
    const list = buildModel().workspaces[workspace.index()].windows
        .filter(w => w !== window);

    let at = list.length;
    if (neighbor) {
        const i = list.indexOf(neighbor);
        if (i >= 0) at = before ? i : i + 1;
    }
    list.splice(at, 0, window);
    for (const w of list) ranks.set(w, nextRank++);
}

// ---- signals ----
let _signals = [];
let _wsSignals = [];
let _settings = null;

function _listen(obj, signal, cb) { _signals.push([obj, obj.connect(signal, cb)]); }

function _watchWorkspaces() {
    for (const [obj, id] of _wsSignals) {
        try { obj.disconnect(id); } catch (e) { /* already gone */ }
    }
    _wsSignals = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < WorkspaceManager.n_workspaces; i++) {
        const ws = WorkspaceManager.get_workspace_by_index(i);
        _wsSignals.push([ws, ws.connect('window-added', () => requestRender())]);
        _wsSignals.push([ws, ws.connect('window-removed', () => requestRender())]);
    }
}

export function connectModelSignals(settings = null) {
    disconnectModelSignals();
    _settings = settings;

    if (settings) applySettings(settings);

    _listen(WorkspaceManager, 'notify::n-workspaces', () => { _watchWorkspaces(); requestRender(); });
    _listen(WorkspaceManager, 'workspace-switched', () => requestRender());
    _listen(Display, 'window-created', () => requestRender());
    _listen(Display, 'notify::focus-window', () => noteFocusedWindow());
    _listen(Main.sessionMode, 'updated', () => requestRender());
    if (settings) {
        _listen(settings, 'changed', () => {
            applySettings(settings);
            requestRender();
        });
    }

    _watchWorkspaces();
    noteFocusedWindow();
}

export function disconnectModelSignals() {
    for (const [obj, id] of _wsSignals) {
        try { obj.disconnect(id); } catch (e) { /* already gone */ }
    }
    for (const [obj, id] of _signals) {
        try { obj.disconnect(id); } catch (e) { /* already gone */ }
    }
    _wsSignals = [];
    _signals = [];
    _settings = null;
}

export function getModelWindows(workspace = null) {
    const model = buildModel();
    if (workspace === null)
        return model.workspaces.flatMap(ws => ws.windows);
    const entry = model.workspaces.find(ws => ws.workspace === workspace);
    return entry ? entry.windows : [];
}

export function getWorkspaceGroups(workspaceIndex) {
    const entry = buildModel().workspaces[workspaceIndex];
    return entry ? entry.groups : [];
}

export function getGroupWindows(workspaceIndex, appId) {
    return getWorkspaceGroups(workspaceIndex).find(g => g.appId === appId) ?? null;
}

export function getModelSnapshot(model) {
    return {
        drag: !!model.drag,
        hintWs: model.hintWs,
        workspaces: model.workspaces.map(ws => ({
            index: ws.index,
            windowCount: ws.windows.length,
            groups: ws.groups.map(g => `${g.appName}:${g.count}`),
        })),
    };
}

export function logModel(model) {
    journal('[Model Snapshot]\n' + JSON.stringify(getModelSnapshot(model), null, 2));
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/overlayService.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/overlayService.js =====
import Meta from 'gi://Meta';

import { SearchOverlayBase } from './overlayBase.js';
import { WindowSearchOverlay } from './windowOverlay.js';
import { AppSearchOverlay } from './appOverlay.js';
import { GenericSearchOverlay } from './genericOverlay.js';
import * as Model from './model.js';

import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

// ==================== OVERLAY SERVICE ====================
// The single place any search overlay is opened or closed.
//
// showWindowOverlay() passes all candidate windows plus an optional
// `initialFilters` object to pre-seed the filter bar. When
// `initialFilters` is null, the overlay uses whatever filters are
// currently persisted in `Model.intent.overlayFilters` — that's the
// "remember the filter across opens" behaviour.
//
// Filters are single-axis values:
//   { workspace: null|0..n, app: null|appId, recent: null|1..7, pinnedOnly: bool }

let _settings = null;

export function showWindowOverlay(windows, context = null, focusedWindow = null, initialFilters = null) {
    if (!_settings) {
        journal('[overlayService] showWindowOverlay before init — ignoring');
        return false;
    }
    SearchOverlayBase.closeCurrent();
    const overlay = new WindowSearchOverlay(windows, _settings, focusedWindow, initialFilters);
    overlay._overlayContext = context;
    return true;
}

export function showAllWindows(initialFilters = null) {
    if (!_settings) {
        journal('[overlayService] showAllWindows before init — ignoring');
        return false;
    }
    const activeIdx = Model.WorkspaceManager.get_active_workspace_index();
    const focusedWindow = Model.Display.focus_window
        ?? Model.lastFocusedWindow(activeIdx)
        ?? null;
    return showWindowOverlay(
        Model.getModelWindows(null),
        { type: 'all-windows' },
        focusedWindow,
        initialFilters);
}

export function showAllApps() {
    if (!_settings) {
        journal('[overlayService] showAllApps before init — ignoring');
        return false;
    }
    SearchOverlayBase.closeCurrent();
    new AppSearchOverlay(_settings);
    return true;
}

export function toggleAllApps() {
    if (SearchOverlayBase.current instanceof AppSearchOverlay) {
        SearchOverlayBase.closeCurrent();
        return false;
    }
    return showAllApps();
}

/**
 * Toggle the all-windows overlay. Press once to open, again to close.
 * The app overlay is reached through ToggleAllApps, not by cycling.
 */
export function toggleAllWindows() {
    const current = SearchOverlayBase.current;
    if (current instanceof WindowSearchOverlay &&
        current._overlayContext?.type === 'all-windows') {
        SearchOverlayBase.closeCurrent();
        return false;
    }
    return showAllWindows();
}

/**
 * Show windows for a given workspace. The overlay opens pre-filtered to
 * that workspace; the user can clear the filter (Reset) to see all windows.
 */
export function showWorkspaceWindows(workspaceIndex) {
    const workspace = Model.WorkspaceManager.get_workspace_by_index(workspaceIndex);
    if (!workspace) throw new Error(`Workspace ${workspaceIndex} not found`);

    const focusedWindow = Model.Display.get_tab_list(Meta.TabList.NORMAL, workspace)[0]
        ?? Model.lastFocusedWindow(workspaceIndex)
        ?? null;

    workspace.activate(global.get_current_time());
    return showWindowOverlay(
        Model.getModelWindows(null),
        { type: 'workspace', workspaceIndex },
        focusedWindow,
        { workspace: workspaceIndex });
}

export function toggleWorkspaceWindows(workspaceIndex) {
    const current = SearchOverlayBase.current;
    if (current instanceof WindowSearchOverlay &&
        current._overlayContext?.type === 'workspace' &&
        current._overlayContext.workspaceIndex === workspaceIndex) {
        SearchOverlayBase.closeCurrent();
        return false;
    }
    return showWorkspaceWindows(workspaceIndex);
}

/**
 * Show windows for a given (workspace, app) group. Pre-filters to
 * workspace, but leaves the app filter off so the user can broaden
 * back to "all windows on this workspace" with one click.
 * (Different from the old behaviour — pass `null` focusedWindow so
 * the group's own representative doesn't get lost if the app has more
 * than one window on this workspace.)
 */
export function showGroupWindows(workspaceIndex, appId) {
    const entry = Model.buildModel().workspaces[workspaceIndex];
    if (!entry) return false;

    const group = entry.groups.find(g => g.appId === appId);
    if (!group || !group.windows.length) return false;

    const initial = [Model.Display.focus_window, Model.lastFocusedWindow(workspaceIndex)]
        .find(w => w && group.windows.includes(w)) ?? group.windows[0];

    const opened = showWindowOverlay(
        Model.getModelWindows(null),
        { type: 'group', workspaceIndex, appId, appName: group.appName },
        initial,
        { workspace: workspaceIndex });
    journal(`[overlayService] showGroupWindows ws${workspaceIndex} ${group.appName} (${group.windows.length})`);
    return opened;
}

export function toggleGroupWindows(workspaceIndex, appId) {
    const current = SearchOverlayBase.current;
    if (current instanceof WindowSearchOverlay &&
        current._overlayContext?.type === 'group' &&
        current._overlayContext.workspaceIndex === workspaceIndex &&
        current._overlayContext.appId === appId) {
        SearchOverlayBase.closeCurrent();
        return false;
    }
    return showGroupWindows(workspaceIndex, appId);
}

export function closeOverlay() { return SearchOverlayBase.closeCurrent(); }

// -------------------- Dmenu-style picker --------------------
// `Show(items)` reports its result through the Selected / Cancelled
// D-Bus signals (see dbusService.js). `onResult` fires exactly once per
// invocation: an array of labels, or `null` on cancellation.
export function showDmenuItems(items, opts = {}, onResult = null) {
    if (!_settings) {
        journal('[overlayService] showDmenuItems before init — ignoring');
        return false;
    }
    SearchOverlayBase.closeCurrent();
    new GenericSearchOverlay(items, _settings, {
        multi: !!opts.multi,
        hint: opts.hint || null,
        controls: opts.controls ?? [],
        onResult,
    });
    return true;
}

// -------------------- Lifecycle --------------------
export function initOverlayService(settings) { _settings = settings; }

export function destroyOverlayService() {
    closeOverlay();
    _settings = null;
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/workspaceActions.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/workspaceActions.js =====
// Window/workspace actions that are neither model state nor overlay logic.
// Shared by the DBus adapter, the top-bar scroll handler, the flyout,
// and every preview/activation path in the indicator and overlays.

import Meta from 'gi://Meta';

import * as Model from './model.js';

import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

/**
 * Bring `win` to the foreground on its own workspace. Maximizes only
 * NORMAL windows — a dialog or utility palette forced full-screen is
 * the wrong outcome. Window eligibility (which windows this extension
 * works with at all) is decided by Model, not here.
 *
 * Exceptions (dead window object, race during close) are swallowed
 * and logged — callers treat this as fire-and-forget.
 *
 * Note: the previous version had an inverted branch here that called
 * activate_with_workspace() with a null workspace whenever
 * get_workspace() returned null. Mutter switches workspace on its own
 * when activate() is called on a window on another workspace, so the
 * plain activate() call is sufficient.
 */
export function focusWindow(win) {
    if (!win) return;
    try {
        if (win.minimized) win.unminimize();
        if (Model.isNormalWindow(win)) win.maximize(Meta.MaximizeFlags.BOTH);
        win.activate(global.get_current_time());
    } catch (e) {
        journal(`[focusWindow] failed: ${e.message}`);
    }
}

export function scrollModelWindows(direction = 1) {
    const model = Model.buildModel();

    const windows = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const entry of model.workspaces) {
        for (const w of entry.windows) {
            const id = w.get_id();
            if (seen.has(id)) continue;
            seen.add(id);
            windows.push(w);
        }
    }
    if (windows.length === 0) return false;

    const n = windows.length;
    const idx = windows.indexOf(Model.Display.focus_window);
    const nextIdx = idx === -1
        ? (direction > 0 ? 0 : n - 1)
        : ((idx + direction) % n + n) % n;
    const target = windows[nextIdx];

    if (target.minimized) target.unminimize();
    const ws = target.get_workspace();
    if (ws) ws.activate_with_focus(target, global.get_current_time());
    else target.activate(global.get_current_time());
    return true;
}

export function cycleWindowsOnWorkspace(workspaceIndex, direction = 1) {
    const entry = Model.buildModel().workspaces[workspaceIndex];
    if (!entry || entry.windows.length === 0) return false;

    const windows = entry.windows;
    const n = windows.length;
    const idx = windows.indexOf(Model.Display.focus_window);

    const nextIdx = idx === -1
        ? (direction > 0 ? 0 : n - 1)
        : ((idx + direction) % n + n) % n;
    const target = windows[nextIdx];

    focusWindow(target);
    return true;
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/util.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/util.js =====
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

export class Emitter {
    constructor() { this._cbs = new Set(); }
    on(cb) { this._cbs.add(cb); return () => this._cbs.delete(cb); }
    emit(...a) {
        for (const cb of [...this._cbs]) {
            try { cb(...a); } catch (e) { journal(`listener failed: ${e.message}`, true); }
        }
    }
    clear() { this._cbs.clear(); }
}

export class Store {
    constructor(initial) {
        this._initial = { ...initial };
        this.state = { ...initial };
        this.changed = new Emitter();
    }
    patch(p) {
        let dirty = false;
        for (const k of Object.keys(p))
            if (this.state[k] !== p[k]) { this.state[k] = p[k]; dirty = true; }
        if (dirty) this.changed.emit(this.state);
        return dirty;
    }
    reset() { Object.assign(this.state, this._initial); }
}

export class Hold {
    constructor() { this._n = 0; }
    get active() { return this._n > 0; }
    acquire() {
        this._n++;
        let done = false;
        return () => { if (done) return; done = true; if (this._n > 0) this._n--; };
    }
}

export class Dwell {
    constructor(scope, ms, { rearmAfterFire = false } = {}) {
        this._scope = scope;
        this._ms = typeof ms === 'function' ? ms : () => ms;
        this._rearm = rearmAfterFire;
        this._id = 0; this._key = null;
    }
    get key() { return this._key; }
    arm(key, fn) {
        if (key === this._key) return false;
        this.cancel();
        this._key = key;
        this._id = this._scope.timeout(this._ms(), () => {
            this._id = 0;
            if (this._rearm) this._key = null;
            fn();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
        return true;
    }
    cancel() {
        if (this._id) { this._scope.cancel(this._id); this._id = 0; }
        this._key = null;
    }
}

export class Poller {
    constructor(scope, { interval, tick, name = 'poll' }) {
        this._scope = scope; this._interval = interval; this._tick = tick;
        this._name = name; this._id = 0;
    }
    get running() { return this._id !== 0; }
    start() {
        if (this._id) return;
        this._id = this._scope.timeout(this._interval, () => {
            let r;
            try { r = this._tick(); }
            catch (e) { journal(`${this._name} tick threw: ${e.message}\n${e.stack}`, true); }
            if (r === false) { this._id = 0; return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE; }
            return GLib.SOURCE_CONTINUE;
        });
    }
    stop() { if (this._id) { this._scope.cancel(this._id); this._id = 0; } }
}

export class Misses {
    constructor(limit, onLimit) { this.limit = limit; this.onLimit = onLimit; this.n = 0; }
    reset() { this.n = 0; }
    miss() { if (++this.n >= this.limit) { this.n = 0; this.onLimit(); return true; } return false; }
}
// ===== FILE: ./lib/fuzzy.js =====
// Fuzzy string matching for the search overlays. Pure: no imports, no state,
// no actors. Both WindowSearchOverlay and AppSearchOverlay run their queries
// through fuzzyMatch. Returns matched character offsets so callers can bold
// them in their markup.
//
// Order of attempts per token: substring > subsequence > typo-tolerant
// word-prefix.

function subsequenceMatch(token, text) {
    const positions = [];
    let qi = 0, score = 0, consecutive = 0;
    for (let ti = 0; ti < text.length && qi < token.length; ti++) {
        if (text[ti] === token[qi]) { score += 1 + consecutive; consecutive++; qi++; positions.push(ti); }
        else consecutive = 0;
    }
    return qi < token.length ? { matched: false } : { matched: true, score, positions };
}

// Optimal-string-alignment distance (insert / delete / substitute / transpose).
function osaDistance(a, b) {
    const d = Array.from({ length: a.length + 1 }, (_, i) => [i, ...new Array(b.length).fill(0)]);
    for (let j = 0; j <= b.length; j++) d[0][j] = j;
    for (let i = 1; i <= a.length; i++) {
        for (let j = 1; j <= b.length; j++) {
            const cost = a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1;
            d[i][j] = Math.min(d[i - 1][j] + 1, d[i][j - 1] + 1, d[i - 1][j - 1] + cost);
            if (i > 1 && j > 1 && a[i - 1] === b[j - 2] && a[i - 2] === b[j - 1])
                d[i][j] = Math.min(d[i][j], d[i - 2][j - 2] + 1);
        }
    }
    return d[a.length][b.length];
}

function typoMatch(token, text) {
    if (token.length < 4) return null;
    const limit = token.length >= 6 ? 2 : 1;
    for (const m of text.matchAll(/[^\s\-_.\/:—]+/g)) {
        const word = m[0];
        for (let len = token.length - 1; len <= token.length + 1; len++) {
            if (len < 1 || len > word.length) continue;
            if (osaDistance(token, word.slice(0, len)) <= limit) {
                const positions = [];
                for (let i = 0; i < word.length; i++) positions.push(m.index + i);
                return { score: 10, positions };
            }
        }
    }
    return null;
}

export function fuzzyMatch(query, text) {
    const tokens = query.trim().toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    const indices = new Set();
    if (tokens.length === 0) return { matched: true, score: 0, indices };

    const lower = text.toLowerCase();
    let score = 0;
    for (const token of tokens) {
        const idx = lower.indexOf(token);
        if (idx !== -1) {
            const boundary = idx === 0 || /[\s\-_.\/]/.test(lower[idx - 1]);
            score += 60 - Math.min(idx, 40) + (boundary ? 20 : 0);
            for (let i = idx; i < idx + token.length; i++) indices.add(i);
            continue;
        }
        const sub = subsequenceMatch(token, lower);
        if (sub.matched) { score += sub.score; sub.positions.forEach(p => indices.add(p)); continue; }
        const typo = typoMatch(token, lower);
        if (!typo) return { matched: false, score: -1, indices: new Set() };
        score += typo.score;
        typo.positions.forEach(p => indices.add(p));
    }
    return { matched: true, score, indices };
}

// ===== FILE: ./lib/windowBorder.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./lib/windowBorder.js =====
import St from 'gi://St';
import * as Motion from './motion.js';

const SIGNALS = ['position-changed', 'size-changed',
    'notify::maximized-horizontally', 'notify::maximized-vertically'];

export class WindowBorder {
    constructor(styleClass, { fade = null } = {}) {
        this._styleClass = styleClass;
        this._fade = fade;
        this._bin = null;
        this._win = null;
        this._actor = null;
        this._ids = [];
    }
    get window() { return this._win; }
    get actor() { return this._actor; }

    show(win) {
        const actor = win?.get_compositor_private();
        const parent = actor?.get_parent();
        if (!parent) { this.hide(); return false; }

        if (this._win !== win || this._actor !== actor || this._bin?.get_parent() !== parent) {
            this.hide();
            this._bin ??= new St.Bin({ style_class: this._styleClass, reactive: false });
            this._win = win;
            this._actor = actor;
            parent.add_child(this._bin);
            this._ids = SIGNALS.map(s => win.connect(s, () => this.reposition()));
            if (this._fade) {
                this._bin.opacity = 0;
                Motion.animate(this._bin, { opacity: 255 }, { duration: this._fade });
            }
        }
        this.reposition();
        return true;
    }

    reposition() {
        if (!this._bin || !this._win || !this._bin.get_parent()) return;
        const r = this._win.get_frame_rect();
        this._bin.set_position(r.x, r.y);
        this._bin.set_size(r.width, r.height);
        this.restack();
    }

    restack() {
        if (!this._bin?.get_parent() || !this._actor) return;
        global.get_window_group().set_child_above_sibling(this._bin, this._actor);
    }

    hide() {
        for (const id of this._ids) { try { this._win?.disconnect(id); } catch (e) { /* gone */ } }
        this._ids = [];
        if (this._bin) {
            this._bin.remove_all_transitions();
            this._bin.get_parent()?.remove_child(this._bin);
        }
        this._win = null;
        this._actor = null;
    }

    destroy() {
        this.hide();
        this._bin?.destroy();
        this._bin = null;
    }
}

import Clutter from 'gi://Clutter';
