// Shell versions store the app on different properties; try each.
const appOf = item => item?._app ?? item?.child?.app ?? item?.child?._app ?? null;

// ==================== EdgeDash ====================
const EdgeDash = GObject.registerClass(
    class EdgeDash extends Dash.Dash {
        _init(monitor, callbacks = {}) {
            super._init();

            journal('[pinnedEdge.dash] _init:enter');
            this._monitor = monitor;
            this._onShowApps = callbacks.onShowApps ?? null;
            this._onActivated = callbacks.onActivated ?? null;
            this._keepDashShown = false;
            this._destroying = false;

            this._reorder = Interactions.createReorderBar({
                axis: 'y',
                isTarget: () => true,
                items: () => this._favoriteItems(),
                sourceItem: s => this._favoriteItems().find(i => appOf(i) === s?.app) ?? null,
            });

            this._forceVertical();

            this.reactive = true;
            this.track_hover = true;
            this._background.reactive = true;
            this._background.set_style('background: none; box-shadow: none; border: none;');

            this.set_pivot_point(0, 0.5);
            this.add_style_class_name('pinned-edge-dash');
            this.add_style_class_name('left');

            try {
                Main.layoutManager.addTopChrome(this, {
                    affectsInputRegion: true,
                    trackFullscreen: false,
                });
                journal('[pinnedEdge.dash] addTopChrome OK');
            } catch (e) {
                journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] addTopChrome THREW: ${e.message}`);
            }

            try {
                this._showAppsIcon.visible = true;
                this.updateShowAppsButton();
            } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }

            this._setupShowAppsTooltip();

            if (this.showAppsButton) {
                this.showAppsButton.connect('button-press-event', (a, e) => {
                    if (e.get_button() !== Clutter.BUTTON_PRIMARY)
                        return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
                    journal('[pinnedEdge.dash] Show Apps pressed');
                    this._onShowApps?.();
                    return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
                });
            }

            this._setGeometry();
            this.connect('notify::width', () => this._setGeometry());
            this.connect('notify::height', () => this._setGeometry());

            this.connect('notify::visible', () => {
                if (!this.visible) Interactions.getTooltip().hideNow();
            });

            for (const delay of [0, 300, 1000]) {
                if (delay === 0) {
                    GLib.idle_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT, () => {
                        if (this._destroying) return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
                        this._forceIconSizes('idle');
                        this._hideNonFavoriteApps();
                        return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
                    });
                } else {
                    GLib.timeout_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT, delay, () => {
                        if (this._destroying) return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
                        this._forceIconSizes(`timeout-${delay}`);
                        this._hideNonFavoriteApps();
                        return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
                    });
                }
            }

            journal('[pinnedEdge.dash] _init:exit');
        }

        _setupShowAppsTooltip() {
            const showApps = this._showAppsIcon;
            if (!showApps) return;

            this._suppressLabel(showApps);

            try {
                showApps.connect('notify::hover', () => {
                    if (showApps.hover) {
                        Interactions.getTooltip().showRight(
                            showApps.icon ?? showApps, 'Show Apps', S.dash.tooltipGap);
                    } else {
                        Interactions.getTooltip().hide();
                    }
                });
            } catch (e) {
                journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] showApps hover hook failed: ${e.message}`);
            }
        }

        _suppressLabel(container) {
            const label = container?.label;
            if (!label) return;
            label.visible = false;
            label.connect('notify::visible', () => {
                if (label.visible) label.visible = false;
            });
        }

        _styleAppMenu(item) {
            const menu = item?._menu;
            if (!menu?.actor) return;
            try {
                if (!menu.actor.has_style_class_name('pinned-edge-menu'))
                    menu.actor.add_style_class_name('pinned-edge-menu');
            } catch (e) {
                journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] menu style class failed: ${e.message}`);
            }
        }

        _forceVertical() {
            if (this._box) {
                try {
                    this._box.layout_manager = new Clutter.BoxLayout({
                        orientation: Clutter.Orientation.VERTICAL,
                    });
                } catch (e) {
                    journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] layout swap failed: ${e.message}`);
                    try { this._box.vertical = true; } catch (e2) { /* ignore */ }
                }
                this._box.x_align = Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER;
                this._box.y_align = Clutter.ActorAlign.START;
                this._box.x_expand = false;
                this._box.y_expand = true;
            }

            if (this._boxContainer) {
                try { this._boxContainer.vertical = true; } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                this._boxContainer.x_align = Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER;
                this._boxContainer.y_align = Clutter.ActorAlign.START;
                this._boxContainer.x_expand = false;
                this._boxContainer.y_expand = true;
            }

            if (this._scrollView) {
                this._scrollView.hscrollbar_policy = St.PolicyType.NEVER;
                this._scrollView.vscrollbar_policy = St.PolicyType.EXTERNAL;
                this._scrollView.x_expand = false;
                this._scrollView.y_expand = true;
            }

            if (this._dashContainer) {
                try { this._dashContainer.vertical = true; } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                this._dashContainer.x_align = Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER;
                this._dashContainer.y_align = Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER;
                this._dashContainer.x_expand = false;
                this._dashContainer.y_expand = true;
            }

            if (this._showAppsIcon) {
                this._showAppsIcon.x_expand = false;
                this._showAppsIcon.y_expand = false;
                this._showAppsIcon.x_align = Clutter.ActorAlign.CENTER;
                this._showAppsIcon.y_align = Clutter.ActorAlign.START;
            }

            this.iconSize = S.dash.icon;

            journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] _forceVertical box=${!!this._box} ` +
                `boxC=${!!this._boxContainer} scroll=${!!this._scrollView} ` +
                `dashC=${!!this._dashContainer} apps=${!!this._showAppsIcon} ` +
                `iconSize=${this.iconSize}`);
        }

        _initializeIconSize(maxSize) {
            this._availableIconSizes = BASE_ICON_SIZES.filter(v => v < maxSize);
            if (!this._availableIconSizes.includes(maxSize))
                this._availableIconSizes.push(maxSize);
            journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] _initializeIconSize(${maxSize}) -> ` +
                `[${this._availableIconSizes.join(',')}]`);
        }

        _forceIconSizes(trigger = 'manual') {
            const SIZE = S.dash.icon;
            this.iconSize = SIZE;

            const children = this._box?.get_children() ?? [];
            let appResized = 0, appFailed = 0;

            for (const item of children) {
                const dashIcon = item?.child;
                const appIcon = dashIcon?.icon;
                if (!appIcon) { appFailed++; continue; }

                let ok = false;

                if (typeof appIcon.setIconSize === 'function') {
                    try { appIcon.setIconSize(SIZE); ok = true; }
                    catch (e) { journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] setIconSize THREW: ${e.message}`); }
                }

                const stIcon = appIcon.icon;
                if (stIcon) {
                    try { stIcon.icon_size = SIZE; ok = true; } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                    try { stIcon.set_icon_size?.(SIZE); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                }

                const container = appIcon._iconContainer;
                if (container) {
                    try { container.set_size(SIZE, SIZE); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                }

                if (ok) appResized++; else appFailed++;
            }

            const appsIcon = this._showAppsIcon?.icon;
            if (appsIcon) {
                if (typeof appsIcon.setIconSize === 'function') {
                    try { appsIcon.setIconSize(SIZE); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                }
                const stIcon = appsIcon.icon;
                if (stIcon) {
                    try { stIcon.icon_size = SIZE; } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                    try { stIcon.set_icon_size?.(SIZE); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                }
            }

            journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] _forceIconSizes[${trigger}] ` +
                `children=${children.length} resized=${appResized} failed=${appFailed} ` +
                `iconSize=${this.iconSize}`);
        }

        _hideNonFavoriteApps() {
            const favorites = new Set(
                AppFavorites.getAppFavorites().getFavorites().map(a => a.get_id()));

            const children = this._box?.get_children() ?? [];
            let hidden = 0;
            for (const item of children) {
                if (item === this._showAppsIcon) {
                    item.visible = true;
                    continue;
                }
                const app = appOf(item);
                const id = app?.get_id?.();
                if (id) {
                    const show = favorites.has(id);
                    item.visible = show;
                    if (!show) hidden++;
                } else {
                    item.visible = false;
                    hidden++;
                }
            }
            if (this._separator) this._separator.visible = false;

            if (hidden)
                journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] _hideNonFavoriteApps hid ${hidden} non-favorite item(s)`);
        }

        _adjustIconSize() {
            this._forceIconSizes('_adjustIconSize');
            this._hideNonFavoriteApps();
        }

        _redisplay() {
            super._redisplay();
            this._forceIconSizes('_redisplay');
            this._hideNonFavoriteApps();
        }

        _createAppItem(app) {
            const item = super._createAppItem(app);

            this._suppressLabel(item);
            this._styleAppMenu(item);

            const dashIcon = item?.child;
            if (!dashIcon) return item;

            try {
                dashIcon.connect('clicked', () => {
                    journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] icon clicked: ${app.get_name?.() ?? ''}`);
                    this._onActivated?.();
                });
            } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }

            try {
                dashIcon.connect('notify::hover', () => {
                    if (dashIcon.hover) {
                        Interactions.getTooltip().showRight(
                            dashIcon, app.get_name?.() ?? '', S.dash.tooltipGap);
                    } else {
                        Interactions.getTooltip().hide();
                    }
                });
            } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }

            const appIcon = dashIcon.icon;
            if (appIcon) {
                if (typeof appIcon.setIconSize === 'function') {
                    try { appIcon.setIconSize(S.dash.icon); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                }
                const stIcon = appIcon.icon;
                if (stIcon) {
                    try { stIcon.icon_size = S.dash.icon; } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
                }
            }

            return item;
        }

        _setGeometry() {
            if (!this._monitor) return;
            if (this._setGeometryTimeout) {
                GLib.Source.remove(this._setGeometryTimeout);
                this._setGeometryTimeout = 0;
            }

            this._setGeometryTimeout = GLib.idle_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT, () => {
                this._setGeometryTimeout = 0;
                if (!this._monitor) return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;

                const { width, height, x, y } = this._monitor;
                const maxW = Math.round(width * 0.35);
                const maxH = Math.round(height * 0.90);
                this.setMaxSize(maxW, maxH);

                const w = this.width || 0;
                const h = this.height || 0;
                const nx = x;
                const ny = y + Math.round((height - h) / 2);
                this.set_position(nx, Math.max(y, ny));

                journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] _setGeometry pos=${nx},${Math.max(y, ny)} ` +
                    `size=${w}x${h} max=${maxW}x${maxH}`);
                return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
            });
        }

        _itemMenuStateChanged(item, opened) {
            super._itemMenuStateChanged(item, opened);
            this._keepDashShown = !!opened;
            if (opened) {
                this._styleAppMenu(item);
                Interactions.getTooltip().hideNow();
            }
            journal(`[pinnedEdge.dash] menu ${opened ? 'opened' : 'closed'}`);
        }

        // ---------- drag & drop reorder ----------
        _favoriteItems() {
            return (this._box?.get_children() ?? []).filter(i =>
                i.visible && i !== this._showAppsIcon && appOf(i));
        }

        _clearDragPlaceholder() {
            this._reorder.clear();
            super._clearDragPlaceholder();
        }

        _onItemDragBegin(...args) {
            this._keepDashShown = true;
            super._onItemDragBegin(...args);
        }
        _onItemDragEnd(...args) {
            this._keepDashShown = false;
            this._reorder.clear();
            super._onItemDragEnd(...args);
        }
        _onItemDragCancelled(...args) {
            this._keepDashShown = false;
            this._reorder.clear();
            super._onItemDragCancelled(...args);
        }

        handleDragOver(source, _actor, _x, _y, _time) {
            const app = source?.app;
            if (!app || app.is_window_backed()) return DND.DragMotionResult.NO_DROP;
            const [, py] = global.get_pointer();
            this._reorder.begin(this, source);
            this._reorder.update(py);
            return DND.DragMotionResult.MOVE_DROP;
        }

        acceptDrop(source, _actor, _x, _y, _time) {
            const app = source?.app;
            if (!app || app.is_window_backed()) return false;

            const [, py] = global.get_pointer();
            this._reorder.begin(this, source);
            const pos = this._reorder.update(py)?.index ?? 0;
            this._reorder.clear();

            const favs = AppFavorites.getAppFavorites();
            const id = app.get_id();
            const cur = favs.getFavorites().map(a => a.get_id()).indexOf(id);

            if (cur >= 0) { if (pos !== cur) favs.moveFavoriteToPos(id, pos); }
            else favs.addFavoriteAtPos(id, pos);
            return true;
        }

        destroy() {
            this._destroying = true;
            Interactions.getTooltip().hideNow();
            if (this._setGeometryTimeout) {
                GLib.Source.remove(this._setGeometryTimeout);
                this._setGeometryTimeout = 0;
            }
            this._reorder?.destroy();
            this._reorder = null;
            if (this.get_parent() === Main.layoutManager.uiGroup)
                Main.layoutManager.removeChrome(this);
            super.destroy();
        }
    });

// ==================== PinnedEdgeFlyout ====================
export class PinnedEdgeFlyout {
    constructor(settings = null, callbacks = {}) {
        journal('[pinnedEdge] constructor:enter');

        this._settings = settings;
        this._onShowAppsCb = callbacks.onShowApps ?? null;
        this._scope = new Scheduling.Scope();

        this._dash = null;
        this._open = false;
        this._edgeSince = 0;

        this._grabActive = false;
        this._needLeave = false;

        this._poll = new Poller(this._scope, {
            interval: POLL_MS,
            name: 'pinnedEdge',
            tick: () => this._tick(),
        });

        this._pressureBarrier = null;
        this._barrier = null;
        this._barrierKey = null;
        this._barrierIdleId = 0;

        this._scope.connect(global.display, 'grab-op-begin', () => {
            this._grabActive = true;
            this._edgeSince = 0;
            this._close('grab');
        });
        this._scope.connect(global.display, 'grab-op-end', () => {
            this._grabActive = false;
            this._edgeSince = 0;
            this._needLeave = true;
        });

        this._installBarrierDeferred();
        this._poll.start();

        this._scope.connect(Main.layoutManager, 'monitors-changed', () => {
            journal('[pinnedEdge] monitors changed — rebuilding');
            if (this._dash) {
                try { this._dash.destroy(); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
                this._dash = null;
            }
            this._open = false;
            this._removeBarrier();
            this._barrierKey = null;
            this._installBarrierDeferred();
        });

        journal('[pinnedEdge] constructor:exit');
    }

    _hasLeftNeighbor(monitor) {
        return Main.layoutManager.monitors.some(other =>
            other !== monitor
            && other.x + other.width <= monitor.x
            && other.y < monitor.y + monitor.height
            && other.y + other.height > monitor.y);
    }

    _zone() {
        const m = Main.layoutManager.primaryMonitor;
        if (!m || !this._dash || this._hasLeftNeighbor(m)) return null;

        let h = 0;
        try {
            h = (this._dash.visible && this._dash.height > 0)
                ? this._dash.height
                : this._dash.get_preferred_height(-1)[1];
        } catch (e) { h = 0; }

        h = Math.min(Math.round(h), Math.round(m.height * 0.90));
        if (!Number.isFinite(h) || h <= 0) return null;

        const y1 = m.y + Math.round((m.height - h) / 2);
        return { x1: m.x, x2: m.x + EDGE_PX, y1, y2: y1 + h };
    }

    _inZone(zone, x, y) {
        return !!zone && x >= zone.x1 && x <= zone.x2 && y >= zone.y1 && y < zone.y2;
    }

    _zoneKey(zone) {
        return zone ? `${zone.x1},${zone.y1},${zone.y2}` : '';
    }

    _ensureDash() {
        if (this._dash) return true;
        const monitor = Main.layoutManager.primaryMonitor;
        if (!monitor) {
            journal('[pinnedEdge] _ensureDash: no primary monitor');
            return false;
        }
        try {
            this._dash = new EdgeDash(monitor, {
                onShowApps: () => this._handleShowApps(),
                onActivated: () => this._close('activate'),
            });
            this._dash.hide();
            this._dash.opacity = 0;
            this._dash.set_scale(0, 1);

            this._scope.connect(this._dash, 'leave-event', () => {
                if (!this._open || this._dash?._keepDashShown)
                    return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
                const [x, y] = global.get_pointer();
                if (!this._pointerOverDash(x, y) && !this._inZone(this._zone(), x, y))
                    this._close('leave');
                return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
            });

            journal('[pinnedEdge] _ensureDash: created');
            return true;
        } catch (e) {
            journal(`[pinnedEdge] _ensureDash THREW: ${e.message}\n${e.stack}`);
            this._dash = null;
            return false;
        }
    }

    _handleShowApps() {
        journal('[pinnedEdge] show-apps requested');
        this._close('show-apps');
        GLib.timeout_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT, Motion.dur('dashClose') + 20, () => {
            this._onShowAppsCb?.();
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _installBarrierDeferred() {
        if (this._barrierIdleId) return;
        this._barrierIdleId = GLib.timeout_add(GLib.PRIORITY_DEFAULT, 200, () => {
            this._barrierIdleId = 0;
            try { this._installBarrier(); }
            catch (e) { journal(`[pinnedEdge.barrier] THREW: ${e.message}\n${e.stack}`); }
            return GLib.SOURCE_REMOVE;
        });
    }

    _installBarrier() {
        if (!this._ensureDash()) return;
        const zone = this._zone();
        this._barrierKey = this._zoneKey(zone);
        if (!zone) {
            journal('[pinnedEdge.barrier] no zone — SKIP');
            return;
        }

        const y1 = zone.y1 + 1;
        const y2 = zone.y2 - 1;
        const xBarrier = zone.x1 + 1;
        if (y2 <= y1) return;

        try {
            this._pressureBarrier = new Layout.PressureBarrier(
                PRESSURE_THRESHOLD,
                PRESSURE_TIMEOUT_MS,
                Shell.ActionMode.NORMAL | Shell.ActionMode.OVERVIEW);
        } catch (e) {
            journal(`[pinnedEdge.barrier] PressureBarrier THREW: ${e.message}`);
            return;
        }

        try {
            this._barrier = new Meta.Barrier({
                backend: global.backend,
                x1: xBarrier, y1,
                x2: xBarrier, y2,
                directions: Meta.BarrierDirection.POSITIVE_X,
            });
        } catch (e) {
            journal(`[pinnedEdge.barrier] Meta.Barrier THREW: ${e.message}`);
            this._pressureBarrier = null;
            return;
        }

        try { this._pressureBarrier.addBarrier(this._barrier); }
        catch (e) { journal(`[pinnedEdge.barrier] addBarrier THREW: ${e.message}`); }

        this._scope.connect(this._pressureBarrier, 'trigger', () => {
            if (this._open || this._grabActive || this._needLeave) return;
            const [, , mods] = global.get_pointer();
            if (mods & Clutter.ModifierType.BUTTON1_MASK) return;
            this._show('barrier');
        });

        journal(`[pinnedEdge.barrier] installed x=${xBarrier} y=[${y1},${y2}]`);
    }

    _removeBarrier() {
        if (this._barrier) {
            try { this._pressureBarrier?.removeBarrier(this._barrier); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
            try { this._barrier.destroy(); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
            this._barrier = null;
        }
        if (this._pressureBarrier) {
            try { this._pressureBarrier.destroy(); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
            this._pressureBarrier = null;
        }
    }

    _syncBarrier(zone) {
        if (this._open || this._barrierIdleId) return;
        const key = this._zoneKey(zone);
        if (key === this._barrierKey) return;
        this._removeBarrier();
        this._installBarrierDeferred();
    }

    _tick() {
        if (!this._dash && !this._ensureDash()) return;

        const [x, y, mods] = global.get_pointer();
        const zone = this._zone();
        this._syncBarrier(zone);

        const atEdge = this._inZone(zone, x, y);
        if (!atEdge) this._needLeave = false;

        if (!this._open) {
            const blocked = this._grabActive || this._needLeave ||
                (mods & Clutter.ModifierType.BUTTON1_MASK) !== 0;
            if (!atEdge || blocked) { this._edgeSince = 0; return; }

            const now = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
            if (!this._edgeSince) { this._edgeSince = now; return; }
            if (now - this._edgeSince >= DWELL_MS) {
                this._edgeSince = 0;
                this._show('dwell');
            }
            return;
        }

        if (this._dash?._keepDashShown) return;
        if (this._dash?.hover || this._pointerOverDash(x, y) || atEdge) return;

        this._close('left');
    }

    _pointerOverDash(x, y) {
        if (!this._dash || !this._dash.visible) return false;
        const [rx, ry] = this._dash.get_transformed_position();
        const [rw, rh] = this._dash.get_transformed_size();
        if (![rx, ry, rw, rh].every(Number.isFinite)) return false;
        return x >= rx && x <= rx + rw && y >= ry && y <= ry + rh;
    }

    _show(source = '?') {
        if (this._open || this._grabActive) return;
        if (!this._ensureDash()) {
            journal('[pinnedEdge] _show: dash unavailable — ABORT');
            return;
        }

        journal(`[pinnedEdge] _show source=${source}`);

        this._dash.remove_all_transitions();
        this._dash.set_scale(0, 1);
        this._dash.opacity = 0;
        this._dash.reactive = true;
        this._dash.show();

        this._dash._forceIconSizes('show');
        this._dash._hideNonFavoriteApps();

        Motion.animate(this._dash, { scale_x: 1, opacity: 255 },
            { duration: 'dashOpen', mode: Clutter.AnimationMode.EASE_OUT_CUBIC });

        this._open = true;
        this._edgeSince = 0;
    }

    _close(source = '?') {
        if (!this._open) return;
        journal(`[pinnedEdge] _close source=${source}`);
        this._open = false;
        this._edgeSince = 0;

        if (!this._dash) return;

        Interactions.getTooltip().hideNow();

        this._dash.reactive = false;
        this._dash.remove_all_transitions();
        Motion.animate(this._dash, { scale_x: 0, opacity: 0 }, {
            duration: 'dashClose',
            mode: Clutter.AnimationMode.EASE_IN_QUAD,
            onComplete: () => {
                if (!this._open && this._dash) {
                    this._dash.hide();
                    this._dash.set_scale(1, 1);
                }
            },
        });
    }

    destroy() {
        journal('[pinnedEdge] destroy:enter');
        this._open = false;

        this._poll.stop();
        if (this._barrierIdleId) {
            try { GLib.Source.remove(this._barrierIdleId); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
            this._barrierIdleId = 0;
        }
        this._removeBarrier();

        if (this._dash) {
            try { this._dash.destroy(); } catch (e) { /* gone */ }
            this._dash = null;
        }

        this._scope.dispose();
        journal('[pinnedEdge] destroy:exit');
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./logger.js =====
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import Gio from 'gi://Gio';

let loggingEnabled = false;
let logOutput = 'journal';
let uuid = null;
let logFile = null;


/*
 * logger.js is in the extension root.
 */
const extensionDir = GLib.path_get_dirname(
    GLib.filename_from_uri(import.meta.url)[0]
);


/*
 * Initialize logging.
 *
 * output:
 *   'journal' -> GNOME Journal only
 *   'file'    -> file only
 *   'both'    -> GNOME Journal + file
 *
 * enabled:
 *   true  -> logging enabled
 *   false -> logging disabled
 */
function initLogging(
    extensionUuid,
    output = 'journal',
    enabled = true
) {
    uuid = extensionUuid;
    logOutput = output;
    loggingEnabled = enabled;

    if (output === 'file' || output === 'both') {
        logFile = GLib.build_filenamev([
            GLib.get_home_dir(),
            `${uuid}.log`,
        ]);
    } else {
        logFile = null;
    }
}


/*
 * Create a logger for a source file.
 *
 * Usage:
 *
 * const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);
 */
function createLogger(source) {
    let sourceFile;

    try {
        sourceFile = GLib.filename_from_uri(source)[0];
    } catch (e) {
        sourceFile = source;
    }

    /*
     * Convert absolute path into a path
     * relative to the extension root.
     */
    const prefix = `${extensionDir}/`;

    if (sourceFile.startsWith(prefix)) {
        sourceFile = sourceFile.substring(prefix.length);
    }

    const relativeSource = sourceFile;


    return function journal(msg) {

        /*
         * Logging disabled?
         *
         * Do absolutely nothing.
         */
        if (!loggingEnabled)
            return;


        /*
         * Create timestamp once.
         */
        // const timestamp = GLib.DateTime
        //     .new_now_local()
        //     .format('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S');


        /*
         * Create the complete message once.
         *
         * Both Journal and file receive
         * exactly this same string.
         */
        // const output = `[${timestamp}] [${relativeSource}] ${msg}`;
        const output = `[${relativeSource}] ${msg}`;


        if (
            logOutput === 'journal' ||
            logOutput === 'both'
        ) {
            writeToJournal(output);
        }


        if (
            logOutput === 'file' ||
            logOutput === 'both'
        ) {
            writeToFile(output);
        }
    };
}


function writeToJournal(output) {
    GLib.log_structured(
        uuid,
        GLib.LogLevelFlags.LEVEL_MESSAGE,
        {
            MESSAGE: output,
            SYSLOG_IDENTIFIER: uuid,
        }
    );
}


function writeToFile(output) {
    if (!logFile)
        return;

    try {
        const file = Gio.File.new_for_path(logFile);

        const stream = file.append_to(
            Gio.FileCreateFlags.NONE,
            null
        );

        const bytes =
            new TextEncoder().encode(`${output}\n`);

        stream.write_all(bytes, null);
        stream.close(null);

    } catch (e) {
        /*
         * Don't call journal() here.
         */
        console.error(
            `[${uuid}] Failed to write log file: ${e}`
        );
    }
}


export {
    initLogging,
    createLogger,
};
// ===== FILE: ./prefs.js =====
import Gio from 'gi://Gio';
import Gdk from 'gi://Gdk';
import GObject from 'gi://GObject';
import Gtk from 'gi://Gtk';
import Adw from 'gi://Adw';
import { ExtensionPreferences } from 'resource:///org/gnome/Shell/Extensions/js/extensions/prefs.js';

const NUMERIC_KEYS = [
    { key: 'base-size', label: 'UI base size', min: 16, max: 96, step: 2 },
    { key: 'glass-strength', label: 'Transparency strength (%)', min: 0, max: 150, step: 5 },
    { key: 'thumbnail-min-width', label: 'Thumbnail minimum width', min: 20, max: 200, step: 2 },
    { key: 'flyout-dwell-ms', label: 'Flyout dwell (ms)', min: 0, max: 1500, step: 10 },
    { key: 'ext-ws-dwell-ms', label: 'External-drag workspace dwell (ms)', min: 0, max: 2000, step: 50 },
];

const BOOLEAN_KEYS = [
    ['show-window-button', 'Show "all windows" button'],
    ['show-workspace-names', 'Show workspace names'],
    ['group-apps-always', 'Always group windows by app'],
];

const BOXES = [['left', 'Left Box'], ['center', 'Center Box'], ['right', 'Right Box']];

const DATE_CHOICES = ['stock', 'left-first', 'center-first', 'right-first'];
const DATE_LABELS = [
    'Stock (shell default)',
    'Left box, first',
    'Center box, first',
    'Right box, first',
];

export default class WorkspacesOrganizerPreferences extends ExtensionPreferences {
    fillPreferencesWindow(window) {
        const settings = this.getSettings();

        const page = new Adw.PreferencesPage({
            title: 'Appearance',
            icon_name: 'preferences-desktop-font-symbolic',
        });
        window.add(page);

        const byKey = key => NUMERIC_KEYS.find(k => k.key === key);

        // Size
        const sizeGroup = new Adw.PreferencesGroup({
            title: 'Size',
            description: 'Every text and icon size scales from the base value',
        });
        page.add(sizeGroup);
        ['base-size', 'thumbnail-min-width'].forEach(k =>
            this._addSpinRow(sizeGroup, byKey(k), settings));

        // Appearance
        const appearanceGroup = new Adw.PreferencesGroup({
            title: 'Appearance',
            description: 'Surface transparency (100% = designed look)',
        });
        page.add(appearanceGroup);
        this._addSpinRow(appearanceGroup, byKey('glass-strength'), settings);

        // Panel elements
        const panelGroup = new Adw.PreferencesGroup({
            title: 'Panel',
            description: 'Toggle panel elements — changes apply immediately',
        });
        page.add(panelGroup);
        for (const [key, title] of BOOLEAN_KEYS) {
            const row = new Adw.ActionRow({ title });
            if (key === 'group-apps-always') {
                row.set_subtitle(
                    'Off: group only when the panel runs out of room. ' +
                    'On: every app with 2+ windows collapses into one badged icon.');
            }
            const toggle = new Gtk.Switch({ valign: Gtk.Align.CENTER });
            settings.bind(key, toggle, 'active', Gio.SettingsBindFlags.DEFAULT);
            row.add_suffix(toggle);
            row.set_activatable_widget(toggle);
            panelGroup.add(row);
        }

        // Timing
        const timingGroup = new Adw.PreferencesGroup({
            title: 'Timing',
            description: 'Interaction delays in milliseconds (0 = fire as soon as the pointer rests)',
        });
        page.add(timingGroup);
        ['flyout-dwell-ms', 'ext-ws-dwell-ms'].forEach(k =>
            this._addSpinRow(timingGroup, byKey(k), settings));

        // Accessibility
        const accessGroup = new Adw.PreferencesGroup({ title: 'Accessibility' });
        page.add(accessGroup);
        const reduceRow = new Adw.ActionRow({
            title: 'Reduce motion',
            subtitle: 'Disable fade, settle and flyout animations.',
        });
        const reduceToggle = new Gtk.Switch({ valign: Gtk.Align.CENTER });
        settings.bind('reduce-motion', reduceToggle, 'active', Gio.SettingsBindFlags.DEFAULT);
        reduceRow.add_suffix(reduceToggle);
        reduceRow.set_activatable_widget(reduceToggle);
        accessGroup.add(reduceRow);

        // Misc
        const miscGroup = new Adw.PreferencesGroup();
        page.add(miscGroup);

        const resetRow = new Adw.ActionRow({ title: 'Reset all settings to defaults' });
        const resetBtn = new Gtk.Button({ label: 'Reset', css_classes: ['destructive-action'] });
        resetBtn.connect('clicked', () => {
            for (const key of settings.list_keys()) {
                try { settings.reset(key); } catch (e) { /* skip */ }
            }
        });
        resetRow.add_suffix(resetBtn);
        resetRow.set_activatable_widget(resetBtn);
        miscGroup.add(resetRow);

        miscGroup.add(new Adw.ActionRow({
            title: 'Changes apply immediately',
            subtitle: 'The panel re-renders on the next window/workspace change — usually within a second.',
        }));

        // Panel ordering page
        this._addPanelPage(window, settings);
    }

    // ==================== Panel ordering page ====================
    _addPanelPage(window, settings) {
        const page = new Adw.PreferencesPage({
            title: 'Panel Order',
            icon_name: 'view-list-symbolic',
        });
        window.add(page);

        const css = new Gtk.CssProvider();
        css.load_from_string(`
            row.drop-before { box-shadow: inset 0 2px 0 0 @accent_color; }
            row.drop-after  { box-shadow: inset 0 -2px 0 0 @accent_color; }
            row.dragging    { opacity: 0.4; }
        `);
        Gtk.StyleContext.add_provider_for_display(
            Gdk.Display.get_default(), css, Gtk.STYLE_PROVIDER_PRIORITY_APPLICATION);

        let dragged = null;
        const cleanups = [];

        // ---- Behaviour ----
        const behaviour = new Adw.PreferencesGroup({
            title: 'Behaviour',
            description: 'Applied immediately — no restart needed.',
        });
        page.add(behaviour);

        behaviour.add(this._booleanRow(settings,
            'move-panel-to-bottom',
            'Move panel to bottom',
            'Move the top bar to the bottom of the screen. Ordering still applies.'));

        behaviour.add(this._booleanRow(settings,
            'hide-activities',
            'Hide Activities button',
            'Remove Activities from the panel layout.'));

        behaviour.add(this._booleanRow(settings,
            'disable-panel-window-drag',
            'Disable panel window drag',
            'Stop pressing the panel from starting a drag on the maximized window behind it.'));

        behaviour.add(this._comboRow(settings,
            'date-menu-position',
            'Date menu position',
            'Where to move the date/clock menu.',
            DATE_CHOICES, DATE_LABELS, cleanups));

        // ---- Per-box ordering ----
        for (const [type, title] of BOXES) {
            const orderKey = `order-${type}`;
            const discKey = `discovered-${type}`;
            const group = new Adw.PreferencesGroup({ title });
            const list = new Gtk.ListBox({
                selection_mode: Gtk.SelectionMode.NONE,
                css_classes: ['boxed-list'],
            });
            group.add(list);
            page.add(group);

            const shownOrder = () => {
                const present = settings.get_strv(discKey);
                const saved = settings.get_strv(orderKey).filter(r => present.includes(r));
                return [...new Set([...saved, ...present])];
            };

            const persist = shown => {
                if (settings.get_strv(discKey).length === 0) return;
                const raw = settings.get_strv(orderKey);
                const queue = [...shown];
                const next = [...new Set(raw)]
                    .map(r => shown.includes(r) ? queue.shift() : r)
                    .concat(queue);
                if (next.join('\n') !== raw.join('\n'))
                    settings.set_strv(orderKey, next);
            };

            let marked = null;
            const clearMark = () => {
                marked?.remove_css_class('drop-before');
                marked?.remove_css_class('drop-after');
                marked = null;
            };

            const locate = y => {
                const row = list.get_row_at_y(y);
                if (row) return { row, after: y > row.get_allocation().y + row.get_height() / 2 };
                return { row: list.get_last_child(), after: true };
            };

            const valid = row => dragged?.type === type && row?._role && row._role !== dragged.role;

            const drop = Gtk.DropTarget.new(GObject.TYPE_STRING, Gdk.DragAction.MOVE);
            drop.connect('motion', (_t, _x, y) => {
                const { row, after } = locate(y);
                clearMark();
                if (!valid(row)) return 0;
                row.add_css_class(after ? 'drop-after' : 'drop-before');
                marked = row;
                return Gdk.DragAction.MOVE;
            });
            drop.connect('leave', clearMark);
            drop.connect('drop', (_t, _v, _x, y) => {
                clearMark();
                const { row, after } = locate(y);
                if (!valid(row)) return false;
                const shown = shownOrder().filter(r => r !== dragged.role);
                shown.splice(shown.indexOf(row._role) + (after ? 1 : 0), 0, dragged.role);
                persist(shown);
                rebuild(shown);
                return true;
            });
            list.add_controller(drop);

            const addRow = role => {
                const row = new Adw.ActionRow({ title: role });
                row._role = role;
                row.add_prefix(new Gtk.Image({ icon_name: 'list-drag-handle-symbolic' }));

                const source = new Gtk.DragSource({ actions: Gdk.DragAction.MOVE });
                source.connect('prepare', () => {
                    const value = new GObject.Value();
                    value.init(GObject.TYPE_STRING);
                    value.set_string(role);
                    return Gdk.ContentProvider.new_for_value(value);
                });
                source.connect('drag-begin', src => {
                    dragged = { type, role };
                    row.add_css_class('dragging');
                    src.set_icon(new Gtk.WidgetPaintable({ widget: row }),
                        row.get_width() / 2, row.get_height() / 2);
                });
                source.connect('drag-end', () => {
                    dragged = null;
                    row.remove_css_class('dragging');
                    clearMark();
                });
                row.add_controller(source);

                list.append(row);
            };

            const rebuild = (shown = shownOrder()) => {
                list.remove_all();
                marked = null;
                if (shown.length)
                    shown.forEach(addRow);
                else
                    list.append(new Adw.ActionRow({
                        title: 'No indicators found here',
                        sensitive: false,
                    }));
                persist(shown);
            };

            const id = settings.connect(`changed::${discKey}`, () => rebuild());
            cleanups.push(() => settings.disconnect(id));
            rebuild();
        }

        // ---- Reset orders ----
        const miscGroup = new Adw.PreferencesGroup();
        page.add(miscGroup);

        const resetRow = new Adw.ActionRow({
            title: 'Reset panel orders to defaults',
            subtitle: 'Behaviour toggles above are not affected.',
        });
        const resetBtn = new Gtk.Button({
            label: 'Reset',
            css_classes: ['destructive-action'],
        });
        resetBtn.connect('clicked', () => {
            for (const [type] of BOXES) settings.reset(`order-${type}`);
        });
        resetRow.add_suffix(resetBtn);
        resetRow.set_activatable_widget(resetBtn);
        miscGroup.add(resetRow);

        window.connect('close-request', () => {
            cleanups.forEach(fn => fn());
            return false;
        });
    }

    // ---------- helpers ----------
    _addSpinRow(group, { key, label, min, max, step }, settings) {
        const row = new Adw.ActionRow({ title: label });
        const spin = Gtk.SpinButton.new_with_range(min, max, step);
        settings.bind(key, spin, 'value', Gio.SettingsBindFlags.DEFAULT);
        row.add_suffix(spin);
        row.set_activatable_widget(spin);
        group.add(row);
    }

    _booleanRow(settings, key, title, subtitle) {
        const row = new Adw.ActionRow({ title, subtitle });
        const toggle = new Gtk.Switch({ valign: Gtk.Align.CENTER });
        settings.bind(key, toggle, 'active', Gio.SettingsBindFlags.DEFAULT);
        row.add_suffix(toggle);
        row.set_activatable_widget(toggle);
        return row;
    }

    _comboRow(settings, key, title, subtitle, choices, labels, cleanups) {
        const row = new Adw.ComboRow({
            title, subtitle,
            model: new Gtk.StringList({ strings: labels }),
        });

        const sync = () => {
            const v = settings.get_string(key);
            const i = choices.indexOf(v);
            if (i >= 0 && row.selected !== i) row.selected = i;
        };
        row.connect('notify::selected', () => {
            const v = choices[row.selected];
            if (v && settings.get_string(key) !== v) settings.set_string(key, v);
        });

        const id = settings.connect(`changed::${key}`, sync);
        cleanups.push(() => settings.disconnect(id));
        sync();
        return row;
    }
}
// ===== FILE: ./extension.js =====
// ===== FILE: ./extension.js =====
import Clutter from 'gi://Clutter';
import GLib from 'gi://GLib';
import * as ExtensionModule from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/extensions/extension.js';
import * as Main from 'resource:///org/gnome/shell/ui/main.js';

import { WorkspaceIndicator } from './lib/workspaceIndicator.js';
import { TitleBarMoveMonitor } from './lib/titleBarMoveMonitor.js';
import { PinnedEdgeFlyout } from './lib/pinnedEdgeFlyout.js';
import { EdgeHud } from './lib/edgeHud.js';
import { PanelManager } from './lib/panel.js';
import * as OverlayService from './lib/overlayService.js';
import * as DBusService from './lib/dbusService.js';
import * as Interactions from './lib/interactions.js';
import * as Model from './lib/model.js';
import * as WorkspaceActions from './lib/workspaceActions.js';
import * as Sizes from './lib/sizes.js';
import * as Theme from './lib/theme.js';

import { initExternalDrag, destroyExternalDrag } from './lib/externalDrag.js';
import { initFocusedWindowBorder, destroyFocusedWindowBorder } from './lib/focusedWindowBorder.js';
import { initSelectedWindows, destroySelectedWindows } from './lib/selectedWindows.js';

import { initLogging, createLogger } from './logger.js';

const journal = createLogger(import.meta.url);

const SCROLL_THROTTLE_MS = 150;

export default class TopNotchWorkspaces extends ExtensionModule.Extension {
    constructor(metadata) {
        super(metadata);
        this._indicator = null;
        this._titleBarMoveMonitor = null;
        this._pinnedEdgeFlyout = null;
        this._edgeHud = null;
        this._panel = null;
        this._lastScrollTime = 0;
        this._unsubSizes = null;
    }

    enable() {
        initLogging(this.uuid, 'file', false);
        journal(`Enabled`);

        const settings = this.getSettings();

        Sizes.initSizes(settings);
        Theme.initTheme(settings);
        this._unsubSizes = Sizes.onSizesChanged(() => {
            Theme.refreshTheme();
            Model.requestRender();
        });

        Model.connectModelSignals(settings);
        initExternalDrag();
        initFocusedWindowBorder();
        initSelectedWindows();

        OverlayService.initOverlayService(settings);
        DBusService.initDBusService();
        DBusService.installCli(this.path);

        this._indicator = new WorkspaceIndicator(settings, {
            showAllApps: OverlayService.showAllApps,
            showAllWindows: OverlayService.showAllWindows,
            showWorkspaceWindows: OverlayService.showWorkspaceWindows,
            showGroupWindows: OverlayService.showGroupWindows,
        });
        Main.panel.addToStatusArea('workspace-indicator', this._indicator, 0, 'left');

        this._titleBarMoveMonitor = new TitleBarMoveMonitor();
        this._pinnedEdgeFlyout = new PinnedEdgeFlyout(settings, {
            onShowApps: OverlayService.showAllApps,
        });
        this._edgeHud = new EdgeHud(settings);

        // Panel order / position — after all custom UI is in place.
        this._panel = new PanelManager(settings);

        this._lastScrollTime = 0;
        Main.panel.connectObject('scroll-event',
            (_actor, event) => this._handleScroll(event), this);
    }

    disable() {
        Main.panel.disconnectObject(this);

        if (this._panel) {
            this._panel.destroy();
            this._panel = null;
        }

        DBusService.removeCli(this.path);
        DBusService.destroyDBusService();
        OverlayService.destroyOverlayService();

        if (this._pinnedEdgeFlyout) {
            this._pinnedEdgeFlyout.destroy();
            this._pinnedEdgeFlyout = null;
        }
        if (this._titleBarMoveMonitor) {
            this._titleBarMoveMonitor.destroy();
            this._titleBarMoveMonitor = null;
        }
        if (this._indicator) {
            this._indicator.destroy();
            this._indicator = null;
        }
        if (this._edgeHud) {
            this._edgeHud.destroy();
            this._edgeHud = null;
        }

        destroySelectedWindows();
        destroyFocusedWindowBorder();
        destroyExternalDrag();
        Model.disconnectModelSignals();
        Interactions.destroyTooltip();

        this._unsubSizes?.(); this._unsubSizes = null;
        Theme.destroyTheme();
        Sizes.destroySizes();
    }

    _handleScroll(event) {
        if (event.type() !== Clutter.EventType.SCROLL)
            return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;

        let step;
        switch (event.get_scroll_direction()) {
            case Clutter.ScrollDirection.UP:
            case Clutter.ScrollDirection.LEFT:
                step = -1; break;
            case Clutter.ScrollDirection.DOWN:
            case Clutter.ScrollDirection.RIGHT:
                step = 1; break;
            default:
                return Clutter.EVENT_PROPAGATE;
        }

        const now = GLib.get_monotonic_time() / 1000;
        if (now - this._lastScrollTime < SCROLL_THROTTLE_MS)
            return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
        this._lastScrollTime = now;

        WorkspaceActions.scrollModelWindows(step);
        return Clutter.EVENT_STOP;
    }
}
